--- START OF FILE: tableUtils.ts ---
/**
 * Video Analysis Table Utilities
 * Industry-standard SMPTE Timecode Logic
 */

/**
 * Converts a total frame count to a SMPTE timecode string.
 * Supports both Drop-Frame (DF) and Non-Drop-Frame (NDF).
 *
 * @param frameCount The total number of frames.
 * @param frameRate The frame rate (e.g., 23.976, 24, 25, 29.97, 30, 59.94, 60).
 * @param useDropFrame Whether to use Drop-Frame timecode (standard for 29.97/59.94).
 * @returns Formatted timecode string (HH:MM:SS:FF for NDF, HH:MM:SS;FF for DF).
 */
export function framesToTimecode(frameCount: number, frameRate: number, useDropFrame: boolean = false): string {
    // Handle 0 or negative
    if (frameCount < 0) frameCount = 0;

    let fps = Math.round(frameRate);

    // Correction for Drop Frame
    if (useDropFrame && (frameRate === 29.97 || frameRate === 59.94)) {
        // For 29.97 DF: Drop 2 frames every minute, except every 10th minute.
        // Total dropped frames per 10 mins = 18.
        // Frame rate base is 30 (or 60).
        const dropFrames = frameRate === 29.97 ? 2 : 4;
        const framesPerMinute = (fps * 60) - dropFrames;
        const framesPer10Minutes = (fps * 60 * 10) - (dropFrames * 9); // 9 drops in 10 mins

        const D = Math.floor(frameCount / framesPer10Minutes);
        const M = frameCount % framesPer10Minutes;

        if (M > dropFrames) {
            frameCount = frameCount + (dropFrames * 9 * D) + dropFrames * Math.floor((M - dropFrames) / framesPerMinute);
        } else {
            frameCount = frameCount + dropFrames * 9 * D;
        }
    }

    const frames = Math.floor(frameCount % fps);
    const secondsTotal = Math.floor(frameCount / fps);
    const seconds = Math.floor(secondsTotal % 60);
    const minutesTotal = Math.floor(secondsTotal / 60);
    const minutes = Math.floor(minutesTotal % 60);
    const hours = Math.floor(minutesTotal / 60);

    const pad = (n: number) => n.toString().padStart(2, '0');
    const separator = useDropFrame ? ';' : ':';

    return `${pad(hours)}:${pad(minutes)}:${pad(seconds)}${separator}${pad(frames)}`;
}

/**
 * Converts a SMPTE timecode string to total frame count.
 *
 * @param timecode Formatted timecode (HH:MM:SS:FF or HH:MM:SS;FF).
 * @param frameRate Video frame rate.
 * @returns Total frame count.
 */
export function timecodeToFrames(timecode: string, frameRate: number): number {
    if (!timecode) return 0;

    // Normalize separator
    const isDropFrame = timecode.includes(';') || (frameRate === 29.97 && timecode.length === 11);
    const parts = timecode.replace(';', ':').split(':').map(Number);

    if (parts.length < 4) return 0;

    const [hours, minutes, seconds, frames] = parts;
    let fps = Math.round(frameRate);

    let totalFrames = (hours * 3600 + minutes * 60 + seconds) * fps + frames;

    if (isDropFrame && (frameRate === 29.97 || frameRate === 59.94)) {
        const dropFrames = frameRate === 29.97 ? 2 : 4;
        const totalMinutes = hours * 60 + minutes;
        const droppedFrames = (dropFrames * totalMinutes) - (dropFrames * Math.floor(totalMinutes / 10));
        totalFrames -= droppedFrames;
    }

    return totalFrames;
}

/**
 * Converts seconds to a SMPTE timecode string.
 * 
 * @param seconds Time in seconds (float).
 * @param frameRate Video frame rate.
 * @param forceDropFrame Force usage of Drop Frame (optional, auto-detected otherwise).
 * @returns Timecode string.
 */
export function secondsToTimecode(seconds: number, frameRate: number, forceDropFrame?: boolean): string {
    const isDropFrame = forceDropFrame ?? (Math.abs(frameRate - 29.97) < 0.01 || Math.abs(frameRate - 59.94) < 0.01);
    const totalFrames = Math.round(seconds * (isDropFrame ? frameRate : Math.round(frameRate)));
    // Note: iterating seconds * frameRate is approximation. 
    // For precise calculations we need to be careful. 
    // If drop frame, 29.97 * seconds gives precise frame count effectively.

    return framesToTimecode(totalFrames, frameRate, isDropFrame);
}

/**
 * Converts timecode to seconds.
 */
export function timecodeToSeconds(timecode: string, frameRate: number): number {
    const frames = timecodeToFrames(timecode, frameRate);
    const isDropFrame = timecode.includes(';') || (Math.abs(frameRate - 29.97) < 0.01);

    // For Drop Frame, we divide by the REAL frame rate (29.97) to get wall-clock time
    // For Non-Drop, we divide by the integer frame rate (e.g. 24 or 25) because NDF drifts from wall clock.
    const divisor = isDropFrame ? frameRate : Math.round(frameRate);

    return frames / divisor;
}

/**
 * Auto-detects if a frame rate should default to Drop Frame.
 */
export function isDropFrame(frameRate: number): boolean {
    return Math.abs(frameRate - 29.97) < 0.01 || Math.abs(frameRate - 59.94) < 0.01;
}

/**
 * Formats a duration in seconds to a human-readable string (e.g., "1h 20m").
 */
export function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
    if (!seconds) return '0s';
    const h = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
    const m = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
    const s = Math.floor(seconds % 60);

    const parts = [];
    if (h > 0) parts.push(`${h}h`);
    if (m > 0) parts.push(`${m}m`);
    if (s > 0 || parts.length === 0) parts.push(`${s}s`);

    return parts.join(' ');
}


   - Name: `gemini-super-app`

   - Click "uploading an existing file"
   - Drag all files (except `.env.local`)

--- START OF FILE: SETUP_GUIDE.md ---
# TMS Clip Analyzer - Quick Setup Guide

## What's New

This consolidated application combines FOUR video analysis tools into one unified interface:

### All-in-One Analysis Tool:
1. **Synopsis Generation** - Professional film synopsis with customizable parameters
2. **Talent Bio Generation** - Auto-detect or manually enter talent for biography creation
3. **Graphics & Lower Thirds Detection** - Timecode log of all on-screen graphics
4. **Fauna Identification** - AI-powered animal species identification with scientific names

## Installation Steps

1. **Extract the zip file**
   ```bash
   unzip clip-analyzer.zip
   cd clip-analyzer
   ```

2. **Install dependencies**
   ```bash
   npm install
   ```

3. **Set up environment variable**
   Create a `.env` file in the root directory:
   ```
   API_KEY=your_gemini_api_key_here
   ```

4. **Run the development server**
   ```bash
   npm run dev
   ```

5. **Build for production**
   ```bash
   npm run build
   ```

## How It Works

### Workflow

1. **Upload Video** 
   - Single upload interface
   - Drag & drop or click to browse
   - Supports MP4, MOV, WebM, AVI, MKV (up to 5GB)

2. **Choose Analysis Type**
   - **Generate Synopsis**: Create professional film synopsis with customizable parameters
   - **Generate Talent Bios**: Auto-detect or manually enter talent for biography generation
   - **Find Graphics & Lower Thirds**: Detect and log all on-screen graphics with timecodes
   - **Identify Animal Species**: AI-powered fauna identification with scientific classifications

3. **Configure & Generate**
   - Each analysis type has its own configuration screen
   - Real-time progress tracking
   - Cancel anytime with ESC key

4. **View & Export Results**
   - Synopsis: Copy all text with one click
   - Bios: Download all as formatted text file
   - Graphics: Download timecode log
   - Fauna: Download as TXT or CSV

## Key Features

✅ **Unified Upload**: Upload your video once, run multiple analysis types
✅ **Four Analysis Modes**: Synopsis, bios, graphics detection, and species identification
✅ **Smart Processing**: Efficient frame extraction and AI analysis
✅ **Flexible Options**: Auto-detect or manual entry where applicable
✅ **Progress Tracking**: See exactly what's happening at each step
✅ **Multi-language**: Generate content in 8+ different languages
✅ **Error Handling**: Clear error messages with retry options
✅ **Responsive UI**: Beautiful, modern interface that works on all devices
✅ **Export Options**: Download results in multiple formats (TXT, CSV)

## Troubleshooting

### "API_KEY not found"
- Ensure `.env` file exists in root directory
- Check that `API_KEY=your_key` is properly formatted
- Restart dev server after adding `.env`

### Video processing fails
- Check file size (max 5GB)
- Verify format is supported (MP4, MOV, WebM, AVI, MKV)
- Ensure video is not corrupted

### Frames extraction timeout
- Video may be too long (max 60 minutes for synopsis)
- Try shorter video clip
- Check browser console for specific errors

> Why do I have a folder named ".vercel" in my project?
The ".vercel" folder is created when you link a directory to a Vercel project.

> What does the "project.json" file contain?
The "project.json" file contains:
- The ID of the Vercel project that you linked ("projectId")
- The ID of the user or team your Vercel project is owned by ("orgId")

> Should I commit the ".vercel" folder?
No, you should not share the ".vercel" folder with anyone.
Upon creation, it will be automatically added to your ".gitignore" file.

--- START OF FILE: utils/videoProcessor.ts ---
import { ERROR_IDS, MAX_VIDEO_DURATION_SECONDS, MAX_FRAMES_LONG_VIDEO, MAX_FRAMES_SHORT_VIDEO } from '../types';

const FRAME_EXTRACTION_TIMEOUT = 1800000; // 30 minutes
const MAX_FRAME_DIMENSION = 720; // Scale frames to max 720p for performance
const YIELD_EVERY_N_FRAMES = 5; // Yield to main thread to keep UI responsive

export const extractFramesFromVideo = (
    videoUrl: string, 
    signal: AbortSignal, 
    density: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' = 'low'
): Promise<string[]> => {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (signal.aborted) return reject(new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED));

    const video = document.createElement('video');
    video.preload = 'auto';
    video.muted = true;
    video.src = videoUrl;
    video.crossOrigin = "anonymous";

    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    const context = canvas.getContext('2d');

    if (!context) {
      return reject(new Error('Could not get canvas context. Your browser may not be supported.'));
    }

    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
        cleanup();
        reject(new Error(ERROR_IDS.EXTRACTION_TIMEOUT));
    }, FRAME_EXTRACTION_TIMEOUT);
    
    const frames: string[] = [];
    let captureInterval = 1;
    let maxFrames = MAX_FRAMES_SHORT_VIDEO;
    
    const cleanup = () => {
        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
        video.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', onMetadataLoaded);
        video.removeEventListener('canplay', onCanPlay);
        video.removeEventListener('seeked', onSeeked);
        video.removeEventListener('error', onError);
        signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
        video.remove();
        canvas.remove();
    };

    const onAbort = () => {
        cleanup();
        reject(new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED));
    };
    signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort);

    const captureFrameAt = (time: number) => {
        video.currentTime = time;
    };
    
    const onSeeked = () => {
        if (signal.aborted) return cleanup();

        context.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        const base64Data = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.9).split(',')[1];
        if (base64Data) {
            frames.push(base64Data);
        }

        const nextTime = frames.length * captureInterval;
        if (frames.length < maxFrames && nextTime <= video.duration) {
             if (frames.length % YIELD_EVERY_N_FRAMES === 0) {
                setTimeout(() => captureFrameAt(nextTime), 0);
            } else {
                captureFrameAt(nextTime);
            }
        } else {
            cleanup();
            resolve(frames);
        }
    };
    
    const onCanPlay = () => {
        if (signal.aborted) return cleanup();
        video.addEventListener('seeked', onSeeked);
        if (video.duration > 0) {
            captureFrameAt(0);
        } else {
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (signal.aborted) return cleanup();
                context.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
                const base64Data = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.9).split(',')[1];
                 if (base64Data) frames.push(base64Data);
                 cleanup();
                 resolve(frames);
            }, 500);
        }
    };
    
    const onMetadataLoaded = () => {
        if (signal.aborted) return cleanup();
        
        if (video.duration > MAX_VIDEO_DURATION_SECONDS) {
            cleanup();
            return reject(new Error(ERROR_IDS.VIDEO_TOO_LONG));
        }

        let { videoWidth, videoHeight } = video;
        if (videoWidth > MAX_FRAME_DIMENSION || videoHeight > MAX_FRAME_DIMENSION) {
            const ratio = videoWidth > videoHeight ? MAX_FRAME_DIMENSION / videoWidth : MAX_FRAME_DIMENSION / videoHeight;
            videoWidth = Math.round(videoWidth * ratio);
            videoHeight = Math.round(videoHeight * ratio);
        }
        
        canvas.width = videoWidth;
        canvas.height = videoHeight;
        
        const duration = video.duration;
        const HARD_FRAME_CAP = 500;

        switch(density) {
            case 'high':
                maxFrames = Math.floor(duration); // ~1 frame per second
                break;
            case 'medium':
                maxFrames = Math.floor(duration / 5); // ~1 frame every 5 seconds
                break;
            case 'low':
            default:
                maxFrames = Math.ceil(duration / 10);
                if (duration > 300) {
                    maxFrames = Math.min(maxFrames, MAX_FRAMES_LONG_VIDEO);
                } else {
                    maxFrames = Math.min(maxFrames, MAX_FRAMES_SHORT_VIDEO);
                }
                break;
        }

        maxFrames = Math.min(maxFrames, HARD_FRAME_CAP);
        captureInterval = Math.max(1, duration / maxFrames);
    };

    const onError = () => {
        cleanup();
        reject(new Error(ERROR_IDS.VIDEO_LOAD_FAIL));
    };

    video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', onMetadataLoaded);
    video.addEventListener('canplay', onCanPlay);
    video.addEventListener('error', onError);
  });
};


function audioBufferToWavBase64(buffer: AudioBuffer): string {
    const numOfChan = buffer.numberOfChannels;
    const length = buffer.length * numOfChan * 2 + 44;
    const bufferArr = new ArrayBuffer(length);
    const view = new DataView(bufferArr);
    const channels = [];
    let pos = 0;

    const writeString = (view: DataView, offset: number, string: string) => {
        for (let i = 0; i < string.length; i++) {
            view.setUint8(offset + i, string.charCodeAt(i));
        }
    };

    writeString(view, pos, 'RIFF'); pos += 4;
    view.setUint32(pos, length - 8, true); pos += 4;
    writeString(view, pos, 'WAVE'); pos += 4;
    writeString(view, pos, 'fmt '); pos += 4;
    view.setUint32(pos, 16, true); pos += 4;
    view.setUint16(pos, 1, true); pos += 2;
    view.setUint16(pos, numOfChan, true); pos += 2;
    view.setUint32(pos, buffer.sampleRate, true); pos += 4;
    view.setUint32(pos, buffer.sampleRate * 2 * numOfChan, true); pos += 4;
    view.setUint16(pos, numOfChan * 2, true); pos += 2;
    view.setUint16(pos, 16, true); pos += 2;
    writeString(view, pos, 'data'); pos += 4;
    view.setUint32(pos, length - pos - 4, true); pos += 4;

    for (let i = 0; i < buffer.numberOfChannels; i++) {
        channels.push(buffer.getChannelData(i));
    }

    let offset = pos;
    for (let i = 0; i < buffer.length; i++) {
        for (let ch = 0; ch < numOfChan; ch++) {
            const sample = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, channels[ch][i]));
            view.setInt16(offset, sample < 0 ? sample * 0x8000 : sample * 0x7fff, true);
            offset += 2;
        }
    }
    
    const uint8Array = new Uint8Array(bufferArr);
    let binary = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < uint8Array.byteLength; i++) {
        binary += String.fromCharCode(uint8Array[i]);
    }
    return window.btoa(binary);
}


export const extractAudioFromVideo = (videoFile: File): Promise<string | null> => {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
        const audioContext = new (window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext)();
        const reader = new FileReader();

        reader.onload = async (event) => {
            try {
                const arrayBuffer = event.target?.result as ArrayBuffer;
                if (!arrayBuffer) {
                    return resolve(null);
                }
                
                const audioBuffer = await audioContext.decodeAudioData(arrayBuffer);
                const wavBase64 = audioBufferToWavBase64(audioBuffer);
                resolve(wavBase64);
            } catch (error) {
                console.warn("Could not decode audio from video file. It might not contain an audio track.", error);
                resolve(null);
            }
        };

        reader.onerror = (error) => {
            console.error("FileReader error while extracting audio:", error);
            resolve(null);
        };

        reader.readAsArrayBuffer(videoFile);
    });
};

--- START OF FILE: utils/videoMetadataExtractor.ts ---
/**
 * Video Metadata Extractor
 * Extracts technical details (Frame Rate, Resolution, Codec) using Browser APIs.
 */

import { VideoMetadata } from '../types';

/**
 * Extracts metadata from a given Video File object.
 * 
 * @param file The video file to analyze.
 * @returns Promise resolving to VideoMetadata.
 */
export async function extractVideoMetadata(file: File): Promise<VideoMetadata> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const video = document.createElement('video');
        video.preload = 'metadata';
        video.muted = true;
        video.playsInline = true;

        const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
        video.src = objectUrl;

        // Timeout safety
        const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
            reject(new Error('Metadata extraction timed out'));
        }, 5000);

        video.onloadedmetadata = async () => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);

            const width = video.videoWidth;
            const height = video.videoHeight;
            const duration = video.duration;
            let frameRate = 30; // Default fallback

            // 1. Try to get Frame Rate from MediaStreamTrack settings (Chrome/Edge/Firefox)
            // This is the most reliable "browser-native" way without libraries
            try {
                // @ts-ignore - captureStream is valid in modern browsers but TS might complain
                if (video.captureStream) {
                    // @ts-ignore
                    const stream = video.captureStream();
                    const track = stream.getVideoTracks()[0];
                    if (track) {
                        const settings = track.getSettings();
                        if (settings.frameRate) {
                            frameRate = settings.frameRate;
                        }
                    }
                } else if ((video as any).mozCaptureStream) { // Firefox
                    const stream = (video as any).mozCaptureStream();
                    const track = stream.getVideoTracks()[0];
                    if (track && track.getSettings().frameRate) {
                        frameRate = track.getSettings().frameRate;
                    }
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.warn('Could not extract precise frame rate via MediaStream API, using fallback.', e);
            }

            // Round common frame rates to standard values for cleaner UI/Timecode match
            if (Math.abs(frameRate - 23.976) < 0.1) frameRate = 23.976;
            else if (Math.abs(frameRate - 24) < 0.1) frameRate = 24;
            else if (Math.abs(frameRate - 25) < 0.1) frameRate = 25;
            else if (Math.abs(frameRate - 29.97) < 0.1) frameRate = 29.97;
            else if (Math.abs(frameRate - 30) < 0.1) frameRate = 30;
            else if (Math.abs(frameRate - 59.94) < 0.1) frameRate = 59.94;
            else if (Math.abs(frameRate - 60) < 0.1) frameRate = 60;

            // Detect codec (basic)
            // Note: "video/mp4; codecs=avc1..." is hard to get from File object reliably without parsing atoms.
            // We return the MIME type as a proxy.
            const mimeType = file.type;
            let codec = '';
            if (mimeType.includes('mp4')) codec = 'H.264 (AVC) / H.265 (HEVC)';
            if (mimeType.includes('webm')) codec = 'VP8 / VP9 / AV1';
            if (mimeType.includes('quicktime')) codec = 'ProRes / H.264';

            URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);

            resolve({
                fileName: file.name,
                fileSize: file.size,
                mimeType: file.type,
                width,
                height,
                duration,
                frameRate,
                aspectRatio: calculateAspectRatio(width, height),
                hasAudio: true, // simplified assumption, would need AudioContext to verify
                hasVideo: true,
                codec,
                format: file.name.split('.').pop()?.toUpperCase() || 'UNKNOWN'
            });
        };

        video.onerror = () => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
            reject(new Error('Failed to load video metadata.'));
        };
    });
}

function calculateAspectRatio(w: number, h: number): string {
    const gcd = (a: number, b: number): number => b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;
    const divisor = gcd(w, h);
    const wRatio = w / divisor;
    const hRatio = h / divisor;

    // Common video approximations
    const ratio = w / h;
    if (Math.abs(ratio - 1.777) < 0.05) return '16:9';
    if (Math.abs(ratio - 1.333) < 0.05) return '4:3';
    if (Math.abs(ratio - 2.39) < 0.05) return '2.39:1';

    return `${wRatio}:${hRatio}`;
}

--- START OF FILE: utils/videoFrameExtractor.ts ---

export class VideoFrameExtractor {
    static async extractFrames(file: File, timestamps: number[]): Promise<Blob[]> {
        return new Promise((resolve) => {
            const video = document.createElement('video');
            const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
            const blobs: Blob[] = [];
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);

            let currentIndex = 0;

            video.src = url;
            video.muted = true;
            video.playsInline = true;

            const processNext = async () => {
                if (currentIndex >= timestamps.length) {
                    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
                    resolve(blobs);
                    return;
                }

                const time = timestamps[currentIndex];
                video.currentTime = time;
            };

            video.onloadedmetadata = () => {
                canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
                canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
                // Optionally scale down for AI analysis to save bandwidth?
                // Let's keep original for now or limit to 720p if huge
                if (canvas.width > 1280) {
                    const scale = 1280 / canvas.width;
                    canvas.width = 1280;
                    canvas.height = video.videoHeight * scale;
                }
            };

            video.onseeked = () => {
                if (!ctx) return;
                ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

                canvas.toBlob((blob) => {
                    if (blob) blobs.push(blob);
                    currentIndex++;
                    processNext();
                }, 'image/jpeg', 0.8);
            };

            video.onerror = () => {
                console.error("Video error during extraction");
                currentIndex++;
                processNext();
            };

            // Start
            processNext();
        });
    }
}

--- START OF FILE: utils/tableUtils.ts ---
/**
 * Utility functions for table formatting and data export
 * Optimized for professional production document formats
 */

export type TimecodeFormat = 'NDF' | 'DF'; // Non-Drop-Frame vs Drop-Frame

/**
 * Detect if timecode uses drop-frame format (semicolon separator)
 */
export function isDropFrameTimecode(tc: string): boolean {
  return tc.includes(';');
}

/**
 * Convert drop-frame timecode to seconds
 * Only valid for 29.97fps and 59.94fps
 */
export function dropFrameToSeconds(tc: string, frameRate: number): number {
  // Only valid for 29.97fps and 59.94fps
  // Only valid for 29.97fps and 59.94fps
  if (Math.abs(frameRate - 29.97) > 0.01 && Math.abs(frameRate - 59.94) > 0.01) {
    // console.warn(`Drop-frame timecode is only valid for 29.97fps and 59.94fps, got ${frameRate}fps`);
    // Fall back to non-drop-frame parsing
    return parseTimecode(tc.replace(/;/g, ':'), frameRate);
  }

  // Parse the timecode
  const parts = tc.split(/[:;]/);
  if (parts.length !== 4) return 0;

  const hours = parseInt(parts[0]);
  const minutes = parseInt(parts[1]);
  const seconds = parseInt(parts[2]);
  const frames = parseInt(parts[3]);

  // Calculate drop-frame parameters
  const dropFrames = frameRate === 29.97 ? 2 : 4; // 2 for 29.97fps, 4 for 59.94fps

  // Total minutes
  const totalMinutes = hours * 60 + minutes;

  // Calculate total frames with drop-frame compensation
  // Frames are dropped at the start of each minute EXCEPT every 10th minute
  const droppedFrames = dropFrames * (totalMinutes - Math.floor(totalMinutes / 10));

  const totalFrames =
    (hours * 60 * 60 * frameRate) +
    (minutes * 60 * frameRate) +
    (seconds * frameRate) +
    frames -
    droppedFrames;

  return totalFrames / frameRate;
}

/**
 * Convert seconds to drop-frame timecode
 * Only valid for 29.97fps and 59.94fps
 */
export function secondsToDropFrameTimecode(seconds: number, frameRate: number): string {
  // Only valid for 29.97fps and 59.94fps
  if (frameRate !== 29.97 && frameRate !== 59.94) {
    console.warn(`Drop-frame timecode is only valid for 29.97fps and 59.94fps, got ${frameRate}fps`);
    // Fall back to non-drop-frame
    return secondsToTimecode(seconds, frameRate);
  }

  const dropFrames = frameRate === 29.97 ? 2 : 4; // 2 for 29.97fps, 4 for 59.94fps

  // Total frames from seconds
  let totalFrames = Math.round(seconds * frameRate);

  // Calculate 10-minute chunks
  const tenMinuteChunks = Math.floor(totalFrames / (frameRate * 600));
  let remainingFrames = totalFrames % (frameRate * 600);

  // Add back the dropped frames (except for 10-minute marks)
  if (remainingFrames >= dropFrames) {
    // Calculate how many minutes have passed in this 10-minute chunk
    const minutesInChunk = Math.floor((remainingFrames - dropFrames) / (frameRate * 60 - dropFrames));
    const adjustedFrames = remainingFrames + (dropFrames * minutesInChunk);
    remainingFrames = adjustedFrames;
  }

  totalFrames = (tenMinuteChunks * (frameRate * 600)) + remainingFrames;

  // Convert to HH:MM:SS;FF
  const fps = Math.round(frameRate);
  const frames = totalFrames % fps;
  const totalSeconds = Math.floor(totalFrames / frameRate);
  const secs = totalSeconds % 60;
  const totalMinutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
  const mins = totalMinutes % 60;
  const hours = Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60);

  return `${hours.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${mins.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')};${frames.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/**
 * Convert timecode between NDF and DF formats
 */
export function convertTimecode(
  tc: string,
  fromFormat: TimecodeFormat,
  toFormat: TimecodeFormat,
  frameRate: number
): string {
  if (fromFormat === toFormat) return tc;

  // Convert to seconds first
  const seconds = fromFormat === 'DF' ? dropFrameToSeconds(tc, frameRate) : parseTimecode(tc, frameRate);

  // Convert to target format
  return toFormat === 'DF' ? secondsToDropFrameTimecode(seconds, frameRate) : secondsToTimecode(seconds, frameRate);
}

/**
 * Parse timecode string to seconds
 * Supports formats: HH:MM:SS, HH:MM:SS:FF, HH:MM:SS.mmm, HH:MM:SS;FF (drop-frame)
 */
export function parseTimecode(tc: string, frameRate: number = 25): number {
  if (!tc) return 0;
  let cleanTc = tc.trim();

  // Check if it's drop-frame format (uses semicolon)
  // Only parse as DF if the frame rate actually supports it to avoid console spam
  const isSupportedDF = Math.abs(frameRate - 29.97) < 0.01 || Math.abs(frameRate - 59.94) < 0.01;

  if (cleanTc.includes(';')) {
    if (isSupportedDF) {
      return dropFrameToSeconds(cleanTc, frameRate);
    } else {
      // Treat semicolon as colon for non-DF rates
      cleanTc = cleanTc.replace(/;/g, ':');
    }
  }

  const parts = cleanTc.split(/[:.]/);

  if (parts.length === 2) {
    // Handle MM:SS
    const [minutes, seconds] = parts;
    const s = parseFloat(seconds);
    return parseInt(minutes) * 60 + (isNaN(s) ? 0 : s);
  } else if (parts.length === 3) {
    // Handle HH:MM:SS
    const [hours, minutes, seconds] = parts;
    const s = parseFloat(seconds);
    return parseInt(hours) * 3600 + parseInt(minutes) * 60 + (isNaN(s) ? 0 : s);
  } else if (parts.length === 4) {
    if (cleanTc.includes('.')) {
      // Handle HH:MM:SS.mmm
      const [hours, minutes, seconds, milliseconds] = parts;
      return parseInt(hours) * 3600 + parseInt(minutes) * 60 + parseInt(seconds) + parseInt(milliseconds) / 1000;
    } else {
      // Handle HH:MM:SS:FF (with frames)
      const [hours, minutes, seconds, frames] = parts;
      return parseInt(hours) * 3600 + parseInt(minutes) * 60 + parseInt(seconds) + parseInt(frames) / frameRate;
    }
  }

  return 0;
}

/**
 * Convert seconds to timecode
 * Supports both frame-based (HH:MM:SS:FF) and millisecond-based (HH:MM:SS.mmm) formats
 */
export function secondsToTimecode(seconds: number, frameRate: number = 25, includeFrames: boolean = true): string {
  const hours = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  const secs = Math.floor(seconds % 60);

  const base = `${hours.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${minutes.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;

  if (includeFrames) {
    const frames = Math.floor((seconds % 1) * frameRate);
    return `${base}:${frames.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
  }

  const milliseconds = Math.floor((seconds % 1) * 1000);
  return `${base}.${milliseconds.toString().padStart(3, '0')}`;
}

/**
 * Generate CSV content from headers and rows
 */
export function generateCSV(headers: string[], rows: any[][]): string {
  const escape = (str: string) => {
    if (str === null || str === undefined) return '';
    const s = String(str);
    return s.includes(',') || s.includes('"') || s.includes('\n') ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
  };

  const headerLine = headers.map(escape).join(',');
  const dataLines = rows.map(row => row.map(escape).join(',')).join('\n');

  return `${headerLine}\n${dataLines}`;
}

/**
 * Trigger file download in browser
 */
export function downloadFile(content: string, filename: string, mimeType: string = 'text/plain') {
  const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mimeType });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement('a');

  link.href = url;
  link.download = filename;
  link.style.display = 'none';

  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);

  // Cleanup
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 100);
}

// ... (previous imports if any)

/**
 * Detect most likely timecode format from frame rate
 */
export function detectTimecodeFormat(frameRate: number): TimecodeFormat {
  // Standard broadcast definition: 29.97 and 59.94 usually use Drop Frame
  if (Math.abs(frameRate - 29.97) < 0.01 || Math.abs(frameRate - 59.94) < 0.01) {
    return 'DF';
  }
  return 'NDF';
}

/**
 * Format file size bytes to human readable string (MB/GB)
 */
export function formatFileSize(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
  const k = 1024;
  const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
}

/**
 * Copy text to clipboard using modern Clipboard API
 * Returns true if successful, false otherwise
 */
export async function copyToClipboard(text: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to copy to clipboard:', err);
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Apply a timecode offset to a given timecode string or seconds value
 * @param time The original time (string "HH:MM:SS:FF" or number of seconds)
 * @param offset Timecode string "HH:MM:SS:FF" acting as the start time
 * @param frameRate Video frame rate
 * @returns The new timecode string with offset applied
 */
export function applyTimecodeOffset(
  time: string | number,
  offset: string | undefined | null,
  frameRate: number = 25
): string {
  // 1. Calculate base seconds
  let baseSeconds = 0;
  if (typeof time === 'number') {
    baseSeconds = time;
  } else {
    baseSeconds = parseTimecode(time, frameRate);
  }

  // 2. Calculate offset seconds
  let offsetSeconds = 0;
  if (offset && offset !== '00:00:00:00') {
    offsetSeconds = parseTimecode(offset, frameRate);
  }

  // 3. Combine
  const totalSeconds = baseSeconds + offsetSeconds;

  // 4. Return new timecode
  // Use Drop Frame logic if appropriate for the frame rate
  const format = detectTimecodeFormat(frameRate);
  if (format === 'DF') {
    return secondsToDropFrameTimecode(totalSeconds, frameRate);
  }
  return secondsToTimecode(totalSeconds, frameRate);
}

--- START OF FILE: utils/thumbnailUtils.ts ---

import { parseTimecode } from './tableUtils';

interface Task {
  id: string;
  videoUrl: string;
  time: number;
  originalTimecode?: string; // Store original string to retry parsing if needed
  resolve: (dataUrl: string) => void;
  reject: (err: any) => void;
}

class ThumbnailGenerator {
  private stack: Task[] = [];
  private isProcessing = false;
  private videoElement: HTMLVideoElement | null = null;
  private canvasElement: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
  private currentVideoUrl: string | null = null;

  constructor() {
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
      this.videoElement = document.createElement('video');
      this.videoElement.muted = true;
      this.videoElement.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
      this.videoElement.preload = 'auto';
      this.videoElement.playsInline = true;

      // FORCE DOM ATTACHMENT: Browsers deprioritize detached video rendering
      // Styles to keep it hidden but "visible" to the layout engine
      this.videoElement.style.position = 'fixed';
      this.videoElement.style.top = '-9999px';
      this.videoElement.style.left = '-9999px';
      this.videoElement.style.width = '240px';
      this.videoElement.style.height = '135px';
      this.videoElement.style.opacity = '0';
      this.videoElement.style.pointerEvents = 'none';

      document.body.appendChild(this.videoElement);

      this.canvasElement = document.createElement('canvas');
    }
  }

  public generateThumbnail(videoUrl: string, timecode: string | number): Promise<string> {
    const time = typeof timecode === 'string' ? parseTimecode(timecode) : timecode;
    const id = `${videoUrl}-${time}`;

    // Deduplicate: If request exists, remove it (we will re-add at the top of stack)
    this.stack = this.stack.filter(t => t.id !== id);

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      // LIFO: Unshift (add to front) to prioritize most recent request (scrolling down)
      this.stack.unshift({
        id,
        videoUrl,
        time,
        originalTimecode: typeof timecode === 'string' ? timecode : undefined,
        resolve,
        reject
      });
      this.processQueue();
    });
  }

  private async processQueue() {
    if (this.isProcessing || this.stack.length === 0 || !this.videoElement || !this.canvasElement) return;

    this.isProcessing = true;
    const task = this.stack.shift(); // Get newest task

    if (!task) {
      this.isProcessing = false;
      return;
    }

    try {
      // 1. Load Source
      if (this.currentVideoUrl !== task.videoUrl) {
        this.videoElement.src = task.videoUrl;
        this.currentVideoUrl = task.videoUrl;
        await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          const onLoaded = () => { cleanup(); resolve(true); };
          const onError = () => { cleanup(); reject(new Error('Video load failed')); };
          const cleanup = () => {
            this.videoElement?.removeEventListener('loadeddata', onLoaded);
            this.videoElement?.removeEventListener('error', onError);
          };
          this.videoElement?.addEventListener('loadeddata', onLoaded);
          this.videoElement?.addEventListener('error', onError);
        });
      }

      // 2. Validate and Correct Time
      let seekTime = task.time;
      const duration = this.videoElement.duration;

      // Heuristic: If parsed time is way longer than duration, and we have the original string,
      // it's likely a parsing error (e.g. MM:SS:FF parsed as HH:MM:SS)
      if (seekTime > duration && task.originalTimecode) {
        console.warn(`Timecode ${task.originalTimecode} parsed as ${seekTime}s which exceeds duration ${duration}s. Attempting fallback parse.`);

        const parts = task.originalTimecode.split(/[:;]/);
        // If 3 parts (XX:YY:ZZ) and first parse failed, try MM:SS:FF interpretation
        if (parts.length === 3) {
          const m = parseInt(parts[0], 10);
          const s = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
          const f = parseInt(parts[2], 10);
          const alternateTime = (m * 60) + s + (f / 25); // Assume 25fps for fallback

          if (alternateTime <= duration) {
            console.log(`Fallback parse successful: Interpreted ${task.originalTimecode} as MM:SS:FF -> ${alternateTime}s`);
            seekTime = alternateTime;
          }
        }
      }

      // Clamp to duration to avoid seeking past end
      if (seekTime > duration) {
        console.warn(`Clamping seek time ${seekTime}s to duration ${duration}s`);
        seekTime = duration - 0.1; // Seek slightly before end to get a frame
      }

      // 3. Seek to specific time
      this.videoElement.currentTime = seekTime;

      // 4. Wait for seek
      const seekSuccess = await new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
        const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
          cleanup();
          resolve(false); // Seek failed
        }, 6000);

        const onSeeked = () => {
          cleanup();
          resolve(true); // Seek success
        };

        const onError = () => {
          cleanup();
          resolve(false); // Seek failed
        };

        const cleanup = () => {
          clearTimeout(timeoutId);
          this.videoElement?.removeEventListener('seeked', onSeeked);
          this.videoElement?.removeEventListener('error', onError);
        };

        this.videoElement?.addEventListener('seeked', onSeeked);
        this.videoElement?.addEventListener('error', onError);
      });

      // 5. Capture
      if (seekSuccess && this.canvasElement && this.videoElement) {
        // Wait for one paint frame to ensure video buffer is updated
        await new Promise(r => requestAnimationFrame(r));
        // Add a small delay to allow the video decoder to actually render the frame
        // (Fixes issue where seeked fires but frame is stale)
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 450));

        const ctx = this.canvasElement.getContext('2d');
        if (ctx) {
          const videoRatio = this.videoElement.videoWidth / this.videoElement.videoHeight;
          const targetWidth = 240;
          const targetHeight = targetWidth / videoRatio;

          this.canvasElement.width = targetWidth;
          this.canvasElement.height = targetHeight;

          ctx.drawImage(this.videoElement, 0, 0, targetWidth, targetHeight);
          try {
            const dataUrl = this.canvasElement.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.6);
            task.resolve(dataUrl);
            return;
          } catch (e) {
            console.warn('Canvas export failed', e);
          }
        }
      }

      // Fallback (Timeout, Error, or Canvas Fail)
      task.resolve('data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7');

    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Thumbnail generation failed:', error);
      // Transparent pixel fallback
      task.resolve('data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7');
    } finally {
      this.isProcessing = false;
      // Use requestAnimationFrame for smoother UI yielding
      requestAnimationFrame(() => this.processQueue());
    }
  }
}

export const thumbnailGenerator = new ThumbnailGenerator();

--- START OF FILE: utils/retryUtils.ts ---
/**
 * Retries an async operation with exponential backoff.
 * 
 * @param fn The async function to retry.
 * @param retries Maximum number of retries (default: 3).
 * @param initialDelayMs Initial delay in milliseconds (default: 1000).
 * @param backoffFactor Factor to multiply delay by after each failure (default: 2).
 * @param retryOn Error condition to retry on (default: always retry unless aborted).
 */
export async function retryWithBackoff<T>(
    fn: () => Promise<T>,
    retries: number = 3,
    initialDelayMs: number = 1000,
    backoffFactor: number = 2,
    retryOn: (error: any) => boolean = () => true
): Promise<T> {
    let attempt = 0;
    let delay = initialDelayMs;

    while (true) {
        try {
            return await fn();
        } catch (error: any) {
            // Never retry on AbortSignal
            if (error.name === 'AbortError' || error.message === 'Operation Cancelled' || error?.message?.includes('cancelled')) {
                throw error;
            }

            if (attempt >= retries || !retryOn(error)) {
                console.error(`Retry failed after ${attempt} attempts. Last error:`, error);
                throw error;
            }

            attempt++;
            console.warn(`Attempt ${attempt}/${retries} failed. Retrying in ${delay}ms...`, error.message);

            await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, delay));
            delay *= backoffFactor;
        }
    }
}

--- START OF FILE: backend/scripts/test-local.ts ---
import fetch from 'node-fetch';

const API_URL = 'http://localhost:8080';

const sleep = (ms: number) => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function runTest() {
    console.log('🚀 Starting Local Backend Test...');

    // 1. Create Job
    const createPayload = {
        gcsUri: "gs://test-bucket/test-video.mp4",
        mimeType: "video/mp4",
        duration: 3600,
        analysisConfig: { fauna: true, transcription: true },
        userId: "test-user-local"
    };

    try {
        console.log('📝 Creating Job...');
        const createRes = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/jobs`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify(createPayload)
        });

        if (!createRes.ok) throw new Error(`Create Failed: ${createRes.statusText}`);
        const jobData = await createRes.json() as { id: string, status: string };
        const jobId = jobData.id;
        console.log(`✅ Job Created! ID: ${jobId}, Status: ${jobData.status}`);

        // If running with "npm run dev", the server likely logs "Cloud Tasks not configured"
        // and we might need to manually trigger the worker if we don't have a local task emulator.
        // However, the user asked for a "dev" script that calls /workers/process directly.
        // If that logic is server-side within the Create Job handler, we don't need to do it here.
        // BUT if the architecture relies on Cloud Tasks to call the worker, and we are skipping Cloud Tasks locally,
        // we must manually trigger the worker OR the server must do it.

        // Let's assume the "dev" mode on the server handles the direct call, OR we simulate it here:
        // SIMULATION: Manually trigger worker for testing purposes if the queue didn't fire.
        // For now, let's poll and see. If it stays 'queued', we trigger the worker manually.

        let status = jobData.status;
        let attempts = 0;

        while (status !== 'completed' && status !== 'failed' && attempts < 30) {
            await sleep(2000);
            attempts++;

            const getRes = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/jobs/${jobId}`);
            const currentJob = await getRes.json() as any;
            status = currentJob.status;

            console.log(`⏳ Poll #${attempts}: Status = ${status}, Progress = ${currentJob.progress?.overall}%`);

            if (status === 'queued' && attempts === 2) {
                console.log('⚡️ Triggering Worker Manually (Simulating Cloud Task)...');
                await fetch(`${API_URL}/workers/process`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ jobId })
                });
            }
        }

        if (status === 'completed') {
            console.log('🎉 Test Passed! Job Completed.');
        } else {
            console.error('❌ Test Failed or Timed Out.');
        }

    } catch (error) {
        console.error('💥 Error:', error);
    }
}

runTest();

import { Timestamp } from 'firebase-admin/firestore';

export interface ModuleStatus {
    status: 'pending' | 'processing' | 'completed' | 'failed';
    progress: number;
    error?: string;
}

export interface JobDocument {
    id: string; // Document ID
    userId: string;
    createdAt: Timestamp;
    updatedAt: Timestamp;
    startedAt?: Timestamp; // Optional, set when processing starts
    hasSubcollections?: boolean; // Flag to indicate if results are stored in subcollections
    status: 'queued' | 'processing' | 'completed' | 'failed';
    input: {
        gcsUri: string;
        mimeType: string;
        duration: number;
        analysisConfig: Record<string, boolean>;
    };
    progress: {
        overall: number;
        currentStage: string;
        modules: {
            fauna: ModuleStatus;
            transcription: ModuleStatus;
            shotList: ModuleStatus;
            synopsis: ModuleStatus;
            graphics: ModuleStatus;
            cast: ModuleStatus;
        };
    };
    results: Record<string, unknown>; // Flexible structure for varied results
    error?: { code: string; message: string };

    // Detailed Analysis Results (Mapped from Frontend)
    analysisResults?: {
        synopsis?: Synopsis;
        bio?: Bio[];
        graphics?: GraphicsAnalysisResult;
        fauna?: SpeciesSighting[];
        transcription?: TranscriptionResult;
        shotList?: ShotListResult;
        full_analysis?: FullAnalysisResult;
    };
}

--- START OF FILE: backend/src/utils/logger.ts ---
export const logger = {
    info: (message: string, meta?: any) => {
        console.log(JSON.stringify({ severity: 'INFO', message, ...meta }));
    },
    warn: (message: string, meta?: any) => {
        console.warn(JSON.stringify({ severity: 'WARNING', message, ...meta }));
    },
    error: (message: string, error?: any) => {
        console.error(JSON.stringify({
            severity: 'ERROR',
            message,
            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : error,
            stack: error instanceof Error ? error.stack : undefined
        }));
    }
};

--- END OF FILE: backend/src/utils/logger.ts ---

--- START OF FILE: backend/src/utils/retryUtils.ts ---
/**
 * Retries a promise-returning function with exponential backoff.
 * 
 * @param fn The function to retry.
 * @param retries Maximum number of retries (default: 3).
 * @param delay Initial delay in milliseconds (default: 1000).
 * @param factor Multiplier for the delay (default: 2).
 */
export async function retryWithBackoff<T>(
    fn: () => Promise<T>,
    retries: number = 3,
    delay: number = 1000,
    factor: number = 2
): Promise<T> {
    try {
        return await fn();
    } catch (error) {
        if (retries <= 0) {
            throw error;
        }

        // Check if error is retryable (e.g., rate limits 429, or 503)
        // For simplicity, we retry most errors except explicit cancellations if we had abort signals
        // But here we just catch generic errors.

        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, delay));
        return retryWithBackoff(fn, retries - 1, delay * factor, factor);
    }
}

--- END OF FILE: backend/src/utils/retryUtils.ts ---

--- START OF FILE: backend/src/controllers/jobs.ts ---
import { Request, Response } from 'express';
import { db } from '../config/firebase';
import { config } from '../config/env';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';
import { CloudTasksClient } from '@google-cloud/tasks';
import { JobDocument, ModuleStatus } from '../types';
import { Timestamp } from 'firebase-admin/firestore';

const tasksClient = new CloudTasksClient();

// Define enhanced type for Cloud Tasks request
interface HttpRequestWithAuth {
    httpMethod: 'POST';
    url: string;
    body: string;
    headers: Record<string, string>;
    oidcToken?: { serviceAccountEmail: string };
}

export const createJob = async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { gcsUri, mimeType, duration, analysisConfig, userId } = req.body;

        if (!gcsUri) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: 'gcsUri is required' });
        }

        // 1. Create Job Document
        const initialModuleStatus: ModuleStatus = { status: 'pending', progress: 0 };

        // Convert duration to number if not already
        const durationNum = typeof duration === 'string' ? parseFloat(duration) : (duration || 0);

        const jobData: Omit<JobDocument, 'id'> = {
            userId: userId || 'anonymous',
            createdAt: Timestamp.now(),
            updatedAt: Timestamp.now(),
            status: 'queued',
            input: {
                gcsUri,
                mimeType: mimeType || 'video/mp4',
                duration: durationNum,
                analysisConfig: analysisConfig || {}
            },
            progress: {
                overall: 0,
                currentStage: 'Initialized',
                modules: {
                    fauna: { ...initialModuleStatus },
                    transcription: { ...initialModuleStatus },
                    shotList: { ...initialModuleStatus },
                    synopsis: { ...initialModuleStatus },
                    graphics: { ...initialModuleStatus },
                    cast: { ...initialModuleStatus }
                }
            },
            results: {}
        };

        const docRef = await db.collection('jobs').add(jobData);
        const jobId = docRef.id;

        logger.info('Job created', { jobId });

        // 2. Dispatch Cloud Task
        if (config.projectId && config.queueName && config.location && config.workerUrl) {
            const parent = tasksClient.queuePath(config.projectId, config.location, config.queueName);

            const taskRequest: HttpRequestWithAuth = {
                httpMethod: 'POST',
                url: config.workerUrl.endsWith('/workers/process')
                    ? config.workerUrl
                    : `${config.workerUrl}/workers/process`,
                body: Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ jobId })).toString('base64'),
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                },
            };

            if (config.serviceAccountEmail) {
                taskRequest.oidcToken = {
                    serviceAccountEmail: config.serviceAccountEmail
                };
            }

            const task = { httpRequest: taskRequest as any }; // Cast to satisfy library type if needed

            const [response] = await tasksClient.createTask({ parent, task });
            logger.info('Cloud Task dispatched', { taskId: response.name });
        } else {
            logger.warn('Cloud Tasks not configured, skipping dispatch (Local Mode?)');
        }

        res.status(201).json({ id: jobId, status: 'queued' });

    } catch (error) {
        logger.error('Error creating job', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal Server Error' });
    }
};

export const getJob = async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { id } = req.params;
        const jobRef = db.collection('jobs').doc(id);
        const doc = await jobRef.get();

        if (!doc.exists) {
            logger.warn(`Job document not found for ID: "${id}"`);
            return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Job not found' });
        }

        const data = doc.data() as Omit<JobDocument, 'id'> & { hasSubcollections?: boolean; results?: Record<string, any> }; // Allow extra fields

        // S5: Result Hydration (GCS Pointers)
        // Check for "isOffloaded" or "gcsUri" in results and fetch content.
        if (data.results) {
            const hydrationPromises: Promise<void>[] = [];

            Object.keys(data.results).forEach(key => {
                const moduleData = data.results![key];
                // Check if this is a pointer
                if (moduleData && moduleData.isOffloaded && moduleData.url) {
                    hydrationPromises.push((async () => {
                        try {
                            // Fetch the JSON content from the signed URL
                            // We use fetch here for simplicity. 
                            // In a high-throughput backend, we might use Storage bucket.file().download() but that requires parsing URI.
                            // The signed URL is already optimized for reading.
                            const response = await fetch(moduleData.url);
                            if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to fetch ${key}: ${response.statusText}`);
                            const json = await response.json();

                            // Inline the data, replacing the pointer 
                            // (Or merge it if you want to keep metadata, but frontend likely expects the raw analysis shape)
                            data.results![key] = json;
                        } catch (e) {
                            logger.error(`Failed to hydrate result for ${key}`, e);
                            data.results![key] = { error: 'Failed to load result' };
                        }
                    })());
                }
            });

            if (hydrationPromises.length > 0) {
                await Promise.all(hydrationPromises);
            }
        }

        // Legacy Subcollection Support (Optional, keep if needed for old jobs)
        if (data.hasSubcollections) {
            const resultsRef = jobRef.collection('results');
            const modules = ['transcription', 'shotList', 'fauna', 'synopsis'];
            const subDocs = await Promise.all(modules.map(m => resultsRef.doc(m).get()));

            if (!data.results) data.results = {};
            subDocs.forEach((subDoc, index) => {
                if (subDoc.exists) {
                    const content = subDoc.data();
                    if (content && content.data) {
                        data.results![modules[index]] = content.data;
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        res.json({ ...data, id: doc.id } as JobDocument);

    } catch (error) {
        logger.error('Error fetching job', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal Server Error' });
    }
};

export const debugLatestJobs = async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const snapshot = await db.collection('jobs')
            .orderBy('createdAt', 'desc')
            .limit(5)
            .get();

        const jobs = snapshot.docs.map(doc => ({
            id: doc.id,
            status: doc.data().status,
            createdAt: doc.data().createdAt
        }));

        res.json({
            count: snapshot.size,
            projectId: process.env.GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT || 'unknown',
            jobs
        });
    } catch (error) {
        logger.error('Debug list failed', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Error' });
    }
};

export const getUploadUrl = async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { fileName, contentType } = req.body;

        if (!fileName || !contentType) {
            logger.warn('[Upload] Missing file info for upload URL request');
            return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Missing fileName or contentType' });
        }

        const data = await storageService.generateUploadUrl(fileName, contentType);

        logger.info(`[Upload] Generated Signed URL for ${fileName}`);
        res.json(data);
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('[Upload] Failed to generate upload URL', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: error.message || 'Failed to generate upload URL' });
    }
};

--- START OF FILE: backend/src/controllers/worker.ts ---
import { Request, Response } from 'express';
import { db } from '../config/firebase';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';
import { Timestamp } from 'firebase-admin/firestore';
import { JobDocument } from '../types';
import { performAnalysis } from '../services/analysisCoordinator';
import { storageService } from '../services/storageService';

export const processJob = async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    const { jobId } = req.body;

    if (!jobId) {
        logger.error('Worker received request without jobId');
        return res.status(400).send('Missing jobId');
    }

    logger.info(`Worker starting job: ${jobId}`, { jobId });

    try {
        const jobRef = db.collection('jobs').doc(jobId);
        let job: JobDocument | null = null;

        // 1. Atomic Transaction to Claim Job
        await db.runTransaction(async (transaction) => {
            const jobDoc = await transaction.get(jobRef);
            if (!jobDoc.exists) {
                throw new Error('JOB_NOT_FOUND');
            }

            job = jobDoc.data() as JobDocument;

            if (job!.status === 'processing') {
                throw new Error('ALREADY_PROCESSING');
            }
            if (job!.status === 'completed') {
                throw new Error('ALREADY_COMPLETED');
            }

            // Lock the job
            transaction.update(jobRef, {
                status: 'processing',
                startedAt: Timestamp.now(),
                updatedAt: Timestamp.now(),
                'progress.currentStage': 'Processing started'
            });
        });

        // 2. Perform Real Analysis (Outside Transaction)
        logger.info(`Starting Analysis Coordination for Job ${jobId}`);
        const results = await performAnalysis(jobId, job!);

        // 3. Offload Large Results to GCS (The Fix)
        // Instead of writing massive JSONs to subcollections, we upload to GCS and store pointers.

        const modulesToOffload = ['transcription', 'shotList', 'fauna', 'synopsis', 'graphics'];

        for (const module of modulesToOffload) {
            const data = results[module as keyof typeof results];
            if (data) {
                const pointer = await storageService.saveLargeResult(jobId, module, data);
                // Replace large data with pointer in the results object
                results[module as keyof typeof results] = pointer as any;
            }
        }

        // 4. Complete Job (Update Main Doc with formatted results)
        await jobRef.update({
            status: 'completed',
            'progress.overall': 100,
            'progress.currentStage': 'Analysis Complete',
            updatedAt: Timestamp.now(),
            results: results,
            hasSubcollections: false // We are using GCS pointers in the main doc now
        });

        logger.info(`Worker completed job: ${jobId}`);
        res.status(200).send('Job processed successfully');

    } catch (error: any) {
        if (error.message === 'ALREADY_PROCESSING' || error.message === 'ALREADY_COMPLETED') {
            logger.warn(`Skipping duplicate task for ${jobId}: ${error.message}`);
            return res.status(200).send('Skipped duplicate');
        }
        if (error.message === 'JOB_NOT_FOUND') {
            logger.error(`Job ${jobId} not found`);
            return res.status(404).send('Job not found');
        }

        logger.error(`Worker failed processing job: ${jobId}`, error);

        // Mark as failed in DB
        const isRetryable = error.code === 429 || error.code === 503 || error.message.includes('timeout') || error.message.includes('network');

        if (isRetryable) {
            logger.warn(`Transient error for ${jobId}, triggering Cloud Task retry`, error);
            return res.status(500).send('Transient failure');
        }

        await db.collection('jobs').doc(jobId).update({
            status: 'failed',
            error: {
                code: 'WORKER_ERROR',
                message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown worker error'
            },
            updatedAt: Timestamp.now()
        }).catch(e => logger.error('Failed to update job status to failed', e));

        res.status(200).send('Job failed (terminal)');
    }
};

--- END OF FILE: backend/src/controllers/worker.ts ---

--- START OF FILE: backend/src/services/analysisCoordinator.ts ---
import { Storage } from '@google-cloud/storage';
import { GoogleAIFileManager, FileState } from "@google/generative-ai/server";
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';
import * as os from 'os';
import * as https from 'https';
import { db } from '../config/firebase';
import { config } from '../config/env';
import { JobDocument, FullAnalysisResult } from '../types';
import * as geminiService from './geminiService';
import * as videoIntelligenceService from './videoIntelligenceService';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';
import { pipeline } from 'stream/promises';

const storage = new Storage();
if (!config.geminiApiKey) {
    logger.warn("GEMINI_API_KEY is missing. Gemini File Manager will fail.");
}
const fileManager = new GoogleAIFileManager(config.geminiApiKey || '');

/**
 * Main Orchestrator for Video Analysis.
 */
export async function performAnalysis(jobId: string, job: JobDocument): Promise<Partial<FullAnalysisResult>> {
    logger.info(`Starting Analysis Coordinator for Job ${jobId}`);

    const { gcsUri, analysisConfig } = job.input;
    // Removed tempFilePath logic (No Disk Usage)
    let geminiFileUri: string | null = null;
    let geminiFileName: string | null = null;
    const results: Partial<FullAnalysisResult> = {};

    try {
        // 1. PREPARATION: Stream to Gemini (if needed)
        const needsGemini = analysisConfig.synopsis || analysisConfig.fauna ||
            analysisConfig.graphics || analysisConfig.transcription || analysisConfig.cast;

        if (needsGemini) {
            await updateProgress(jobId, 'preparing', 5, 'Streaming video from GCS to Gemini...');

            // STREAMING UPLOAD (No Disk Buffer)
            const uploadResult = await streamGcsToGemini(gcsUri, 'video/mp4', config.geminiApiKey || '');

            geminiFileUri = uploadResult.uri;
            geminiFileName = uploadResult.name; // "files/..." name

            await updateProgress(jobId, 'preparing', 15, 'Waiting for Gemini processing...');
            await waitForGeminiFileActive(geminiFileName);
        }

        // 2. PARALLEL EXECUTION
        const promises: Promise<any>[] = [];

        // --- A. Video Intelligence ---
        if (analysisConfig.videoIntelligence) {
            promises.push((async () => {
                try {
                    await updateModuleStatus(jobId, 'shotList', 'processing', 0);
                    // Use AnnotateVideo directly (already supports GCS URI natively)
                    await videoIntelligenceService.annotateVideo(gcsUri, ['SHOT_CHANGE_DETECTION', 'LABEL_DETECTION'] as any);
                    await updateModuleStatus(jobId, 'shotList', 'completed', 100);
                } catch (e: any) {
                    logger.error("Video Intelligence Failed", e);
                    await updateModuleStatus(jobId, 'shotList', 'failed', 0, e.message);
                }
            })());
        }

        // --- B. Gemini Modules ---
        if (geminiFileUri) {
            // 1. Synopsis
            if (analysisConfig.synopsis) {
                promises.push(runModule(jobId, 'synopsis', async () => {
                    const analysis = await geminiService.analyzeVideoContent(geminiFileUri!, 'video/mp4');
                    return geminiService.generateSynopsisFromAnalysis(
                        analysis, 'AI Writer', 'AI Director', 'English', '', job.input.analysisConfig as any // reusing config
                    );
                }, results));
            }

            // 2. Fauna
            if (analysisConfig.fauna) {
                promises.push(runModule(jobId, 'fauna', async () => {
                    return geminiService.analyzeVideoForSpecies(geminiFileUri!, 'video/mp4', 'English', 'German', job.input.duration || 0);
                }, results));
            }

            // 3. Graphics
            if (analysisConfig.graphics) {
                promises.push(runModule(jobId, 'graphics', async () => {
                    return geminiService.analyzeGraphicsAndLowerThirds(geminiFileUri!, 'video/mp4', job.input.duration || 0, 'Unknown Title');
                }, results));
            }

            // 4. Transcription
            if (analysisConfig.transcription) {
                promises.push(runModule(jobId, 'transcription', async () => {
                    return geminiService.transcribeMedia(geminiFileUri!, 'video/mp4');
                }, results));
            }
        }

        await Promise.all(promises);
        return results;

    } catch (error) {
        logger.error("Analysis Coordinator Critical Error", error);
        throw error;
    } finally {
        if (geminiFileName) {
            try {
                await fileManager.deleteFile(geminiFileName);
                logger.info(`Deleted Gemini file: ${geminiFileName}`);
            } catch (e) {
                logger.warn(`Failed to delete Gemini file: ${geminiFileName}`, e);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Helper to standardise module execution & error handling
async function runModule(jobId: string, moduleName: string, action: () => Promise<any>, resultsObj: any) {
    try {
        await updateModuleStatus(jobId, moduleName, 'processing', 0);
        const res = await action();
        resultsObj[moduleName] = res;
        await updateModuleStatus(jobId, moduleName, 'completed', 100);
    } catch (e: any) {
        logger.error(`Module ${moduleName} Failed`, e);
        await updateModuleStatus(jobId, moduleName, 'failed', 0, e.message);
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// STREAMING UPLOAD IMPLEMENTATION
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

async function streamGcsToGemini(gcsUri: string, mimeType: string, apiKey: string): Promise<{ name: string, uri: string }> {
    const matches = gcsUri.match(/gs:\/\/([^\/]+)\/(.+)/);
    if (!matches) throw new Error(`Invalid GCS URI: ${gcsUri}`);
    const bucketName = matches[1];
    const fileName = matches[2];

    const bucket = storage.bucket(bucketName);
    const gcsFile = bucket.file(fileName);
    const [metadata] = await gcsFile.getMetadata();
    const fileSize = parseInt(metadata.size as string, 10);

    logger.info(`Starting stream upload for ${fileName} (${fileSize} bytes)`);

    // 1. Start Resumable Upload Session (Gemini API)
    const initRes = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/upload/v1beta/files?key=${apiKey}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
            'X-Goog-Upload-Protocol': 'resumable',
            'X-Goog-Upload-Command': 'start',
            'X-Goog-Upload-Header-Content-Length': fileSize.toString(),
            'X-Goog-Upload-Header-Content-Type': mimeType,
            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ file: { display_name: fileName } })
    });

    if (!initRes.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to init upload: ${initRes.statusText}`);
    const uploadUrl = initRes.headers.get('x-goog-upload-url');
    if (!uploadUrl) throw new Error("No upload URL returned");

    // 2. Pipe Stream
    // We use Node's native https to pipe the stream because fetch with duplex streams can be tricky in some environments

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const readStream = gcsFile.createReadStream();

        const req = https.request(uploadUrl, {
            method: 'POST', // or PUT depending on command? Actually for resumable 'upload, finalize' usually PUT or POST. standard is PUT for bytes.
            // But wait, the uploadUrl is unique.
            // X-Goog-Upload-Command: upload, finalize
            headers: {
                'Content-Length': fileSize,
                'X-Goog-Upload-Offset': '0',
                'X-Goog-Upload-Command': 'upload, finalize'
            }
        }, (res) => {
            let data = '';
            res.on('data', (chunk) => data += chunk);
            res.on('end', () => {
                if (res.statusCode && res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300) {
                    try {
                        const json = JSON.parse(data);
                        logger.info(`Stream upload complete: ${json.file.uri}`);
                        resolve(json.file);
                    } catch (e) {
                        reject(e);
                    }
                } else {
                    reject(new Error(`Upload failed with status ${res.statusCode}: ${data}`));
                }
            });
        });

        req.on('error', (e) => reject(e));

        // Pipe GCS stream to Request
        readStream.pipe(req);
    });
}

async function waitForGeminiFileActive(name: string) {
    logger.info(`Waiting for Gemini file ${name} to be ACTIVE...`);
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const TIMEOUT_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000;
    let file = await fileManager.getFile(name);
    while (file.state === FileState.PROCESSING) {
        if (Date.now() - startTime > TIMEOUT_MS) throw new Error(`Timeout ${name}`);
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2000));
        file = await fileManager.getFile(name);
    }
    if (file.state === FileState.FAILED) throw new Error("Gemini Processing Failed");
    logger.info("Gemini File ACTIVE");
}

async function updateProgress(jobId: string, stage: string, percentage: number, message: string) {
    await db.collection('jobs').doc(jobId).update({
        'progress.overall': percentage,
        'progress.currentStage': message,
        updatedAt: new Date()
    });
}

async function updateModuleStatus(jobId: string, module: string, status: string, progress: number, error?: string) {
    const update: any = { [`progress.modules.${module}.status`]: status, [`progress.modules.${module}.progress`]: progress };
    if (error) update[`progress.modules.${module}.error`] = error;
    await db.collection('jobs').doc(jobId).update(update);
}

--- START OF FILE: backend/src/services/videoIntelligenceService.ts ---
import { v1 } from '@google-cloud/video-intelligence';
import { google } from '@google-cloud/video-intelligence/build/protos/protos';

const client = new v1.VideoIntelligenceServiceClient();

export type Feature = google.cloud.videointelligence.v1.Feature;

export interface VideoAnnotationResults extends google.cloud.videointelligence.v1.IVideoAnnotationResults { }

/**
 * Annotates a video using Google Cloud Video Intelligence API.
 * 
 * @param gcsUri The GCS URI of the video (gs://...).
 * @param features List of features to detect.
 * @param context Optional video context.
 */
export async function annotateVideo(
    gcsUri: string,
    features: Feature[],
    context?: google.cloud.videointelligence.v1.IVideoContext
): Promise<VideoAnnotationResults> {
    console.log(`Starting Video Intelligence Analysis for: ${gcsUri}`);

    const request = {
        inputUri: gcsUri,
        features: features,
        videoContext: context,
    };

    try {
        // Start the Long Running Operation
        const [operation] = await client.annotateVideo(request);
        console.log(`Video Intelligence LRO started: ${operation.name}`);

        // Wait for operation to complete (with customized 30m timeout)
        const timeoutMs = 30 * 60 * 1000; // 30 minutes

        const resultPromise = operation.promise();

        const timeoutPromise = new Promise<never>((_, reject) =>
            setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Video Intelligence Analysis timed out after 30 minutes')), timeoutMs)
        );

        const [operationResult] = await Promise.race([resultPromise, timeoutPromise]);

        // The result is an array of annotation results (one per input video)
        const annotationResults = operationResult.annotationResults;

        if (annotationResults && annotationResults.length > 0) {
            return annotationResults[0];
        } else {
            throw new Error('Video Intelligence completed but returned no results.');
        }

    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Video Intelligence API Error:', error);
        throw error;
    }
}
