--- START OF FILE: backend/src/services/storageService.ts ---
import { Storage } from '@google-cloud/storage';
import { db } from '../config/firebase'; // Using existing firestore instance
import { config } from '../config/env';

const storage = new Storage();

// Use env vars or defaults
const UPLOAD_BUCKET = process.env.VITE_GOOGLE_CLOUD_STORAGE_BUCKET || 'gemini-video-uploads';
const RESULTS_BUCKET = process.env.GCS_RESULTS_BUCKET || 'gemini-video-results';

export const storageService = {
    /**
     * Generates a signed URL so the frontend uploads directly to Google.
     */
    async generateUploadUrl(fileName: string, contentType: string) {
        // Sanitize filename to prevent issues
        const cleanName = fileName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9.-]/g, '_');
        const uniqueName = `uploads/${Date.now()}_${cleanName}`;
        const file = storage.bucket(UPLOAD_BUCKET).file(uniqueName);

        // V4 Signed URL
        const [url] = await file.getSignedUrl({
            version: 'v4',
            action: 'write',
            expires: Date.now() + 60 * 60 * 1000, // 60 minutes
            contentType,
        });

        return {
            uploadUrl: url,
            gcsUri: `gs://${UPLOAD_BUCKET}/${uniqueName}`
        };
    },

    /**
     * Saves large analysis results to GCS and writes a pointer to Firestore.
     */
    async saveLargeResult(jobId: string, module: string, data: any) {
        const fileName = `jobs/${jobId}/${module}.json`;
        const file = storage.bucket(RESULTS_BUCKET).file(fileName);

        // 1. Upload JSON to GCS
        await file.save(JSON.stringify(data), {
            contentType: 'application/json',
            resumable: false,
            metadata: { cacheControl: 'public, max-age=31536000' }
        });

        // 2. Generate a Read-Only Signed URL (Valid for 7 days)
        const [readUrl] = await file.getSignedUrl({
            version: 'v4',
            action: 'read',
            expires: Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
        });

        console.log(`[Storage] Offloaded ${module} for ${jobId} to GCS`);

        // Return the pointer data
        return {
            status: 'completed',
            url: readUrl, // Signed URL for frontend/backend immediate access
            gcsUri: `gs://${RESULTS_BUCKET}/${fileName}`, // Permanent reference
            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            isOffloaded: true // Flag to help hydration
        };
    }
};

--- END OF FILE: backend/src/services/storageService.ts ---

--- START OF FILE: backend/src/services/geminiService.ts ---
import { GoogleGenerativeAI, SchemaType as Type } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { config } from '../config/env';
import {
    AnalysisResult,
    Synopsis,
    SynopsisConfig,
    GraphicsAnalysisResult,
    TranscriptionResult,
    SpeciesSighting
} from '../types';

// Initialize the Official SDK
const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(config.geminiApiKey || '');

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// UTILITY FUNCTIONS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

function repairJson(jsonString: string): string {
    let fixed = jsonString.trim();
    // 1. Remove Markdown code blocks
    fixed = fixed.replace(/^```(json)?|```$/g, '');
    // 2. Extract JSON object/array if embedded in text
    const jsonMatch = fixed.match(/(\{[\s\S]*\}|\[[\s\S]*\])/);
    if (jsonMatch) {
        fixed = jsonMatch[0];
    }
    // 3. Fix trailing commas (common AI error)
    fixed = fixed.replace(/,(\s*[}\]])/g, '$1');
    return fixed;
}

function safeJsonParse<T>(jsonString: string): T {
    try {
        const cleanString = repairJson(jsonString);
        if (!cleanString) throw new Error("Empty JSON response");
        return JSON.parse(cleanString);
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn("Initial JSON parse failed, attempting aggressive repair...", e);
        try {
            let aggressiveFix = jsonString.replace(/,(\s*)/g, ',$1');
            aggressiveFix = aggressiveFix.replace(/([}\]])[\s\S]*$/, '$1');
            return JSON.parse(repairJson(aggressiveFix));
        } catch (e2) {
            console.error("Failed to parse JSON response:", jsonString, e2);
            throw new Error("INVALID_API_RESPONSE");
        }
    }
}

function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
    if (isNaN(seconds) || seconds < 0) return "00:00:00";
    const totalSeconds = Math.floor(seconds);
    const h = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600), m = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60), s = totalSeconds % 60;
    return [h, m, s].map(v => String(v).padStart(2, '0')).join(':');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SYNOPSIS GENERATION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export const analyzeVideoContent = async (
    fileUri: string,
    mimeType: string
): Promise<AnalysisResult> => {

    try {
        const prompt = `
      You are an expert video analysis AI. Analyze the provided video file.
      Your task is to synthesize all information into a final, cohesive analysis in JSON format.
      - Transcribe spoken words if present.
      - Summarize the entire video in one sentence.
      - Create a single, chronological list of the most important key events.
      - Infer any potential dialogue or important sounds based on visual cues if audio is unclear.
      
      **CRITICAL:** Base all descriptions strictly on the video content. Do not invent details not present in the video.
    `;

        const modelConfig: any = {
            model: 'gemini-1.5-pro', // Use 1.5 Pro for backend analysis (better context)
            generationConfig: {
                responseMimeType: 'application/json',
                responseSchema: {
                    type: Type.OBJECT,
                    properties: {
                        summary: { type: Type.STRING, description: "A brief, one-sentence summary of the video's content." },
                        events: {
                            type: Type.ARRAY,
                            items: {
                                type: Type.OBJECT,
                                properties: {
                                    timestamp: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Approximate time or sequence of the event (e.g., '00:05', 'Beginning', 'Middle')." },
                                    description: { type: Type.STRING, description: "A detailed description of what is happening in the event." },
                                },
                                required: ['timestamp', 'description'],
                            },
                        },
                        inferred_dialogue: { type: Type.STRING, description: "An inferred transcription of what might be said, based on visual cues." },
                        transcription: { type: Type.STRING, description: "A transcription of the spoken content from the audio track. If no audio was provided, state that." },
                    },
                    required: ['summary', 'events', 'inferred_dialogue', 'transcription'],
                },
            }
        };

        const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel(modelConfig);

        const result = await model.generateContent([
            { fileData: { fileUri: fileUri, mimeType: mimeType } },
            { text: prompt }
        ]);

        const rawText = result.response.text() || '';
        return safeJsonParse<AnalysisResult>(rawText);

    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error("Error analyzing video with Gemini:", error);
        throw new Error(error.message || "ANALYSIS_FAIL");
    }
};

export const generateSynopsisFromAnalysis = async (
    analysis: AnalysisResult,
    writer: string,
    director: string,
    language: string,
    keywords: string,
    synopsisConfig: SynopsisConfig
): Promise<Synopsis> => {

    try {
        const prompt = `
          You are a master screenwriter and film marketing expert. Your task is to transform raw video analysis data into a compelling, professional film synopsis and marketing profile. Your writing should be evocative and ready for a pitch deck.

          **Your Constraints:**
          - **Language:** Generate all content in: ${language}.
          - **Title:** Maximum ${synopsisConfig.titleMaxChars} characters.
          - **One Line (Logline):** Approximately ${synopsisConfig.loglineMaxWords} words.
          - **Paragraph (Summary):** Approximately ${synopsisConfig.summaryMaxWords} words.
          - **Key Themes:** Approximately ${synopsisConfig.keyThemesMaxWords} words.
          - **Target Audience:** Approximately ${synopsisConfig.targetAudienceMaxWords} words.
          
          **Video Analysis Data:**
          - Transcription: "${analysis.transcription}"
          - Summary: "${analysis.summary}"
          - Key Events: ${JSON.stringify(analysis.events.map(e => e.description))}
          - Inferred Dialogue: "${analysis.inferred_dialogue}"

          Your output must be a single JSON object with keys: "title", "oneLine", "paragraph", "fullSynopsis", "keyThemes", "targetAudience".
      `;

        const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
            model: 'gemini-1.5-pro',
            generationConfig: {
                responseMimeType: 'application/json',
                responseSchema: {
                    type: Type.OBJECT,
                    properties: {
                        title: { type: Type.STRING },
                        oneLine: { type: Type.STRING },
                        paragraph: { type: Type.STRING },
                        fullSynopsis: { type: Type.STRING },
                        keyThemes: { type: Type.STRING },
                        targetAudience: { type: Type.STRING }
                    },
                    required: ['title', 'oneLine', 'paragraph', 'fullSynopsis', 'keyThemes', 'targetAudience']
                }
            }
        });

        const result = await model.generateContent(prompt);
        const aiGeneratedContent = safeJsonParse<any>(result.response.text() || '');

        return {
            ...aiGeneratedContent,
            writtenBy: writer,
            directedBy: director,
        };

    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error("Error generating synopsis with Gemini:", error);
        throw new Error("SYNOPSIS_FAIL");
    }
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// GRAPHICS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export async function analyzeGraphicsAndLowerThirds(
    fileUri: string, mimeType: string, duration: number, filmTitle: string
): Promise<GraphicsAnalysisResult> {

    const prompt = `You are a post-production assistant with excellent OCR skills. Analyze the provided video file. Your task is to identify and transcribe all on-screen graphics (e.g., title cards, location indicators) and lower thirds. Output strictly JSON.`;

    try {
        const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
            model: 'gemini-1.5-flash', // Flash is better for OCR/Vision tasks usually
            generationConfig: {
                responseMimeType: 'application/json',
                temperature: 0.2,
                responseSchema: {
                    type: Type.OBJECT,
                    properties: {
                        programTitle: { type: Type.STRING },
                        runtime: { type: Type.STRING },
                        graphics: {
                            type: Type.ARRAY,
                            items: {
                                type: Type.OBJECT, properties: {
                                    description: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    timecodeIn: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    timecodeOut: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    cleanTimecodeIn: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    cleanTimecodeOut: { type: Type.STRING },
                                },
                                required: ["description", "timecodeIn", "timecodeOut"],
                            },
                        },
                        lowerThirds: {
                            type: Type.ARRAY,
                            items: {
                                type: Type.OBJECT, properties: {
                                    text: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    timecodeIn: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    timecodeOut: { type: Type.STRING }
                                },
                                required: ["text", "timecodeIn", "timecodeOut"],
                            },
                        },
                    },
                    required: ["programTitle", "runtime", "graphics", "lowerThirds"],
                },
            }
        });

        const result = await model.generateContent([
            { fileData: { fileUri: fileUri, mimeType: mimeType } },
            { text: prompt }
        ]);

        const parsedResult = safeJsonParse<GraphicsAnalysisResult>(result.response.text() || '');
        parsedResult.programTitle = filmTitle;
        parsedResult.runtime = formatDuration(duration);
        return parsedResult;
    } catch (e) {
        console.error("Error analyzing graphics:", e);
        throw new Error("Graphics analysis failed.");
    }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FAUNA FINDER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export async function analyzeVideoForSpecies(
    fileUri: string,
    mimeType: string,
    primaryLanguage: string,
    secondaryLanguage: string,
    videoDuration: number = 0
): Promise<Omit<SpeciesSighting, 'id' | 'thumbnail'>[]> {

    const prompt = `Analyze this video. Your task is to identify **every single animal sighting**. 
    Provide: 
    - 'commonName': The common name in ${primaryLanguage}.
    - 'latinName': The scientific name.
    - 'germanName': The common name in ${secondaryLanguage}.
    - 'timecode': The EXACT timecode.
    - 'category': (e.g., "Mammal", "Bird").
    - 'confidence': 0.0 to 1.0.
    - 'behavior': Brief description.
    - 'iucnStatus': IUCN status if known.
    - 'encounterGroup': Group ID if same individual seen multiple times.
    
    The final output must be a valid JSON object with a single key "sightings".`;

    try {
        const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
            model: 'gemini-1.5-flash',
            generationConfig: {
                responseMimeType: "application/json",
                responseSchema: {
                    type: Type.OBJECT,
                    properties: {
                        sightings: {
                            type: Type.ARRAY,
                            items: {
                                type: Type.OBJECT,
                                properties: {
                                    commonName: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    latinName: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    germanName: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    timecode: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    category: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    confidence: { type: Type.NUMBER },
                                    behavior: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    iucnStatus: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    encounterGroup: { type: Type.STRING },
                                },
                                required: ["commonName", "latinName", "timecode", "category", "confidence"],
                            },
                        },
                    },
                    required: ["sightings"],
                },
            },
        });

        const result = await model.generateContent([
            { fileData: { fileUri: fileUri, mimeType: mimeType } },
            { text: prompt }
        ]);

        const parsed = safeJsonParse<any>(result.response.text() || '');
        return parsed.sightings || [];

    } catch (e) {
        console.error(`Error analyzing fauna:`, e);
        throw new Error("Fauna analysis failed.");
    }
}

export async function transcribeMedia(
    fileUri: string, mimeType: string
): Promise<Omit<TranscriptionResult, 'speakerMap'>> {

    const prompt = `You are an expert audio transcription AI. Transcribe the provided media, identifying different speakers as SPEAKER_00, SPEAKER_01, etc. Provide the output as a time-coded transcript in a JSON object adhering to the schema.`;

    try {
        const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
            model: 'gemini-1.5-flash',
            generationConfig: {
                responseMimeType: 'application/json',
                responseSchema: {
                    type: Type.OBJECT,
                    properties: {
                        segments: {
                            type: Type.ARRAY,
                            items: {
                                type: Type.OBJECT,
                                properties: {
                                    startTime: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    endTime: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    speaker: { type: Type.STRING },
                                    text: { type: Type.STRING },
                                },
                                required: ["startTime", "endTime", "speaker", "text"],
                            },
                        },
                    },
                    required: ["segments"],
                },
            }
        });

        const result = await model.generateContent([
            { fileData: { fileUri: fileUri, mimeType: mimeType } },
            { text: prompt }
        ]);

        const parsed = safeJsonParse<any>(result.response.text() || '');
        return parsed;

    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error("Transcription error", error);
        throw new Error("Transcription Failed");
    }
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FullAnalysisResult, VideoMetadata, ProjectMetadata, DEFAULT_FAUNA_CONFIG } from '../types';
import ShotListResultView from './ShotListResultView';
import TranscriptionResultView from './TranscriptionResultView';
import SynopsisResultView from './SynopsisResultView';
import BioResultView from './BioResultView';
import FaunaResultView from './FaunaResultView';
import GraphicsResultView from './GraphicsResultView';

import { Clapperboard, MessageSquare, Image, FileText, BookOpen, Users, Bug } from 'lucide-react';
import ExportModal from './ExportModal';

interface FullAnalysisResultViewProps {
    result: FullAnalysisResult;
    videoPreviewUrl: string | null;
    videoFileName: string;
    videoMetadata: VideoMetadata | null;
    projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata;
    onReset: () => void;
    onBack: () => void;
    onSave: (data: FullAnalysisResult) => Promise<void>;
    onUpdateSpeaker: (speakerId: string, newName: string) => void;
}

const FullAnalysisResultView: React.FC<FullAnalysisResultViewProps> = ({
    result,
    videoPreviewUrl,
    videoFileName,
    videoMetadata,
    projectMetadata,
    onReset,
    onBack,
    onSave,
    onUpdateSpeaker
}) => {
    if (!result) return null;

    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'synopsis' | 'bio' | 'shots' | 'transcription' | 'graphics' | 'fauna'>('synopsis');
    const [showExportModal, setShowExportModal] = useState(false);

    const tabs = [
        { id: 'synopsis', label: 'Synopsis', icon: <BookOpen size={20} /> },
        { id: 'transcription', label: 'Transcription', icon: <MessageSquare size={20} /> },
        { id: 'shots', label: 'Shot List', icon: <Clapperboard size={20} /> },
        { id: 'bio', label: 'Bio', icon: <Users size={20} /> },
        { id: 'graphics', label: 'Graphics', icon: <Image size={20} /> },
        { id: 'fauna', label: 'Fauna', icon: <Bug size={20} /> },
    ];

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
            {/* Tab Navigation */}
            <div className="flex border-b border-gray-700 justify-between items-center bg-gray-900/50 rounded-t-lg overflow-x-auto">
                <div className="flex">
                    {tabs.map((tab) => (
                        <button
                            key={tab.id}
                            onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id as any)}
                            className={`
                  flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap
                  ${activeTab === tab.id
                                    ? 'text-purple-400 border-b-2 border-purple-500 bg-gray-800/50'
                                    : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-800/30'}
                `}
                        >
                            {tab.icon}
                            {tab.label}
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                {/* As-Broadcast Export Button */}
                <div className="pr-4 hidden md:block">
                    <button
                        onClick={() => setShowExportModal(true)}
                        disabled={!result.transcription || !result.shotList}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded text-sm font-medium transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                    >
                        <FileText size={16} />
                        Export Options...
                    </button>

                    <ExportModal
                        isOpen={showExportModal}
                        onClose={() => setShowExportModal(false)}
                        type="asbroadcast"
                        data={{
                            transcription: result.transcription,
                            shotList: { shots: result.shotList as any, videoUrl: '', frameRate: videoMetadata?.frameRate || 24, resolution: '1080p' },
                            graphics: result.graphics
                        }}
                        projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
                        videoFileName={videoFileName}
                        frameRate={videoMetadata?.frameRate || 24}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Tab Content */}
            <div className="min-h-[600px] bg-gray-900/20 p-4 rounded-b-lg">
                {activeTab === 'synopsis' && (
                    result.synopsis ? (
                        <div className="animate-fade-in">
                            <SynopsisResultView
                                synopsis={result.synopsis}
                                videoFileName={videoFileName}
                                videoMetadata={videoMetadata}
                                projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
                                onReset={onReset}
                                onBack={onBack}
                                onSave={async () => Promise.resolve()}
                            />
                        </div>
                    ) : <LoadingState label="Generating Synopsis..." />
                )}

                {activeTab === 'bio' && (
                    result.bio ? (
                        <div className="animate-fade-in">
                            <BioResultView
                                bios={result.bio}
                                videoFileName={videoFileName}
                                projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
                                onReset={onReset}
                                onBack={onBack}
                                onSave={async () => Promise.resolve()}
                            />
                        </div>
                    ) : <LoadingState label="Identifying Talent..." />
                )}

                {activeTab === 'shots' && (
                    result.shotList ? (
                        <div className="animate-fade-in">
                            <ShotListResultView
                                result={{ shots: result.shotList as any, videoUrl: videoPreviewUrl || '', frameRate: videoMetadata?.frameRate || 24 }}
                                videoPreviewUrl={videoPreviewUrl}
                                videoFileName={videoFileName}
                                videoMetadata={videoMetadata}
                                projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
                                onReset={onReset}
                                onBack={onBack}
                                onSave={async () => Promise.resolve()}
                            />
                        </div>
                    ) : <LoadingState label="Analyzing Shots..." />
                )}

                {activeTab === 'transcription' && (
                    result.transcription ? (
                        <div className="animate-fade-in">
                            <TranscriptionResultView
                                result={result.transcription}
                                videoPreviewUrl={videoPreviewUrl}
                                videoFileName={videoFileName}
                                videoMetadata={videoMetadata}
                                projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
                                graphicsResult={result.graphics}
                                onReset={onReset}
                                onBack={onBack}
                                onSave={async () => Promise.resolve()}
                                onUpdateSpeaker={onUpdateSpeaker}
                                onUpdateSegment={() => console.log('Segment update not supported in full view yet')}
                            />
                        </div>
                    ) : <LoadingState label="Transcribing Audio..." />
                )}

                {activeTab === 'graphics' && (
                    result.graphics ? (
                        <div className="animate-fade-in">
                            <GraphicsResultView
                                result={result.graphics}
                                videoPreviewUrl={videoPreviewUrl}
                                videoFileName={videoFileName}
                                videoMetadata={videoMetadata}
                                projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
                                onReset={onReset}
                                onBack={onBack}
                                onSave={async () => Promise.resolve()}
                            />
                        </div>
                    ) : <LoadingState label="Detecting Graphics..." />
                )}

                {activeTab === 'fauna' && (
                    result.fauna ? (
                        <div className="animate-fade-in">
                            <FaunaResultView
                                sightings={result.fauna}
                                videoFileName={videoFileName}
                                videoPreviewUrl={videoPreviewUrl}
                                videoMetadata={videoMetadata}
                                projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
                                onReset={onReset}
                                onBack={onBack}
                                onSave={async () => Promise.resolve()}
                                config={DEFAULT_FAUNA_CONFIG}
                            />
                        </div>
                    ) : <LoadingState label="Scanning for Fauna..." />
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const LoadingState: React.FC<{ label: string }> = ({ label }) => (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-12 text-gray-400 h-96">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 border-4 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mb-4"></div>
        <p className="text-lg font-medium">{label}</p>
        <p className="text-sm opacity-50 mt-2">This module is processing in the background.</p>
    </div>
);

--- START OF FILE: components/FeedbackModal.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Send, Bug, AlertTriangle, Lightbulb, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react';

interface FeedbackModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
}

type FeedbackType = 'bug' | 'error' | 'wish';

const FeedbackModal: React.FC<FeedbackModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
    const [type, setType] = useState<FeedbackType>('bug');
    const [comment, setComment] = useState('');

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        const subject = `[Gemini App Feedback] - ${type.toUpperCase()}`;
        const body = `Type: ${type.toUpperCase()}\n\nComment:\n${comment}\n\n---\nSent from Gemini Super App`;

        const mailtoLink = `mailto:nvrdeliverables@gmail.com?subject=${encodeURIComponent(subject)}&body=${encodeURIComponent(body)}`;

        // Open default mail client
        window.location.href = mailtoLink;

        // Optional: Close modal after sending or show success message
        onClose();
    };

    const getTypeIcon = (t: FeedbackType) => {
        switch (t) {
            case 'bug': return <Bug size={18} />;
            case 'error': return <AlertTriangle size={18} />;
            case 'wish': return <Lightbulb size={18} />;
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center pt-24 bg-black/80 animate-fade-in p-4" onClick={onClose}>
            <div
                className="bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 rounded-xl shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full overflow-hidden"
                onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-6 border-b border-gray-800 bg-gray-800/30">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="p-2 bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-400 rounded-lg">
                            <MessageSquare size={24} />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Feedback</h3>
                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Help us improve the app</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-colors">
                        <X size={24} />
                    </button>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-6">

                    {/* Feedback Type Selection */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">What kind of feedback is this?</label>
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                            {(['bug', 'error', 'wish'] as FeedbackType[]).map((t) => (
                                <button
                                    key={t}
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setType(t)}
                                    className={`
                    flex flex-col items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg border transition-all
                    ${type === t
                                            ? 'bg-indigo-600/20 border-indigo-500 text-white shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(99,102,241,0.3)]'
                                            : 'bg-gray-800/50 border-gray-700 text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-800 hover:border-gray-600'}
                  `}
                                >
                                    {getTypeIcon(t)}
                                    <span className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">{t}</span>
                                </button>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Comment Area */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                        <label htmlFor="comment" className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Your Comments</label>
                        <textarea
                            id="comment"
                            required
                            value={comment}
                            onChange={(e) => setComment(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="Describe the issue or your idea..."
                            className="w-full h-32 bg-gray-950 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg p-3 text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-transparent resize-none"
                        />
                    </div>

                    {/* Submit Button */}
                    <button
                        type="submit"
                        className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-3 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors group"
                    >
                        <Send size={18} className="group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
                        Send Feedback
                    </button>

                    <p className="text-center text-xs text-gray-500">
                        This will open your default email client.
                    </p>
                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default FeedbackModal;

--- START OF FILE: components/ShotListResultView.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { ShotListResult, VideoMetadata, ProjectMetadata, Shot } from '../types';
import { parseTimecode } from '../utils/tableUtils';
import { Download, Save } from 'lucide-react';
import ExportModal from './ExportModal';
import { useVerificationState } from '../hooks/useVerificationState';

interface ShotListResultViewProps {
  result: ShotListResult;
  onReset?: () => void;
  onBack: () => void;
  onSave: (data: ShotListResult) => Promise<void>;
  videoPreviewUrl: string | null;
  videoFileName: string;
  videoMetadata?: VideoMetadata | null;
  projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata;
}

// Editable Cell Component
const EditableCell: React.FC<{
  value: string;
  onChange: (val: string) => void;
  isFocused: boolean;
  onFocus: () => void;
  className?: string; // Allow passing classes
  multiline?: boolean; // New prop for multiline support
}> = ({ value, onChange, isFocused, onFocus, className, multiline }) => {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isFocused && inputRef.current) {
      inputRef.current.focus();
    }
  }, [isFocused]);

  if (multiline) {
    return (
      <textarea
        ref={inputRef as React.RefObject<HTMLTextAreaElement>}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        onFocus={onFocus}
        className={`w-full bg-transparent border-none text-gray-300 focus:text-white focus:ring-0 p-0 resize-y min-h-[60px] leading-relaxed ${className || ''}`}
        rows={3}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <input
      ref={inputRef as React.RefObject<HTMLInputElement>}
      value={value}
      onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
      onFocus={onFocus}
      className={`w-full bg-transparent border-none text-gray-300 focus:text-white focus:ring-0 p-0 ${className || ''}`}
    />
  );
};

const ShotListResultView: React.FC<ShotListResultViewProps> = ({
  result,
  onBack,
  onSave,
  videoPreviewUrl,
  videoFileName,
  projectMetadata,
}) => {
  const [showExportModal, setShowExportModal] = useState(false);
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);

  // Verification Hook
  const {
    data: shots,
    isDirty,
    cursor,
    setCursor,
    updateCell,
    navigate,
    save,
    registerFields
  } = useVerificationState<Shot>(result.shots, async (updatedShots) => {
    await onSave({ ...result, shots: updatedShots });
  });

  // Register editable fields
  useEffect(() => {
    registerFields(['nr', 'tcIn', 'tcOut', 'duration', 'description', 'action', 'audio']);
  }, [registerFields]);

  const activeVideoUrl = videoPreviewUrl || result.videoUrl;
  const isVideoAvailable = !!videoPreviewUrl;

  const seekToShot = useCallback((nr: number) => {
    if (!videoRef.current) return;
    const shot = shots.find(s => s.nr === nr);
    if (shot) {
      const time = parseTimecode(shot.tcIn, result.frameRate);
      videoRef.current.currentTime = time;
    }
  }, [shots, result.frameRate]);

  // Sync Video on Cursor Change (Tab Navigation)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (cursor && videoRef.current) {
      const shot = shots[cursor.rowIndex];
      const time = parseTimecode(shot.tcIn, result.frameRate);
      // Only seek if we are far away (prevent micro-stuttering if already there)
      if (Math.abs(videoRef.current.currentTime - time) > 1.0) {
        videoRef.current.currentTime = time;
      }
    }
  }, [cursor, shots, result.frameRate]);

  // Keyboard Shortcuts for Video Control & Navigation
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      // Ignore if typing in an input (except for specific navigation keys if needed, but simple block is safer)
      const isInput = (e.target as HTMLElement).tagName === 'INPUT' || (e.target as HTMLElement).tagName === 'TEXTAREA';

      // J-K-L & Space (Global unless handling text specifically, but we want these available during review)
      if (!isInput) {
        if (e.key === ' ') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (videoRef.current) {
            videoRef.current.paused ? videoRef.current.play() : videoRef.current.pause();
          }
        }
        if (e.key === 'j' || e.key === 'J') {
          if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.currentTime -= 1; // Back 1s
        }
        if (e.key === 'l' || e.key === 'L') {
          if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.currentTime += 1; // Fwd 1s
        }
        if (e.key === 'k' || e.key === 'K') {
          if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.paused ? videoRef.current.play() : videoRef.current.pause();
        }
      }

      // Tab Navigation (Works inside inputs too)
      if (e.key === 'Tab') {
        e.preventDefault();
        navigate(e.shiftKey ? 'prev' : 'next');
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [navigate]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">

      {/* Verification Header */}
      <div className={`rounded-lg p-6 border transition-colors ${isDirty ? 'bg-amber-900/20 border-amber-500/50' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-900/30 to-indigo-900/30 border-purple-500/30'}`}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              Verification Station
              {isDirty && <span className="text-xs bg-amber-500 text-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase">Unsaved Changes</span>}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-gray-400">{shots.length} shots • Tab to Verify • J-K-L Navigation</p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button onClick={save} disabled={!isDirty} className={`px-4 py-2 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 ${isDirty ? 'bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500' : 'bg-gray-700 opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed'}`}>
              <Save size={18} />
              {isDirty ? 'Save Changes' : 'Saved'}
            </button>
            <button onClick={onBack} className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold">Exit</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isVideoAvailable && (
          <div className="mb-6 bg-black rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-700 shadow-xl relative aspect-video max-h-[400px] mx-auto">
            <video
              ref={videoRef}
              src={activeVideoUrl}
              controls
              className="w-full h-full"
            />
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowExportModal(true)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            <Download size={20} />
            Export Options...
          </button>
          <ExportModal
            isOpen={showExportModal}
            onClose={() => setShowExportModal(false)}
            type="shotlist"
            data={{ ...result, shots }} // Pass verified shots
            projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
            videoFileName={videoFileName}
            frameRate={result.frameRate}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Table */}
      <div className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg border border-gray-700 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full border-collapse">
            <thead className="bg-gray-800 border-b border-gray-700">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 w-16">Nr</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 w-32">TC In</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 w-32">TC Out</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 w-24">Dur</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 w-1/3">Description</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 w-1/3">Action</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300">Audio</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700">
              {shots.map((shot, index) => {
                const isRowSelected = cursor?.rowIndex === index;

                return (
                  <tr
                    key={index}
                    className={`${index % 2 === 0 ? 'bg-gray-800/40' : 'bg-gray-800/20'} hover:bg-gray-700/30 transition-colors ${isRowSelected ? 'bg-purple-900/30' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'description' });
                      seekToShot(shot.nr);
                    }}
                  >
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-400 font-mono">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={(shot.nr ?? '').toString()}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'nr', parseInt(v) || shot.nr)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'nr'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'nr' })}
                        className="text-center"
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-400 font-mono whitespace-nowrap">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={shot.tcIn ?? ''}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'tcIn', v)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'tcIn'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'tcIn' })}
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-400 font-mono whitespace-nowrap">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={shot.tcOut ?? ''}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'tcOut', v)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'tcOut'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'tcOut' })}
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-500 font-mono">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={shot.duration ?? ''}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'duration', v)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'duration'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'duration' })}
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-300 align-top">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={shot.description ?? ''}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'description', v)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'description'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'description' })}
                        multiline
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-300 align-top">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={shot.action ?? ''}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'action', v)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'action'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'action' })}
                        multiline
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-400 italic align-top">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={shot.audio ?? ''}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'audio', v)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'audio'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'audio' })}
                        multiline
                      />
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ShotListResultView;
--- END OF FILE: components/ShotListResultView.tsx ---

--- START OF FILE: components/ExportModal.tsx ---
import React from 'react';
import { X, FileText, FileJson, FileSpreadsheet, Film, Clapperboard, MessageSquare, Download } from 'lucide-react';
import { Shot, TranscriptionResult, ProjectMetadata, ShotListResult, GraphicsAnalysisResult, SpeciesSighting } from '../types';
import { generateEDL, generateFCPXML, generateResolveCSV, downloadNLEFile } from '../services/nleExportService';
import { generateSubtitles, downloadSubtitle } from '../services/subtitleService';
import { exportToWord } from '../services/documentExportService';
import { exportToJSON } from '../services/exportService';
import { generateCSV, downloadFile, applyTimecodeOffset } from '../utils/tableUtils';

export type ExportType = 'shotlist' | 'transcription' | 'graphics' | 'fauna' | 'synopsis' | 'bio' | 'asbroadcast';

interface ExportModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    type: ExportType;
    data: any; // ShotListResult | TranscriptionResult | etc.
    projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata;
    videoFileName: string;
    frameRate: number;
}

const ExportModal: React.FC<ExportModalProps> = ({
    isOpen,
    onClose,
    type,
    data,
    projectMetadata,
    videoFileName,
    frameRate
}) => {
    if (!isOpen) return null;

    const exportOptions: { label: string; icon: React.ReactNode; action: () => void; description: string; color: string }[] = [];

    // Common Exports (Word, JSON)
    exportOptions.push({
        label: 'Word Document',
        icon: <FileText size={20} />,
        action: () => exportToWord({ type, data: (type === 'shotlist' ? data.shots : data), projectMetadata, fileName: videoFileName, frameRate }),
        description: 'Formatted report (.docx)',
        color: 'bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700'
    });

    exportOptions.push({
        label: 'JSON Data',
        icon: <FileJson size={20} />,
        action: () => exportToJSON(data, type as any, videoFileName),
        description: 'Raw metadata (.json)',
        color: 'bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-700'
    });

    // Type Specific Exports in Priority Order

    if (type === 'shotlist') {
        const shots = (data as ShotListResult).shots;
        // CSV
        exportOptions.unshift({
            label: 'CSV Spreadsheet',
            icon: <FileSpreadsheet size={20} />,
            action: () => {
                const headers = ['Shot #', 'TC In', 'TC Out', 'Duration', 'Description', 'Action', 'Audio'];
                const rows = shots.map(s => [
                    s.nr,
                    applyTimecodeOffset(s.tcIn, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                    applyTimecodeOffset(s.tcOut, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                    s.duration,
                    s.description,
                    s.action,
                    s.audio
                ]);
                downloadFile(generateCSV(headers, rows), `${videoFileName}_shotlist.csv`, 'text/csv');
            },
            description: 'Excel/Sheets compatible',
            color: 'bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700'
        });

        // Professional NLE Formats
        exportOptions.push({
            label: 'EDL (CMX 3600)',
            icon: <Film size={20} />,
            action: () => downloadNLEFile(generateEDL(shots, videoFileName, projectMetadata, frameRate), videoFileName, 'edl'),
            description: 'Avid / Premiere / Resolve',
            color: 'bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700'
        });

        exportOptions.push({
            label: 'FCP XML (v5)',
            icon: <Clapperboard size={20} />,
            action: () => downloadNLEFile(generateFCPXML(shots, videoFileName, projectMetadata, frameRate), videoFileName, 'fcp_xml'),
            description: 'Final Cut Pro 7 / Premiere',
            color: 'bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700'
        });

        exportOptions.push({
            label: 'Resolve Markers',
            icon: <MessageSquare size={20} />,
            action: () => downloadNLEFile(generateResolveCSV(shots, projectMetadata, frameRate), videoFileName, 'resolve_csv'),
            description: 'DaVinci Resolve (.csv)',
            color: 'bg-pink-600 hover:bg-pink-700'
        });
    }

    if (type === 'transcription') {
        const trans = data as TranscriptionResult;

        // CSV
        exportOptions.unshift({
            label: 'CSV Spreadsheet',
            icon: <FileSpreadsheet size={20} />,
            action: () => {
                const headers = ['Start', 'End', 'Speaker', 'Text'];
                const rows = trans.segments.map(s => [
                    applyTimecodeOffset(s.startTime, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                    applyTimecodeOffset(s.endTime, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                    trans.speakerMap?.[s.speaker] || s.speaker,
                    s.transcript
                ]);
                downloadFile(generateCSV(headers, rows), `${videoFileName}_transcript.csv`, 'text/csv');
            },
            description: 'Excel/Sheets compatible',
            color: 'bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700'
        });

        // Subtitles
        exportOptions.push({
            label: 'SRT Subtitles',
            icon: <MessageSquare size={20} />,
            action: () => downloadSubtitle(generateSubtitles(trans, 'srt', projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate), videoFileName, 'srt'),
            description: 'Standard Subtitles (.srt)',
            color: 'bg-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-700'
        });

        exportOptions.push({
            label: 'VTT Captions',
            icon: <MessageSquare size={20} />,
            action: () => downloadSubtitle(generateSubtitles(trans, 'vtt', projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate), videoFileName, 'vtt'),
            description: 'Web & Broadcast (.vtt)',
            color: 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700'
        });
    }

    if (type === 'fauna') {
        const sightings = data as SpeciesSighting[];
        exportOptions.unshift({
            label: 'CSV Spreadsheet',
            icon: <FileSpreadsheet size={20} />,
            action: () => {
                const headers = ['Common Name', 'Latin Name', 'Behavior', 'IUCN Status', 'Timecode', 'Category'];
                const rows = sightings.map(s => [
                    s.commonName,
                    s.latinName,
                    s.behavior || '',
                    s.iucnStatus || '',
                    applyTimecodeOffset(s.timecode, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                    s.category
                ]);
                downloadFile(generateCSV(headers, rows), `${videoFileName}_fauna.csv`, 'text/csv');
            },
            description: 'Excel/Sheets compatible',
            color: 'bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700'
        });
    }

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center pt-24 bg-black/80 animate-fade-in p-4" onClick={onClose}>
            <div className="bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 rounded-xl shadow-2xl max-w-2xl w-full overflow-hidden" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-6 border-b border-gray-800">
                    <div>
                        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Export Options</h3>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Select a format to download</p>
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-colors">
                        <X size={24} />
                    </button>
                </div>

                <div className="p-6 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    {exportOptions.map((opt, idx) => (
                        <button
                            key={idx}
                            onClick={() => { opt.action(); onClose(); }}
                            className={`${opt.color} p-4 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-4 transition-transform hover:scale-[1.02] text-left group`}
                        >
                            <div className="p-2 bg-black/20 rounded-full group-hover:bg-black/30 transition-colors">
                                {opt.icon}
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <h4 className="font-bold text-white">{opt.label}</h4>
                                <p className="text-xs text-white/70">{opt.description}</p>
                            </div>
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                {projectMetadata.timecodeStart && projectMetadata.timecodeStart !== '00:00:00:00' && (
                    <div className="bg-gray-800/50 p-4 text-center border-t border-gray-800">
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                            Timecode Offset Active: <span className="text-blue-400 font-mono">{projectMetadata.timecodeStart}</span>
                        </p>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default ExportModal;

--- END OF FILE: components/ExportModal.tsx ---

--- START OF FILE: components/ModelRouterSettings.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { modelRouter } from '../services/llm/ModelRouter';
import { DeepSeekProvider } from '../services/llm/DeepSeekProvider';
import { Save, Server, Cpu, Key, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';

export const ModelRouterSettings: React.FC = () => {
    const [deepSeekKey, setDeepSeekKey] = useState('');
    const [activeProvider, setActiveProvider] = useState(modelRouter.getActiveProvider().id);
    const [providers, setProviders] = useState(modelRouter.getAllProviders());

    useEffect(() => {
        const storedKey = localStorage.getItem('DEEPSEEK_API_KEY');
        if (storedKey) setDeepSeekKey(storedKey);
    }, []);

    const handleSaveKey = () => {
        if (deepSeekKey.trim()) {
            localStorage.setItem('DEEPSEEK_API_KEY', deepSeekKey);
            // Dynamically register/update
            modelRouter.registerProvider(new DeepSeekProvider(deepSeekKey));
            setProviders(modelRouter.getAllProviders());
            alert('DeepSeek Key Saved & Provider Registered!');
        }
    };

    const handleProviderChange = (id: string) => {
        modelRouter.setActiveProvider(id);
        setActiveProvider(id);
    };

    return (
        <div className="bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto my-8">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
                <div className="bg-blue-500/20 p-3 rounded-full">
                    <Server className="text-blue-400" size={24} />
                </div>
                <div>
                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">Model Router ("The Switchboard")</h2>
                    <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">Decouple your intelligence. Switch brains on the fly.</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-6">
                {/* Active Provider Selection */}
                <div className="bg-gray-800 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-700">
                    <label className="block text-gray-300 font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Cpu size={18} />
                        Active Intelligence Provider
                    </label>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                        {providers.map(p => (
                            <button
                                key={p.id}
                                onClick={() => handleProviderChange(p.id)}
                                className={`p-4 rounded border text-left transition-all ${activeProvider === p.id
                                        ? 'bg-blue-900/40 border-blue-500 ring-1 ring-blue-500'
                                        : 'bg-gray-700 border-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-650'
                                    }`}
                            >
                                <div className="font-bold text-white">{p.name}</div>
                                <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">{p.description}</div>
                            </button>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* API Key Management */}
                <div className="bg-gray-800 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-700">
                    <h3 className="text-white font-semibold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Key size={18} />
                        Provider Keys (Stored Locally)
                    </h3>

                    <div className="space-y-4">
                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-xs uppercase text-gray-500 font-bold mb-1">DeepSeek API Key</label>
                            <div className="flex gap-2">
                                <input
                                    type="password"
                                    value={deepSeekKey}
                                    onChange={(e) => setDeepSeekKey(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="sk-..."
                                    className="flex-1 bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-white placeholder-gray-600 focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none"
                                />
                                <button
                                    onClick={handleSaveKey}
                                    className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded flex items-center gap-2 font-bold"
                                >
                                    <Save size={18} />
                                    Save
                                </button>
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
                                Using <code className="bg-gray-700 px-1 rounded">deepseek-chat</code> (V3) or <code className="bg-gray-700 px-1 rounded">deepseek-reasoner</code> (R1).
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {activeProvider !== 'gemini' && (
                    <div className="bg-amber-900/30 border border-amber-500/30 rounded p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
                        <AlertTriangle className="text-amber-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={20} />
                        <div>
                            <h4 className="text-amber-400 font-bold text-sm">Visual Fallback Active</h4>
                            <p className="text-amber-200/70 text-xs mt-1">
                                {providers.find(p => p.id === activeProvider)?.name} does not support Video Analysis.
                                Video tasks will automatically fall back to **Google Gemini**.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

--- START OF FILE: components/FileUploadView.tsx ---
import React, { useRef, useState, DragEvent, useEffect } from 'react';
import { getAllCachedUploads, CachedUpload } from '../services/uploadService';
import { FileVideo, ArrowRight, Settings } from 'lucide-react';

interface FileUploadViewProps {
  onFileSelect: (file: File) => void;
  onUriSelect?: (uri: string) => void;
  onOpenSettings?: () => void;
}

const FileUploadView: React.FC<FileUploadViewProps> = ({ onFileSelect, onUriSelect, onOpenSettings }) => {
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const [recentUploads, setRecentUploads] = useState<CachedUpload[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Load cached uploads on mount
    setRecentUploads(getAllCachedUploads());
  }, []);

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) {
      onFileSelect(file);
    }
  };

  const handleDragEnter = (e: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setIsDragging(true);
  };

  const handleDragLeave = (e: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setIsDragging(false);
  };

  const handleDragOver = (e: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
  };

  const handleDrop = (e: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setIsDragging(false);

    const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
    if (file) {
      onFileSelect(file);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className={`min-h-[80vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center relative transition-colors duration-300 ${isDragging ? 'bg-zinc-900' : 'bg-transparent'}`}
      onDragEnter={handleDragEnter}
      onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
      onDragOver={handleDragOver}
      onDrop={handleDrop}
    >
      {/* Settings Access */}
      {onOpenSettings && (
        <button
          onClick={onOpenSettings}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 p-2 text-zinc-500 hover:text-[#ccff00] transition-colors z-50"
          title="Login & Settings"
        >
          <Settings className="w-6 h-6" />
        </button>
      )}
      {/* Main Content */}
      <div className="text-center space-y-2 z-10">
        <h1 className="text-[12rem] leading-none font-serif text-white tracking-tighter mix-blend-difference select-none">
          NEVER<span className="text-[#ccff00]">.</span>
        </h1>

        <div className="flex flex-col space-y-1 py-8">
          <p className="font-mono text-zinc-500 text-xl tracking-wider">Frame-accurate.</p>
          <p className="font-mono text-zinc-500 text-xl tracking-wider">Fully automatic.</p>
          <p className="font-mono text-zinc-500 text-xl tracking-wider">Industry Standard</p>
          <p className="font-mono text-zinc-500 text-xl tracking-wider">Deliverables.</p>
        </div>

        <p className="font-serif italic text-white text-4xl pt-8 pb-12 opacity-90">
          Go take a break.
        </p>

        <button
          onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
          className="group bg-[#ccff00] hover:bg-[#bbe600] text-black font-mono text-sm tracking-widest px-8 py-4 uppercase transition-all duration-300 transform hover:scale-105 flex items-center gap-2 mx-auto"
        >
          Start Uploading
          <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <input
        ref={fileInputRef}
        type="file"
        accept="video/mp4,video/quicktime,video/webm,video/x-msvideo,video/x-matroska"
        onChange={handleFileChange}
        className="hidden"
      />

      {/* Drag Overlay Indicator */}
      {isDragging && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 border-4 border-[#ccff00] bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none z-50">
          <p className="text-[#ccff00] font-mono text-2xl animate-pulse">DROP VIDEO FILE HERE</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Recent Uploads Library - kept minimal at bottom */}
      {recentUploads.length > 0 && onUriSelect && (
        <div className="absolute bottom-8 w-full max-w-xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <div className="text-center text-xs font-mono text-zinc-600 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Recent Sessions</div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
              {recentUploads.slice(0, 3).map((upload) => (
                <button
                  key={`${upload.name}-${upload.timestamp}`}
                  onClick={() => onUriSelect(upload.uri)}
                  className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-400 text-xs hover:border-[#ccff00] hover:text-[#ccff00] transition-colors"
                >
                  <FileVideo size={12} />
                  <span className="truncate max-w-[100px]">{upload.name}</span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default FileUploadView;
