--- START OF FILE: components/BioResultView.tsx ---
import React, { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { Bio, ProjectMetadata } from '../types';
import { downloadFile } from '../utils/tableUtils';
import { exportToWord } from '../services/documentExportService';
import { exportToJSON } from '../services/exportService';
import { ArrowLeft, Download, Save, RefreshCw, Check, Database } from 'lucide-react';

interface BioResultViewProps {
  bios: Bio[];
  videoFileName: string;
  projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata; // New prop
  onReset: () => void;
  onBack: () => void;
  onSave: (data: Bio[]) => Promise<void>;
}

const BioResultView: React.FC<BioResultViewProps> = ({ bios, videoFileName, projectMetadata, onReset, onBack, onSave }) => {
  const [isSaved, setIsSaved] = useState(false);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  const handleDownload = useCallback(() => {
    const fileContent = bios.map(bio => {
      if (bio.isLoading) {
        return `${bio.name.toUpperCase()} \n\nBiography generation in progress...\n\n${'-'.repeat(50)} \n\n`;
      }
      if (bio.error) {
        return `${bio.name.toUpperCase()} \n\nFailed to generate biography: ${bio.error} \n\n${'-'.repeat(50)} \n\n`;
      }

      const bioText = bio.biography.split('\n').filter(p => p.trim() !== '').join('\n\n');
      return `${bio.fullName.toUpperCase()} \n${bio.title} \n\n${bioText} \n\n${'-'.repeat(50)} \n\n`;
    }).join('');

    const baseFilename = videoFileName.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, "") || 'talent';
    downloadFile(fileContent, `${baseFilename} _bios.txt`, 'text/plain');
  }, [bios, videoFileName]);

  const handleExportJSON = () => {
    exportToJSON(bios, 'bio', videoFileName);
  };

  const handleExportWord = () => {
    exportToWord({
      type: 'bio',
      data: bios,
      projectMetadata: projectMetadata,
      fileName: videoFileName
    });
  };

  const handleSaveToDatabase = async () => {
    setIsSaving(true);
    await onSave(bios);
    setIsSaved(true);
    setIsSaving(false);
  };

  const successCount = bios.filter(b => !b.isLoading && !b.error).length;
  const failureCount = bios.filter(b => b.error).length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="text-center pb-4 border-b border-gray-700">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-16 h-16 bg-green-500/20 rounded-full mb-4">
          <svg className="w-10 h-10 text-green-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M5 13l4 4L19 7" />
          </svg>
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-200">Biographies Generated!</h3>
        <p className="text-gray-400 mt-2">
          {successCount} successful • {failureCount > 0 ? `${failureCount} failed` : 'All completed'}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4 max-h-[600px] overflow-y-auto pr-2">
        {bios.map((bio, index) => (
          <div
            key={index}
            className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg p-5 border border-gray-700 hover:border-gray-600 transition-colors"
          >
            {bio.isLoading ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 border-3 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
                <p className="text-gray-400">Generating biography for {bio.name}...</p>
              </div>
            ) : bio.error ? (
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-2">
                  <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-red-400 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                  </svg>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <h4 className="text-lg font-bold text-red-400">{bio.name}</h4>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mt-1">{bio.error}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div>
                <div className="mb-3 pb-3 border-b border-gray-700">
                  <h4 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">{bio.fullName}</h4>
                  <p className="text-sm text-indigo-400 font-medium mt-1">{bio.title}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="text-gray-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line mb-4">
                  {bio.biography}
                </div>

                {bio.sources && bio.sources.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-700/50">
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Verified Sources</p>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      {bio.sources.map((source, i) => (
                        <a
                          key={i}
                          href={source}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
                          className="text-xs text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 bg-blue-500/10 hover:bg-blue-500/20 px-2 py-1 rounded transition-colors truncate max-w-[200px]"
                          title={source}
                        >
                          {new URL(source).hostname.replace('www.', '')}
                        </a>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-center pt-4">
        <button
          onClick={handleDownload}
          className="px-6 py-3 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg transform hover:scale-105 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <Download size={20} />
          Download TXT
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={handleExportWord}
          className="px-6 py-3 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg transform hover:scale-105 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <Download size={20} />
          Export Word
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={handleExportJSON}
          className="px-6 py-3 bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg transform hover:scale-105 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <Database size={20} />
          Export JSON
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={handleSaveToDatabase}
          disabled={isSaved || isSaving}
          className="px-6 py-3 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg transform hover:scale-105 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:bg-gray-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
        >
          {isSaved ? (
            <><Check size={18} />Saved</>
          ) : (
            <><Database size={18} />{isSaving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save to Database'}</>
          )}
        </button>
        <button onClick={onBack} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg">
          Back to Dashboard
        </button>
        <button onClick={onReset} className="px-6 py-3 bg-red-800 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg">
          New Video
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default BioResultView;

--- END OF FILE: components/BioResultView.tsx ---

--- START OF FILE: components/HistoryView.tsx ---

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { dbService } from '../services/db';
import { SavedAnalysis } from '../types';
import { ArrowLeft, Trash2, Eye, FileText, Film, Users, Image, Bird, Mic } from 'lucide-react';

interface HistoryViewProps {
  onBack: () => void;
  onLoadAnalysis: (analysis: SavedAnalysis) => void;
}

const HistoryView: React.FC<HistoryViewProps> = ({ onBack, onLoadAnalysis }) => {
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<SavedAnalysis[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadHistory();
  }, []);

  const loadHistory = async () => {
    try {
      const data = await dbService.getAllAnalyses();
      setHistory(data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Failed to load history", error);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id: number, e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (window.confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this analysis?')) {
      await dbService.deleteAnalysis(id);
      loadHistory();
    }
  };

  const getIconForType = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'synopsis': return <FileText className="text-purple-400" />;
      case 'bio': return <Users className="text-indigo-400" />;
      case 'graphics': return <Image className="text-emerald-400" />;
      case 'fauna': return <Bird className="text-amber-400" />;
      case 'transcribe': return <Mic className="text-pink-400" />;
      case 'shotlist': return <Film className="text-cyan-400" />;
      default: return <FileText className="text-gray-400" />;
    }
  };

  const getLabelForType = (type: string) => {
      switch (type) {
          case 'synopsis': return 'Synopsis';
          case 'bio': return 'Talent Bios';
          case 'graphics': return 'Graphics Log';
          case 'fauna': return 'Fauna ID';
          case 'transcribe': return 'Transcription';
          case 'shotlist': return 'Shot List';
          default: return type;
      }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 border-b border-gray-700 pb-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
                onClick={onBack}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-colors px-3 py-2 hover:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg"
            >
                <ArrowLeft size={20} />
            </button>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-200">Analysis Library</h2>
        </div>
        <div className="text-sm text-gray-400">
          {history.length} items saved
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 border-4 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-4"></div>
            <p className="text-gray-400">Loading your library...</p>
        </div>
      ) : history.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 bg-gray-900/50 rounded-xl border border-gray-700 border-dashed">
          <p className="text-gray-400 text-lg mb-2">No saved analyses found.</p>
          <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm">Analyses you save will appear here for offline access.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          {history.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              onClick={() => onLoadAnalysis(item)}
              className="group bg-gray-800/50 hover:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 hover:border-gray-600 p-4 rounded-xl transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 overflow-hidden">
                <div className="p-3 bg-gray-900 rounded-lg group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                    {getIconForType(item.type)}
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <h4 className="text-white font-medium truncate pr-4">{item.fileName}</h4>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm text-gray-400 mt-1">
                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-gray-700/50 text-xs border border-gray-600/50`}>
                        {getLabelForType(item.type)}
                    </span>
                    <span>•</span>
                    <span>{item.date}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                <button
                    onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onLoadAnalysis(item); }}
                    className="p-2 bg-indigo-600/20 text-indigo-400 hover:bg-indigo-600 hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-colors"
                    title="View Analysis"
                >
                    <Eye size={18} />
                </button>
                <button
                    onClick={(e) => handleDelete(item.id!, e)}
                    className="p-2 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 hover:bg-red-600 hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-colors"
                    title="Delete"
                >
                    <Trash2 size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default HistoryView;

--- END OF FILE: components/HistoryView.tsx ---

--- START OF FILE: components/ModeSelectView.tsx ---

import React from 'react';
import { AnalysisMode } from '../types';
import { History, Settings } from 'lucide-react';

interface JobStatus {
  status: 'idle' | 'configuring' | 'processing' | 'results';
  progressPercentage?: number;
}

interface ModeSelectViewProps {
  videoFile: File;
  videoPreviewUrl: string | null;
  onModeSelect: (mode: AnalysisMode) => void;
  onBack: () => void;
  onViewHistory: () => void; // New prop for history
  onViewSettings?: () => void; // Optional project settings callback
  jobs: Record<AnalysisMode, JobStatus>;
  onDownloadAll?: () => void; // New optional prop for batch export
  isUploading?: boolean;
}

const ModeCard: React.FC<{
  mode: AnalysisMode;
  job: JobStatus;
  onSelect: (mode: AnalysisMode) => void;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  className: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}> = ({ mode, job, onSelect, title, description, icon, className, disabled }) => {
  let statusIndicator: React.ReactNode = null;

  switch (job.status) {
    case 'configuring':
      // No indicator for configuring state
      break;
    case 'processing':
      statusIndicator = (
        <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 flex items-center gap-1 bg-gray-900/80 text-xs text-indigo-300 px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
          <div className="w-3 h-3 border-2 border-indigo-400 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
          {job.progressPercentage?.toFixed(0)}%
        </div>
      );
      break;
    case 'results':
      statusIndicator = (
        <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 flex items-center gap-1 bg-green-500/20 text-xs text-green-300 px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
          <span className="w-2 h-2 bg-green-400 rounded-full"></span>
          Done
        </div>
      );
      break;
  }

  const titleForState =
    job.status === 'results' ? `${title}: View Results` :
      title;

  return (
    <button
      onClick={() => !disabled && onSelect(mode)}
      disabled={disabled}
      className={`group relative bg-gradient-to-br border rounded-xl p-6 text-left transition-all duration-200 transform ${disabled ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed grayscale' : 'hover:scale-105'} ${className}`}
    >
      {statusIndicator}
      <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
        <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-12 h-12 bg-black/20 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center group-hover:bg-black/30 transition-colors">
          {icon}
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1">
          <h4 className="text-lg font-bold text-white mb-2 group-hover:text-purple-300 transition-colors">
            {titleForState}
          </h4>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 leading-relaxed">
            {description}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </button>
  );
};


const ModeSelectView: React.FC<ModeSelectViewProps> = ({
  videoFile,

  onBack,

  onViewSettings,

      title: 'Generate Talent Bios',
      description: 'Automatically identify talent and generate professional biographies for everyone in the video',

    },
    {
      mode: 'graphics',
      title: 'Find Graphics & Lower Thirds',
      description: 'Detect all on-screen graphics, title cards, and lower thirds with precise timecode logs',
      icon: <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-400" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M7 4v16M17 4v16M3 8h4m10 0h4M3 12h18M3 16h4m10 0h4M4 20h16a1 1 0 001-1V5a1 1 0 00-1-1H4a1 1 0 00-1 1v14a1 1 0 001 1z" /></svg>,

    },

      mode: 'fauna',

      mode: 'transcribe',

      mode: 'techspecs',

      className: "from-gray-600/20 to-slate-600/20 hover:from-gray-600/30 hover:to-slate-600/30 border-gray-500/30 hover:border-gray-400"
    }
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 w-full md:w-auto">
          {videoPreviewUrl && <video src={videoPreviewUrl} className="w-32 h-20 object-cover rounded bg-black" muted />}
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <p className="text-gray-300 font-medium truncate">{videoFile.name}</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">{(videoFile.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)} MB</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 justify-center md:justify-end w-full md:w-auto">
          {Object.values(jobs).some(j => j.status === 'results') && (
            <button
              onClick={onDownloadAll}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-blue-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 transition-all shadow-lg hover:shadow-blue-500/20"
            >
              <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"><path d="M21 15v4a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-4" /><polyline points="7 10 12 15 17 10" /><line x1="12" y1="15" x2="12" y2="3" /></svg>
              Download All
            </button>
          )}

          <button
            onClick={onViewSettings}
            className="px-4 py-2 border border-blue-500/50 hover:bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-300 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors"
          >
            <Settings size={18} />
            Settings
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={onViewHistory}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-gray-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors"
          >
            <History size={18} />
            Library
          </button>
          <button onClick={onBack} className="px-4 py-2 border border-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-700 text-gray-300 rounded-lg transition-colors whitespace-nowrap">
            New Upload
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              // Trigger feedback modal via custom event since we don't have direct prop
              // This is a quick fix to avoid prop drilling through App.tsx -> ModeSelectView
              // Ideally we would pass onFeedback open prop
              const event = new CustomEvent('openFeedback');
              window.dispatchEvent(event);
            }}
            className="md:hidden px-4 py-2 border border-blue-500/30 text-blue-400 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-500/10 transition-colors"
          >
            Feedback
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-200 mb-4 text-center">
          {isUploading ? 'Video Uploading... (Analysis Disabled)' : 'Choose Analysis Type'}
        </h3>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          {modes.map(modeInfo => (
            <ModeCard
              key={modeInfo.mode}
              mode={modeInfo.mode}
              job={jobs[modeInfo.mode]}
              onSelect={onModeSelect}
              title={modeInfo.title}
              description={modeInfo.description}
              icon={modeInfo.icon}
              className={modeInfo.className}
              disabled={isUploading}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div >
  );
};

export default ModeSelectView;

--- START OF FILE: components/ErrorPopup.tsx ---
import React from 'react';

interface ErrorPopupProps {
  errorMessage: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const ErrorPopup: React.FC<ErrorPopupProps> = ({ errorMessage, onClose }) => {
  // Handle Escape key press to close the modal
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <div 
      className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/70 flex items-center justify-center z-50 animate-fade-in"
      onClick={onClose}
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="error-title"
    >
      <div 
        className="bg-gray-800 rounded-xl shadow-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 m-4 max-w-md w-full border border-red-500/50 transform animate-slide-up"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
          <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-12 h-12 bg-red-500/20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
            <svg className="w-8 h-8 text-red-400" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-3L13.732 4c-.77-1.333-2.694-1.333-3.464 0L3.34 16c-.77 1.333.192 3 1.732 3z" />
            </svg>
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <h3 id="error-title" className="text-xl font-bold text-red-300 mb-3">
              An Error Occurred
            </h3>
            <p className="text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">
              {errorMessage}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-6 text-right">
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-6 py-2 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 transform hover:scale-105 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-gray-800 focus:ring-red-500"
          >
            Close
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ErrorPopup;

--- START OF FILE: components/TranscriptionResultView.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { TranscriptionResult, VideoMetadata, ProjectMetadata, TranscriptionSegment, GraphicsAnalysisResult } from '../types';
import { copyToClipboard } from '../utils/tableUtils';
import { Check, FileText, Download, Save, Wand2 } from 'lucide-react';
import ExportModal from './ExportModal';
import { useVerificationState } from '../hooks/useVerificationState';
import { parseTimecode } from '../utils/tableUtils';

interface TranscriptionResultViewProps {
  result: TranscriptionResult;
  videoPreviewUrl: string | null;
  videoFileName: string;
  videoMetadata: VideoMetadata | null;
  projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata;
  graphicsResult?: GraphicsAnalysisResult | null; // Added prop
  onReset?: () => void;
  onBack: () => void;
  onSave: (data: TranscriptionResult) => Promise<void>;
  onUpdateSpeaker?: (oldName: string, newName: string) => void;
  onUpdateSegment?: (index: number, updates: Partial<TranscriptionSegment>) => void;
}

// Editable Cell Component (Duplicated for isolation)
const EditableCell: React.FC<{
  value: string;
  onChange: (val: string) => void;
  isFocused: boolean;
  onFocus: () => void;
  className?: string;
  multiline?: boolean;
}> = ({ value, onChange, isFocused, onFocus, className, multiline }) => {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isFocused && inputRef.current) {
      inputRef.current.focus();
    }
  }, [isFocused]);

  if (multiline) {
    return (
      <textarea
        ref={inputRef as React.RefObject<HTMLTextAreaElement>}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        onFocus={onFocus}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className={`w-full bg-transparent border-none text-gray-300 focus:text-white focus:ring-0 p-0 resize-y min-h-[60px] leading-relaxed ${className || ''}`}
        rows={3}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <input
      ref={inputRef as React.RefObject<HTMLInputElement>}
      value={value}
      onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
      onFocus={onFocus}
      onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      className={`w-full bg-transparent border-none text-gray-300 focus:text-white focus:ring-0 p-0 cursor-pointer hover:bg-white/5 rounded px-2 -ml-2 transition-colors ${className || ''}`}
    />
  );
};

const TranscriptionResultView: React.FC<TranscriptionResultViewProps> = ({
  result,
  videoPreviewUrl,
  videoFileName,
  videoMetadata,
  projectMetadata,
  graphicsResult,
  onBack,
  onSave,
}) => {
  const [isCopied, setIsCopied] = useState(false);
  const [showExportModal, setShowExportModal] = useState(false);
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);

  // Verification Hook
  const {
    data: segments,
    isDirty,
    cursor,
    setCursor,
    updateCell,
    navigate,
    save,
    registerFields
  } = useVerificationState<TranscriptionSegment>(result.segments, async (updatedSegments) => {
    await onSave({ ...result, segments: updatedSegments });
  });

  // Register fields
  useEffect(() => {
    registerFields(['startTime', 'endTime', 'speaker', 'transcript']);
  }, [registerFields]);

  const activeVideoUrl = videoPreviewUrl || undefined;
  const isVideoAvailable = !!videoPreviewUrl;

  const seekToSegment = useCallback((index: number) => {
    if (!videoRef.current || !segments[index]) return;
    const time = parseTimecode(segments[index].startTime, videoMetadata?.frameRate || 25);
    // Avoid micro-seeks if already close
    if (Math.abs(videoRef.current.currentTime - time) > 0.5) {
      videoRef.current.currentTime = time;
    }
  }, [segments, videoMetadata]);

  // Sync Video on Cursor (Tab)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (cursor && videoRef.current) {
      const time = parseTimecode(segments[cursor.rowIndex].startTime, videoMetadata?.frameRate || 25);
      if (Math.abs(videoRef.current.currentTime - time) > 0.5) {
        videoRef.current.currentTime = time;
      }
    }
  }, [cursor, segments, videoMetadata]);

  // Keyboard Shortcuts
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const isInput = (e.target as HTMLElement).tagName === 'INPUT' || (e.target as HTMLElement).tagName === 'TEXTAREA';

      if (!isInput) {
        if (e.key === ' ') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.paused ? videoRef.current.play() : videoRef.current.pause();
        }
        if (e.key === 'j' || e.key === 'J') {
          if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.currentTime -= 2;
        }
        if (e.key === 'l' || e.key === 'L') {
          if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.currentTime += 2;
        }
        if (e.key === 'k' || e.key === 'K') {
          if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.paused ? videoRef.current.play() : videoRef.current.pause();
        }
      }

      if (e.key === 'Tab') {
        e.preventDefault();
        navigate(e.shiftKey ? 'prev' : 'next');
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [navigate]);

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    const text = segments
      .map(seg => {
        // Use the edited speaker directly
        const status = seg.isOnScreen === false ? ' [V.O.]' : '';
        return `${seg.startTime} - ${seg.endTime}\n${seg.speaker}${status}: ${seg.transcript}`;
      })
      .join('\n\n');

    if (await copyToClipboard(text)) {
      setIsCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setIsCopied(false), 2000);
    }
  };

  const handleAutoAssign = () => {
    if (!graphicsResult?.lowerThirds || graphicsResult.lowerThirds.length === 0) {
      alert("No lower thirst data found. Please run Graphics Analysis first.");
      return;
    }

    const frameRate = videoMetadata?.frameRate || 25;
    let updatesCount = 0;

    // We can't batch update cleanly with the current hook, so we'll do a best-effort approach
    // This loops and updates cells one by one. React usually batches these state updates in event handlers.
    segments.forEach((seg, index) => {
      if (seg.speaker.startsWith("SPEAKER_")) {
        const segStart = parseTimecode(seg.startTime, frameRate);
        const segEnd = parseTimecode(seg.endTime, frameRate);

        // Find matching lower third
        const match = graphicsResult.lowerThirds.find(lt => {
          const ltStart = parseTimecode(lt.timecodeIn, frameRate);
          const ltEnd = parseTimecode(lt.timecodeOut, frameRate);
          // Check for overlap
          return (ltStart < segEnd && ltEnd > segStart);
        });

        if (match && match.text) {
          updateCell(index, 'speaker', match.text);
          updatesCount++;
        }
      }
    });

    if (updatesCount > 0) {
      alert(`Auto-assigned ${updatesCount} speakers based on lower thirds.`);
    } else {
      alert("No matching lower thirds found intersecting with unknown speakers.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">

      {/* Verification Header */}
      <div className={`rounded-lg p-6 border transition-colors ${isDirty ? 'bg-amber-900/20 border-amber-500/50' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-900/30 to-rose-900/30 border-pink-500/30'}`}>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between mb-4 gap-4">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              Transcription Verification
              {isDirty && <span className="text-xs bg-amber-500 text-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase">Unsaved Changes</span>}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-gray-400">{segments.length} segments • Tab to Verify • J-K-L Navigation</p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button onClick={save} disabled={!isDirty} className={`px-4 py-2 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 ${isDirty ? 'bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500' : 'bg-gray-700 opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed'}`}>
              <Save size={18} />
              {isDirty ? 'Save Changes' : 'Saved'}
            </button>
            <button onClick={onBack} className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold">Exit</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isVideoAvailable && (
          <div className="mb-6 bg-black rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-700 shadow-xl relative aspect-video max-h-[400px] mx-auto">
            <video
              ref={videoRef}
              src={activeVideoUrl}
              controls
              className="w-full h-full"
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Buttons Grid - Flex Wrap for Responsive Layout */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <button onClick={handleCopy} className="flex-1 min-w-[140px] px-4 py-2 bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
            {isCopied ? <><Check size={18} />Copied!</> : <><FileText size={18} />Copy Text</>}
          </button>

          {graphicsResult && (
            <button onClick={handleAutoAssign} className="flex-1 min-w-[200px] px-4 py-2 bg-pink-600 hover:bg-pink-700 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
              <Wand2 size={18} />
              Auto-Assign Names
            </button>
          )}

          <button
            onClick={() => setShowExportModal(true)}
            className="flex-1 min-w-[140px] px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            <Download size={20} />
            Export Options...
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <ExportModal
        isOpen={showExportModal}
        onClose={() => setShowExportModal(false)}
        type="transcription"
        data={{ ...result, segments }}
        projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
        videoFileName={videoFileName}
        frameRate={videoMetadata?.frameRate || 25}
      />

      <div className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg border border-gray-700 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full border-collapse">
            <thead className="bg-gray-800 border-b border-gray-700">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 w-32">Start</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 w-32">End</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 w-48">Speaker</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 w-24">Status</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300">Transcript</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700">
              {segments.map((segment, index) => {
                const isRowSelected = cursor?.rowIndex === index;
                return (
                  <tr
                    key={index}
                    className={`${index % 2 === 0 ? 'bg-gray-800/40' : 'bg-gray-800/20'} hover:bg-gray-700/30 transition-colors ${isRowSelected ? 'bg-pink-900/30' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'transcript' });
                      seekToSegment(index);
                    }}
                  >
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-400 font-mono whitespace-nowrap">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={segment.startTime ?? ''}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'startTime', v)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'startTime'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'startTime' })}
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-400 font-mono whitespace-nowrap">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={segment.endTime ?? ''}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'endTime', v)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'endTime'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'endTime' })}
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={segment.speaker ?? ''}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'speaker', v)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'speaker'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'speaker' })}
                        className="font-semibold text-pink-300"
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <button
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          updateCell(index, 'isOnScreen', !segment.isOnScreen);
                        }}
                        className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-bold leading-none transition-colors border ${segment.isOnScreen !== false
                          ? 'bg-green-900/40 text-green-300 border-green-700 hover:bg-green-900/60'
                          : 'bg-amber-900/40 text-amber-300 border-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-900/60'
                          }`}
                      >
                        {segment.isOnScreen !== false ? 'ON SCREEN' : 'V.O.'}
                      </button>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-300 align-top">
                      <EditableCell
                        value={segment.transcript ?? ''}
                        onChange={(v) => updateCell(index, 'transcript', v)}
                        isFocused={cursor?.rowIndex === index && cursor?.fieldId === 'transcript'}
                        onFocus={() => setCursor({ rowIndex: index, fieldId: 'transcript' })}
                        multiline
                      />
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default TranscriptionResultView;

--- START OF FILE: components/TemplateConverterInputView.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { Upload, FileText, ArrowRight, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';

interface TemplateConverterInputViewProps {
    onConvert: (sourceFile: File, targetFile: File) => void;
    onBack: () => void;
}

const TemplateConverterInputView: React.FC<TemplateConverterInputViewProps> = ({ onConvert, onBack }) => {
    const [sourceFile, setSourceFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
    const [targetFile, setTargetFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
    const sourceInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const targetInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

    const handleSourceChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
            setSourceFile(e.target.files[0]);
        }
    };

    const handleTargetChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
            setTargetFile(e.target.files[0]);
        }
    };

    const handleStart = () => {
        if (sourceFile && targetFile) {
            onConvert(sourceFile, targetFile);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-8 animate-fadeIn">
            <div className="text-center mb-8">
                <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold bg-clip-text text-transparent bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-400 to-amber-600 mb-2">
                    Template Converter
                </h2>
                <p className="text-gray-400">
                    Upload your content source and a target template. AI will migrate the content to match the new layout.
                </p>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-8 items-center">
                {/* Source File Card */}
                <div className="bg-gray-800/50 p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-700 hover:border-orange-500/50 transition-colors group">
                    <div className="text-center">
                        <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-orange-900/30 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 group-hover:bg-orange-900/50 transition-colors">
                            <FileText className="text-orange-400" size={24} />
                        </div>
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2">1. Source Content</h3>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">The document containing the text you want to use.</p>

                        <input
                            type="file"
                            ref={sourceInputRef}
                            onChange={handleSourceChange}
                            className="hidden"
                            accept=".pdf,.docx,.txt,.md"
                        />

                        {!sourceFile ? (
                            <button
                                onClick={() => sourceInputRef.current?.click()}
                                className="w-full py-4 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-600 rounded-lg hover:border-orange-500 hover:bg-gray-700/30 transition-all text-gray-400 font-medium"
                            >
                                <Upload className="mx-auto mb-2" size={20} />
                                Click to Upload Source
                            </button>
                        ) : (
                            <div className="bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden">
                                    <div className="min-w-8 min-h-8 bg-orange-500/20 rounded flex items-center justify-center">
                                        <FileText size={16} className="text-orange-400" />
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="truncate">
                                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-white truncate">{sourceFile.name}</p>
                                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{(sourceFile.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => setSourceFile(null)}
                                    className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-600 rounded text-gray-400 hover:text-white"
                                >
                                    <RefreshCw size={14} />
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Arrow for Desktop */}
                <div className="hidden md:flex justify-center">
                    <ArrowRight className="text-gray-600" size={32} />
                </div>

                {/* Target File Card */}
                <div className="bg-gray-800/50 p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-700 hover:border-amber-500/50 transition-colors group">
                    <div className="text-center">
                        <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-amber-900/30 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 group-hover:bg-amber-900/50 transition-colors">
                            <Upload className="text-amber-400" size={24} />
                        </div>
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2">2. Target Template</h3>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">The document with the layout/style you want to use.</p>

                        <input
                            type="file"
                            ref={targetInputRef}
                            onChange={handleTargetChange}
                            className="hidden"
                            accept=".pdf,.docx,.txt,.md"
                        />

                        {!targetFile ? (
                            <button
                                onClick={() => targetInputRef.current?.click()}
                                className="w-full py-4 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-600 rounded-lg hover:border-amber-500 hover:bg-gray-700/30 transition-all text-gray-400 font-medium"
                            >
                                <Upload className="mx-auto mb-2" size={20} />
                                Click to Upload Template
                            </button>
                        ) : (
                            <div className="bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden">
                                    <div className="min-w-8 min-h-8 bg-amber-500/20 rounded flex items-center justify-center">
                                        <FileText size={16} className="text-amber-400" />
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="truncate">
                                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-white truncate">{targetFile.name}</p>
                                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{(targetFile.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => setTargetFile(null)}
                                    className="p-1 hover:bg-gray-600 rounded text-gray-400 hover:text-white"
                                >
                                    <RefreshCw size={14} />
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-4 pt-8">
                <button
                    onClick={onBack}
                    className="px-6 py-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-600 text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-800 transition-colors"
                >
                    Back
                </button>
                <button
                    onClick={handleStart}
                    disabled={!sourceFile || !targetFile}
                    className={`
            flex-1 px-6 py-3 rounded-lg font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all
            ${sourceFile && targetFile
                            ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-amber-600 hover:from-orange-400 hover:to-amber-500 shadow-lg shadow-orange-900/30'
                            : 'bg-gray-700 text-gray-500 cursor-not-allowed'}
          `}
                >
                    <RefreshCw className={sourceFile && targetFile ? "animate-pulse" : ""} size={20} />
                    Convert Format
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default TemplateConverterInputView;

--- START OF FILE: components/SceneDetectorDebugView.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { ffmpegService, VideoMetadataResult } from '../services/ffmpegService';
import { Layout, Play, Clock, Zap, Image as ImageIcon, Film, FileText } from 'lucide-react';
import { secondsToTimecode } from '../utils/tableUtils';

const SceneDetectorDebugView: React.FC = () => {
    const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
    const [scenes, setScenes] = useState<number[]>([]);
    const [keyframes, setKeyframes] = useState<Blob[]>([]);
    const [metadata, setMetadata] = useState<VideoMetadataResult | null>(null);
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'scenes' | 'keyframes' | 'metadata'>('scenes');
    const [logs, setLogs] = useState<string[]>([]);
    const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);
    const [threshold, setThreshold] = useState(0.4);
    const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);

    const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
            setFile(e.target.files[0]);
            setScenes([]);
            setKeyframes([]);
            setMetadata(null);
            setLogs([]);
        }
    };

    const runDetection = async () => {
        if (!file) return;

        setIsProcessing(true);
        setLogs(prev => [...prev, 'Starting Scene Detection...']);

        try {
            const result = await ffmpegService.detectScenes(file, threshold, (msg) => {
                setLogs(prev => [...prev, msg]);
            });
            setScenes(result.scenes);
            setLogs(prev => [...prev, `Detection complete. Found ${result.scenes.length} scenes.`]);
        } catch (error) {
            setLogs(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${error}`]);
        } finally {
            setIsProcessing(false);
        }
    };

    const runKeyframeExtraction = async () => {
        if (!file) return;

        setIsProcessing(true);
        setLogs(prev => [...prev, 'Starting I-Frame Extraction...']);

        try {
            const result = await ffmpegService.extractKeyframes(file, (msg) => {
                setLogs(prev => [...prev, msg]);
            });
            setKeyframes(result.blobs);
            setLogs(prev => [...prev, `Extraction complete. Found ${result.blobs.length} keyframes.`]);
        } catch (error) {
            setLogs(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${error}`]);
        } finally {
            setIsProcessing(false);
        }
    };

    const runMetadataExtraction = async () => {
        if (!file) return;

        setIsProcessing(true);
        setLogs(prev => [...prev, 'Extracting Metadata...']);

        try {
            const result = await ffmpegService.getMetadata(file, (msg) => {
                setLogs(prev => [...prev, msg]);
            });
            setMetadata(result);
            setLogs(prev => [...prev, 'Metadata extraction complete.']);
        } catch (error) {
            setLogs(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${error}`]);
        } finally {
            setIsProcessing(false);
        }
    };

    const seekTo = (time: number) => {
        if (videoRef.current) {
            videoRef.current.currentTime = time;
            videoRef.current.play();
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-black text-white p-6 overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="mb-8">
                <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-green-400 to-blue-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Zap className="text-green-400" />
                    WASM Scene Detector
                </h1>
                <p className="text-gray-400 mt-2">
                    "The Laws of Physics" - Client-side deterministic scene detection.
                </p>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8">

                {/* LEFT COLUMN: Controls & Video */}
                <div className="space-y-6">
                    <div className="bg-gray-900 rounded-lg p-6 border border-gray-800">
                        <input
                            type="file"
                            onChange={handleFileChange}
                            accept="video/*"
                            className="block w-full text-sm text-gray-400 file:mr-4 file:py-2 file:px-4 file:rounded-full file:border-0 file:text-sm file:font-semibold file:bg-blue-600 file:text-white hover:file:bg-blue-700"
                        />
                        {file && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-500">{(file.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)} MB</p>}
                    </div>

                    <div className="bg-gray-900 rounded-lg p-6 border border-gray-800">
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 mb-2">Threshold (0.0 - 1.0)</label>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                            <input
                                type="range"
                                min="0"
                                max="1"
                                step="0.05"
                                value={threshold}
                                onChange={(e) => setThreshold(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                                className="w-full h-2 bg-gray-700 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer"
                            />
                            <span className="font-mono text-blue-400">{threshold}</span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">Lower = More Sensitive. Higher = Only hard cuts.</p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                        <button
                            onClick={() => { setActiveTab('scenes'); runDetection(); }}
                            disabled={!file || isProcessing}
                            className={`flex-1 py-4 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg shadow-lg transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${isProcessing ? 'bg-gray-700 cursor-not-allowed' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-green-500 to-emerald-600 hover:from-green-600 hover:to-emerald-700'}`}
                        >
                            <Film size={20} />
                            Detect Scenes
                        </button>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => { setActiveTab('keyframes'); runKeyframeExtraction(); }}
                            disabled={!file || isProcessing}
                            className={`flex-1 py-4 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg shadow-lg transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${isProcessing ? 'bg-gray-700 cursor-not-allowed' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-500 to-indigo-600 hover:from-purple-600 hover:to-indigo-700'}`}
                        >
                            <ImageIcon size={20} />
                            Extract I-Frames
                        </button>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => { setActiveTab('metadata'); runMetadataExtraction(); }}
                            disabled={!file || isProcessing}
                            className={`flex-1 py-4 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg shadow-lg transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${isProcessing ? 'bg-gray-700 cursor-not-allowed' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-500 to-cyan-600 hover:from-blue-600 hover:to-cyan-700'}`}
                        >
                            <FileText size={20} />
                            Get Metadata
                        </button>
                    </div>

                    {file && (
                        <div className="aspect-video bg-black rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-800 shadow-2xl relative">
                            <video
                                ref={videoRef}
                                src={URL.createObjectURL(file)}
                                controls
                                className="w-full h-full"
                            />
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>

                {/* RIGHT COLUMN: Results & Logs */}
                <div className="space-y-6">

                    {/* Logs */}
                    <div className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg p-4 h-48 overflow-y-auto font-mono text-xs border border-gray-800">
                        <h3 className="text-gray-500 font-bold mb-2 uppercase tracking-wider">System Logs (WebWorker)</h3>
                        {logs.map((log, i) => (
                            <div key={i} className="text-gray-400 border-l-2 border-transparent hover:border-blue-500 pl-2">
                                {log}
                            </div>
                        ))}
                        {logs.length === 0 && <span className="text-gray-600 italic">Ready...</span>}
                    </div>

                    {/* Results */}
                    <div className="bg-gray-900 rounded-lg p-0 overflow-hidden border border-gray-800 flex-1 min-h-[400px] flex flex-col">
                        <div className="bg-gray-800 p-4 border-b border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center">
                            <h3 className="font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                                {activeTab === 'scenes' && <Layout size={18} className="text-green-400" />}
                                {activeTab === 'keyframes' && <ImageIcon size={18} className="text-purple-400" />}
                                {activeTab === 'metadata' && <FileText size={18} className="text-cyan-400" />}

                                {activeTab === 'scenes' && 'Detected Scenes'}
                                {activeTab === 'keyframes' && 'Extracted Keyframes'}
                                {activeTab === 'metadata' && 'File Metadata'}
                            </h3>
                            <span className="bg-blue-900/50 text-blue-300 px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-mono">
                                {activeTab === 'scenes' && `${scenes.length} Cuts`}
                                {activeTab === 'keyframes' && `${keyframes.length} Frames`}
                                {activeTab === 'metadata' && (metadata ? 'Ready' : 'No Data')}
                            </span>
                        </div>

                        <div className="overflow-y-auto max-h-[500px] p-4">
                            {activeTab === 'scenes' && (
                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 divide-y divide-gray-800">
                                    {scenes.map((time, i) => (
                                        <button
                                            key={i}
                                            onClick={() => seekTo(time)}
                                            className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 hover:bg-gray-800 transition-colors group text-left w-full rounded"
                                        >
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                                                <span className="text-gray-500 font-mono w-8 text-right text-xs">#{i + 1}</span>
                                                <div className="flex flex-col">
                                                    <span className="text-white font-mono font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                                                        <Clock size={12} className="text-gray-500" />
                                                        {secondsToTimecode(time)}
                                                    </span>
                                                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-mono">
                                                        {time.toFixed(3)}s
                                                    </span>
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                            <Play size={16} className="text-gray-600 group-hover:text-blue-400 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-all" />
                                        </button>
                                    ))}
                                    {scenes.length === 0 && !isProcessing && (
                                        <div className="p-8 text-center text-gray-500">
                                            No scenes detected yet.
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            )}

                            {activeTab === 'keyframes' && (
                                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                                    {keyframes.map((blob, i) => (
                                        <div key={i} className="aspect-video bg-black rounded overflow-hidden border border-gray-800 relative group">
                                            <img
                                                src={URL.createObjectURL(blob)}
                                                alt={`Frame ${i}`}
                                                className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                                            />
                                            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/50 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity flex items-center justify-center">
                                                <span className="text-white font-mono text-xs">#{i + 1}</span>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    ))}
                                    {keyframes.length === 0 && !isProcessing && (
                                        <div className="col-span-3 p-8 text-center text-gray-500">
                                            No keyframes extracted yet.
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            )}

                            {activeTab === 'metadata' && (
                                <div className="space-y-4 font-mono text-sm">
                                    {metadata ? (
                                        <>
                                            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                                                <div className="bg-gray-800 p-4 rounded border border-gray-700">
                                                    <div className="text-gray-500 text-xs uppercase mb-1">Container</div>
                                                    <div className="text-white font-bold">{metadata.container}</div>
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="bg-gray-800 p-4 rounded border border-gray-700">
                                                    <div className="text-gray-500 text-xs uppercase mb-1">Duration</div>
                                                    <div className="text-green-400 font-bold">{metadata.duration}</div>
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="bg-gray-800 p-4 rounded border border-gray-700">
                                                    <div className="text-gray-500 text-xs uppercase mb-1">Bitrate</div>
                                                    <div className="text-blue-400 font-bold">{metadata.bitrate}</div>
                                                </div>
                                            </div>

                                            <div className="bg-gray-800 p-4 rounded border border-gray-700">
                                                <div className="text-gray-500 text-xs uppercase mb-2">Streams</div>
                                                <div className="space-y-2">
                                                    {metadata.streams.map((s, i) => (
                                                        <div key={i} className="text-xs text-gray-300 border-l-2 border-purple-500 pl-2">
                                                            {s}
                                                        </div>
                                                    ))}
                                                </div>
                                            </div>

                                            <div className="bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 rounded p-4">
                                                <div className="text-gray-500 text-xs uppercase mb-2">Raw FFmpeg Log</div>
                                                <pre className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 whitespace-pre-wrap overflow-x-auto">
                                                    {metadata.rawLogs}
                                                </pre>
                                            </div>
                                        </>
                                    ) : (
                                        <div className="p-8 text-center text-gray-500">
                                            No metadata extracted yet.
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default SceneDetectorDebugView;

--- START OF FILE: components/TemplateConverterResultView.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import { Download, ArrowLeft, Check, Copy } from 'lucide-react';
import { TemplateConverterResult } from '../types';

interface TemplateConverterResultViewProps {
    result: TemplateConverterResult;
    onReset: () => void;
    onBack: () => void;
}

const TemplateConverterResultView: React.FC<TemplateConverterResultViewProps> = ({ result, onReset, onBack }) => {
    const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

    const handleCopy = () => {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(result.convertedContent);
        setCopied(true);
        setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    };

    const handleDownload = () => {
        const blob = new Blob([result.convertedContent], { type: 'text/markdown' });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = url;
        link.download = `${result.originalFileName.split('.')[0]}_converted.md`;
        document.body.appendChild(link);
        link.click();
        document.body.removeChild(link);
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    };

    return (
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-6 animate-fadeIn">
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
                <div>
                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-3">
                        <span className="w-2 h-8 bg-green-500 rounded-full"></span>
                        Conversion Complete
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-gray-400 mt-1 pl-5">
                        Content from <span className="text-gray-300 font-mono text-sm">{result.originalFileName}</span> adapted to <span className="text-gray-300 font-mono text-sm">{result.targetTemplateName}</span>
                    </p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-3">
                    <button
                        onClick={onBack}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-700 text-gray-300 border border-gray-700 transition-colors"
                    >
                        <ArrowLeft size={18} />
                        Back
                    </button>
                    <button
                        onClick={onReset}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-700 text-gray-300 border border-gray-700 transition-colors"
                    >
                        New Conversion
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Toolbar */}
            <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl border-b border-gray-700 p-4 flex justify-between items-center">
                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Preview</span>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button
                        onClick={handleCopy}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-sm transition-colors text-white"
                    >
                        {copied ? <Check size={14} className="text-green-400" /> : <Copy size={14} />}
                        {copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy Text'}
                    </button>
                    <button
                        onClick={handleDownload}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-sm transition-colors text-white shadow-lg shadow-indigo-900/20"
                    >
                        <Download size={14} />
                        Download Markdown
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Content Preview */}
            <div className="bg-gray-900 rounded-b-xl border border-t-0 border-gray-700 p-8 min-h-[500px] prose prose-invert max-w-none">
                <ReactMarkdown>{result.convertedContent}</ReactMarkdown>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default TemplateConverterResultView;

--- START OF FILE: components/SemanticSearchView.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { indexingService } from '../services/indexingService';
import { embeddingService, SearchResult } from '../services/embeddingService';
import { Search, Database, Play, Loader } from 'lucide-react';
import { secondsToTimecode } from '../utils/tableUtils';

interface SemanticSearchViewProps {
    videoFile: File | null;
    onSeek: (time: number) => void;
    isIndexing: boolean;
    indexProgress: number;
    progressMsg: string;
    isIndexed: boolean;
    onStartIndexing: () => void;
}

export const SemanticSearchView: React.FC<SemanticSearchViewProps> = ({
    videoFile,
    onSeek,
    isIndexing,
    indexProgress,
    progressMsg,
    isIndexed,
    onStartIndexing
}) => {
    const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
    const [results, setResults] = useState<SearchResult[]>([]);
    const [isSearching, setIsSearching] = useState(false);
    const [playingFile, setPlayingFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
    const [seekTime, setSeekTime] = useState(0);

    const handleSeek = (time: number) => {
        // If parent `onSeek` is provided it might be used, but here we want local playback
        setPlayingFile(videoFile);
        setSeekTime(time);
        onSeek(time); // Call parent just in case
    };

    const closePlayer = () => {
        setPlayingFile(null);
    };

    // Removed local handleIndex - usage delegated to parent

    const handleSearch = async (e?: React.FormEvent) => {
        e?.preventDefault();
        if (!query.trim()) return;

        setIsSearching(true);
        try {
            const hits = await embeddingService.search(query, 6);
            setResults(hits);
        } catch (e) {
            console.error(e);
        } finally {
            setIsSearching(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-black text-white p-6 overflow-y-auto w-full max-w-4xl mx-auto">
            <div className="mb-8 text-center">
                <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 to-rose-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
                    <Search className="text-pink-500" />
                    Semantic Video Search
                </h1>
                <p className="text-gray-400 mt-2">
                    "Chat with your Video" - Find moments using natural language.
                </p>
            </div>

            {!isIndexed ? (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-12 bg-gray-900 rounded-lg border border-gray-800 border-dashed">
                    <Database size={48} className="text-gray-600 mb-4" />
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-2">Index Required</h3>
                    <p className="text-gray-400 mb-6 text-center max-w-md">
                        To enable search, we need to analyze the video structure (`WASM`) and describe every scene (`Gemini Flash`).
                        <br /><span className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2 block">Note: This happens locally + via API. It may take 30-60s.</span>
                    </p>

                    {isIndexing ? (
                        <div className="w-full max-w-md">
                            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-pink-400 mb-1 font-mono">
                                <span>{progressMsg}</span>
                                <span>{Math.round(indexProgress)}%</span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="w-full bg-gray-800 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden">
                                <div
                                    className="bg-pink-500 h-full transition-all duration-300"
                                    style={{ width: `${indexProgress}%` }}
                                />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <button
                            onClick={onStartIndexing}
                            disabled={!videoFile}
                            className="bg-pink-600 hover:bg-pink-700 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-full font-bold transition-all shadow-lg hover:shadow-pink-900/20 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                        >
                            {videoFile ? 'Start Indexing' : 'Upload Video First'}
                        </button>
                    )}
                </div>
            ) : (
                <div className="space-y-8">
                    {/* Search Bar */}
                    <form onSubmit={handleSearch} className="relative">
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={query}
                            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="Describe a moment (e.g. 'Red car turning', 'Two people arguing')..."
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 rounded-full px-6 py-4 text-lg focus:border-pink-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-pink-500 transition-all placeholder-gray-600"
                        />
                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            className="absolute right-2 top-2 bottom-2 bg-pink-600 hover:bg-pink-700 text-white px-6 rounded-full font-bold transition-colors"
                            disabled={isSearching}
                        >
                            {isSearching ? <Loader className="animate-spin" /> : 'Search'}
                        </button>
                    </form>

                    {/* Results */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                        {results.map((hit) => (
                            <div
                                key={hit.id}
                                className="bg-gray-900 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-800 hover:border-pink-500 transition-all group"
                            >
                                <div className="aspect-video bg-black relative">
                                    {hit.metadata.thumbnail && (
                                        <img
                                            src={hit.metadata.thumbnail}
                                            alt="Scene"
                                            className="w-full h-full object-cover opacity-80 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"
                                        />
                                    )}
                                    <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-black/80 px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-mono text-pink-400 border border-pink-900">
                                        {Math.round(hit.similarity * 100)}% Match
                                    </div>

                                    <button
                                        onClick={() => handleSeek(hit.metadata.timestamp)}
                                        className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity bg-black/40"
                                    >
                                        <div className="bg-pink-600 rounded-full p-3 shadow-xl transform scale-75 group-hover:scale-100 transition-transform">
                                            <Play fill="white" className="ml-1" />
                                        </div>
                                    </button>
                                </div>

                                <div className="p-4">
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                                        <span className="text-gray-500 text-xs font-mono">
                                            ID: {hit.id}
                                        </span>
                                        <span className="text-gray-300 font-bold font-mono text-sm">
                                            {secondsToTimecode(hit.metadata.timestamp)}
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm line-clamp-3 group-hover:text-gray-200 transition-colors">
                                        {hit.text}
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))}

                        {results.length === 0 && query && !isSearching && (
                            <div className="col-span-full text-center py-12 text-gray-500">
                                No matches found. Try a different description.
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Simple Player Overlay */}
            {playingFile && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-black/90 flex items-center justify-center p-8 backdrop-blur-sm">
                    <div className="relative w-full max-w-5xl bg-black rounded-lg border border-gray-800 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
                        <button
                            onClick={closePlayer}
                            className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-10 bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/80 text-white p-2 rounded-full transition-colors"
                        >
                            ✕
                        </button>
                        <video
                            src={URL.createObjectURL(playingFile)}
                            className="w-full h-auto max-h-[80vh]"
                            controls
                            autoPlay
                            ref={(v) => {
                                if (v && seekTime > 0) {
                                    // Avoid infinite seek loop
                                    if (Math.abs(v.currentTime - seekTime) > 0.5) {
                                        v.currentTime = seekTime;
                                    }
                                }
                            }}
                        />
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

--- END OF FILE: components/SemanticSearchView.tsx ---
