import React, { useState } from 'react';

interface ShotListInputViewProps {
  onStartAnalysis: (language: string, frameRate: number) => void;
  onBack: () => void;
}

const ShotListInputView: React.FC<ShotListInputViewProps> = ({
  onStartAnalysis,
  onBack,
}) => {
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState('English');
  const [frameRate, setFrameRate] = useState(25);

  const handleStart = () => {
    onStartAnalysis(language, frameRate);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg p-8 border border-gray-700">
        <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-200 mb-4">

        <div className="space-y-6 mb-6">

              <strong className="text-cyan-400">Features:</strong>
            </p>
            <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-sm text-gray-400 list-disc list-inside">
              <li>Automatic scene detection and shot breakdown</li>
              <li>AI-generated descriptions for each shot</li>
              <li>Precise timecode in/out points</li>
              <li>Shot thumbnails for visual reference</li>
              <li>Downloadable shot list in multiple formats</li>
            </ul>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-2">
                Description Language
              </label>
              <select
                value={language}
                onChange={(e) => setLanguage(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-600 rounded-lg text-gray-200 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-cyan-500 focus:border-transparent"
              >
                <option value="English">English</option>
                <option value="Spanish">Español</option>
                <option value="French">Français</option>
                <option value="German">Deutsch</option>
                <option value="Italian">Italiano</option>
                <option value="Portuguese">Português</option>
                <option value="Japanese">日本語</option>
                <option value="Chinese">中文</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-2">
                Frame Rate (FPS)
              </label>
              <select
                value={frameRate}
                onChange={(e) => setFrameRate(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-600 rounded-lg text-gray-200 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-cyan-500 focus:border-transparent"
              >
                <option value="24">24 fps (Film)</option>
                <option value="25">25 fps (PAL)</option>
                <option value="30">30 fps (NTSC)</option>
                <option value="50">50 fps</option>
                <option value="60">60 fps</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={onBack}
            className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold transition-all duration-200"
          >
            Back
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={handleStart}
            className="flex-1 px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-600 to-sky-600 hover:from-cyan-700 hover:to-sky-700 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg hover:shadow-cyan-500/50"
          >
            Generate Shot List
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

--- START OF FILE: components/TechSpecsView.tsx ---
import React from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Download, FileText, CheckCircle, Video, Clock, Volume2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { VideoMetadata, ProjectMetadata } from '../types';
import { exportToWord } from '../services/documentExportService';

interface TechSpecsViewProps {
    videoMetadata: VideoMetadata;
    projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata; // For "Production", "Episode", etc. in the header
    onBack: () => void;
    videoFileName: string;
}

const TechSpecsView: React.FC<TechSpecsViewProps> = ({ videoMetadata, projectMetadata, onBack, videoFileName }) => {

    const handleExportWord = () => {
        exportToWord({
            type: 'techspecs',
            data: videoMetadata, // We pass the video metadata as the primary data
            projectMetadata: projectMetadata,
            fileName: videoFileName
        });
    };

    // Helper to format large numbers
    const formatSize = (bytes: number) => {
        if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
        const k = 1024;
        const sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
        const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
        return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
    };

    // Helper for Timecode Format
    const getTimecodeFormat = (fps: number) => {
        // Basic logic
        if (Math.abs(fps - 29.97) < 0.01 || Math.abs(fps - 59.94) < 0.01) {
            return "Drop-Frame (SMPTE 12M)";
        }
        return "Non-Drop-Frame";
    };

    const getScanType = () => {
        // Basic assumption for file-based workflows
        return "Progressive";
    };

    const formattedDate = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
        year: 'numeric',
        month: 'long',
        day: 'numeric'
    });

    return (
        <div className="h-full flex flex-col bg-gray-900 text-white">
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-6 border-b border-gray-800 bg-gray-900/50 backdrop-blur-md sticky top-0 z-10">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <button
                        onClick={onBack}
                        className="p-2 hover:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg transition-colors text-gray-400 hover:text-white"
                    >
                        <ArrowLeft size={20} />
                    </button>
                    <div>
                        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                            <FileText className="text-gray-400" />
                            Technical Specifications Report
                        </h2>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
                            {videoFileName}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <button
                    onClick={handleExportWord}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg transition-all shadow-lg hover:shadow-blue-500/20 active:scale-95 font-medium"
                >
                    <Download size={18} />
                    Export Report (.docx)
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* Content */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-8">
                <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-8">

                    {/* Paper-like Representation */}
                    <div className="bg-white text-black p-12 rounded-lg shadow-2xl min-h-[800px] relative">

                        {/* Document Header */}
                        <div className="border-b-2 border-black pb-6 mb-8 flex justify-between items-end">
                            <div>
                                <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold uppercase tracking-wide">Technical Specifications</h1>
                                <p className="text-gray-600 mt-2 font-medium">Production: {projectMetadata.seriesTitle || "Untitled Production"}</p>
                                {projectMetadata.episodeTitle && <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm">Episode: {projectMetadata.episodeNumber} - {projectMetadata.episodeTitle}</p>}
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-right">
                                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Date Generated</p>
                                <p className="font-bold">{formattedDate}</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Video Specs Section */}
                        <div className="mb-8">
                            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold border-b border-gray-300 pb-2 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                                <Video size={20} /> VIDEO SPECIFICATIONS
                            </h3>
                            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-y-4 gap-x-8 text-sm">
                                <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                                    <span className="text-gray-600">File Format / Container</span>
                                    <span className="font-mono">{videoMetadata.format}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                                    <span className="text-gray-600">Video Codec</span>
                                    <span className="font-mono">{videoMetadata.codec}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                                    <span className="text-gray-600">Resolution</span>
                                    <span className="font-mono font-bold">{videoMetadata.width} x {videoMetadata.height}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                                    <span className="text-gray-600">Frame Rate</span>
                                    <span className="font-mono font-bold">{videoMetadata.frameRate.toFixed(3)} fps</span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                                    <span className="text-gray-600">Aspect Ratio</span>
                                    <span className="font-mono">{videoMetadata.aspectRatio}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                                    <span className="text-gray-600">Scan Type</span>
                                    <span className="font-mono">{getScanType()}</span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Audio Specs Section */}
                        <div className="mb-8">
                            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold border-b border-gray-300 pb-2 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                                <Volume2 size={20} /> AUDIO SPECIFICATIONS
                            </h3>
                            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-y-4 gap-x-8 text-sm">
                                <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                                    <span className="text-gray-600">Audio Channels</span>
                                    <span className="font-mono">{videoMetadata.hasAudio ? "Stereo (2.0) [Assumed]" : "None / Mute"}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                                    <span className="text-gray-600">Sample Rate</span>
                                    <span className="font-mono">48 kHz [Standard]</span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Program Info Section */}
                        <div className="mb-8">
                            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold border-b border-gray-300 pb-2 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                                <Clock size={20} /> PROGRAM INFORMATION
                            </h3>
                            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-y-4 gap-x-8 text-sm">
                                <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                                    <span className="text-gray-600">Total Runtime</span>
                                    <span className="font-mono font-bold">{new Date(videoMetadata.duration * 1000).toISOString().substr(11, 12)} (Approx)</span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">

                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Compliance Check */}

                                </div>
                            </div>

                        </div>

                    </div>

                </div>

--- START OF FILE: components/SynopsisInputView.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { SynopsisConfig } from '../types';

interface SynopsisInputViewProps {
  onGenerate: (writer: string, director: string, keywords: string, language: string) => void;
  synopsisConfig: SynopsisConfig;
  onConfigChange: (config: SynopsisConfig) => void;
  onBack: () => void;
}

const SynopsisInputView: React.FC<SynopsisInputViewProps> = ({
  onGenerate,
  synopsisConfig,
  onConfigChange,
  onBack,
}) => {
  const [writer, setWriter] = useState('');
  const [director, setDirector] = useState('');
  const [keywords, setKeywords] = useState('');
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState('English');
  const [showAdvanced, setShowAdvanced] = useState(false);

  const writerInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    writerInputRef.current?.focus();
  }, []);

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    if (writer.trim() && director.trim()) {
      onGenerate(writer, director, keywords, language);
    }
  };

  const isSubmitDisabled = !writer.trim() || !director.trim();

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <button
          onClick={onBack}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-300 transition-colors"
        >
          <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7" />
          </svg>
          Back
        </button>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-200">Synopsis Configuration</h2>
        <div className="w-20"></div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        
        {/* Basic Information */}
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-300">Basic Information</h3>
          
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="writer" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1">
              Written by <span className="text-red-400">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              ref={writerInputRef}
              type="text"
              id="writer"
              value={writer}
              onChange={(e) => setWriter(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 transition-colors"
              placeholder="e.g., Ruth Berry"
              maxLength={100}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="director" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1">
              Directed by <span className="text-red-400">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              id="director"
              value={director}
              onChange={(e) => setDirector(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 transition-colors"
              placeholder="e.g., Wayne de Lange"
              maxLength={100}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="keywords" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1">
              Keywords (optional)
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              id="keywords"
              value={keywords}
              onChange={(e) => setKeywords(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 transition-colors"
              placeholder="Drive the synopsis in a specific direction"
              maxLength={200}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="language" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1">
              Synopsis Language
            </label>
            <select
              id="language"
              value={language}
              onChange={(e) => setLanguage(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 transition-colors"
            >
              <option>English</option>
              <option>Spanish</option>
              <option>French</option>
              <option>German</option>
              <option>Japanese</option>
              <option>Italian</option>
              <option>Portuguese</option>
              <option>Chinese</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Advanced Settings */}
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-300">Synopsis Length</h3>
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowAdvanced(!showAdvanced)}
              className="text-sm text-indigo-400 hover:text-indigo-300 font-medium transition-colors"
            >
              {showAdvanced ? '▼ Hide' : '▶ Show'} Advanced
            </button>
          </div>

          {showAdvanced && (
            <div className="space-y-4 bg-gray-900/50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-700/50">
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mb-3">Customize the length of each synopsis element:</p>
              
              <ConfigControl
                label="Title Length"
                value={synopsisConfig.titleMaxChars}
                min={30}
                max={150}
                step={10}
                unit="characters"
                onChange={(val) => onConfigChange({ ...synopsisConfig, titleMaxChars: val })}
              />
              
              <ConfigControl
                label="Logline"
                value={synopsisConfig.loglineMaxWords}
                min={15}
                max={50}
                step={5}
                unit="words"
                onChange={(val) => onConfigChange({ ...synopsisConfig, loglineMaxWords: val })}
              />
              
              <ConfigControl
                label="Summary"
                value={synopsisConfig.summaryMaxWords}
                min={50}
                max={500}
                step={25}
                unit="words"
                onChange={(val) => onConfigChange({ ...synopsisConfig, summaryMaxWords: val })}
              />
              
              <ConfigControl
                label="Key Themes"
                value={synopsisConfig.keyThemesMaxWords}
                min={20}
                max={150}
                step={10}
                unit="words"
                onChange={(val) => onConfigChange({ ...synopsisConfig, keyThemesMaxWords: val })}
              />
              
              <ConfigControl
                label="Target Audience"
                value={synopsisConfig.targetAudienceMaxWords}
                min={15}
                max={100}
                step={5}
                unit="words"
                onChange={(val) => onConfigChange({ ...synopsisConfig, targetAudienceMaxWords: val })}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Submit Button */}
      <div className="text-center pt-4">
        <button
          onClick={handleSubmit}
          disabled={isSubmitDisabled}
          className="px-10 py-3 font-bold text-white bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-indigo-600 rounded-lg hover:from-purple-700 hover:to-indigo-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-gray-800 focus:ring-purple-500 transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg disabled:from-gray-600 disabled:to-gray-600 disabled:text-gray-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transform hover:scale-105 disabled:hover:scale-100"
        >
          Generate Synopsis
        </button>
        {isSubmitDisabled && (
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mt-3">
            Please fill in the 'Written by' and 'Directed by' fields to continue.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface ConfigControlProps {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  step: number;
  unit: 'words' | 'characters';
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
}

const ConfigControl: React.FC<ConfigControlProps> = ({
  label,
  value,
  min,
  max,
  step,
  unit,
  onChange,
}) => {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">{label}</label>
        <span className="text-sm text-gray-400">{value} {unit}</span>
      </div>
      <input
        type="range"
        min={min}
        max={max}
        step={step}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(parseInt(e.target.value, 10))}
        className="w-full h-2 bg-gray-700 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-indigo-500"
      />
    </div>
  );
};

export default SynopsisInputView;

--- START OF FILE: components/FaunaResultView.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { SpeciesSighting, FaunaConfig, VideoMetadata, ProjectMetadata } from '../types';
// @ts-ignore
import * as ReactWindow from 'react-window';
const FixedSizeList = ReactWindow.FixedSizeList || (ReactWindow as any).default?.FixedSizeList;

import { Download, ArrowLeft, Copy, Check, Filter, Database } from 'lucide-react';
import { copyToClipboard, parseTimecode } from '../utils/tableUtils';
import VideoThumbnail from './VideoThumbnail';
import ExportModal from './ExportModal';



interface FaunaResultViewProps {
  sightings: SpeciesSighting[];
  videoFileName: string;
  videoPreviewUrl: string | null; // Added prop
  videoMetadata: VideoMetadata | null;
  projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata;
  onReset: () => void;
  onBack: () => void;
  config: FaunaConfig;
  onSave: (data: SpeciesSighting[]) => Promise<void>;
}

interface SpeciesEntry {
  commonName: string;
  latinName: string;
  germanName: string;
  firstTimecode: string;
  thumbnail?: string;
  allSightings: SpeciesSighting[];
  behaviors: string[]; // Aggregated unique behaviors
  iucnStatus?: string; // Should be consistent for the species
}

const FaunaResultView: React.FC<FaunaResultViewProps> = ({
  sightings,
  videoFileName,
  videoPreviewUrl,
  videoMetadata,
  projectMetadata,
  onReset,
  onBack,
  config,
  onSave,
}) => {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [isSaved, setIsSaved] = useState(false);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [filterCategory, setFilterCategory] = useState<string>('all');

  const [showExportModal, setShowExportModal] = useState(false);

  const programTitle = videoFileName.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '');

  // Group sightings by species and get first appearance
  const speciesList = useMemo((): SpeciesEntry[] => {
    const speciesMap = new Map<string, SpeciesEntry>();
    const safeSightings = Array.isArray(sightings) ? sightings : [];

    safeSightings.forEach(sighting => {
      const key = sighting.latinName || sighting.commonName;

      if (!speciesMap.has(key)) {
        speciesMap.set(key, {
          commonName: sighting.commonName,
          latinName: sighting.latinName,
          germanName: sighting.germanName || '',
          firstTimecode: sighting.timecode,
          thumbnail: sighting.thumbnail,
          allSightings: [sighting],
          behaviors: sighting.behavior ? [sighting.behavior] : [],
          iucnStatus: sighting.iucnStatus,
        });
      } else {
        const entry = speciesMap.get(key)!;
        entry.allSightings.push(sighting);
        if (sighting.behavior && !entry.behaviors.includes(sighting.behavior)) {
          entry.behaviors.push(sighting.behavior);
        }
        if (sighting.iucnStatus && !entry.iucnStatus) {
          entry.iucnStatus = sighting.iucnStatus;
        }

        // @ts-ignore
        if (parseTimecode(sighting.timecode) < parseTimecode(entry.firstTimecode)) {
          entry.firstTimecode = sighting.timecode;
          entry.thumbnail = sighting.thumbnail;
        }
      }
    });

    // @ts-ignore
    return Array.from(speciesMap.values()).sort((a, b) => parseTimecode(a.firstTimecode) - parseTimecode(b.firstTimecode));
  }, [sightings]);

  const categories = useMemo(() => Array.from(new Set(sightings.map(s => s.category).filter(Boolean))).sort() as string[], [sightings]);

  const filteredSpeciesList = useMemo(() => {
    if (filterCategory === 'all') return speciesList;
    return speciesList.filter(species =>
      species.allSightings.some(s => s.category === filterCategory)
    );
  }, [speciesList, filterCategory]);

  const generateTableText = (): string => {
    let output = `SPECIES LIST / ARTENLISTE\n${programTitle.toUpperCase()}\n\n`;
    output += `┌───────────────────┬──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────┬──────────┬──────────────────┐\n`;
    output += `│ COMMON NAME       │ LATIN NAME               │ ${config.secondaryLanguage.toUpperCase().substring(0, 15).padEnd(15)} │ BEHAVIOR             │ IUCN     │ 1st APPEARANCE   │\n`;
    output += `├───────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼──────────────────────┼──────────┼──────────────────┤\n`;
    filteredSpeciesList.forEach(s => {
      output += `│ ${s.commonName.substring(0, 17).padEnd(17)} │ ${s.latinName.substring(0, 24).padEnd(24)} │ ${(s.germanName || '').substring(0, 24).padEnd(24)} │ ${s.behaviors.join(', ').substring(0, 20).padEnd(20)} │ ${(s.iucnStatus || '-').substring(0, 8).padEnd(8)} │ ${s.firstTimecode.padEnd(16)} │\n`;
    });
    output += `└───────────────────┴──────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────┴──────────────────────┴──────────┴──────────────────┘\n`;
    return output;
  };

  const handleCopyTable = async () => {
    if (await copyToClipboard(generateTableText())) {
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveToDatabase = async () => {
    setIsSaving(true);
    await onSave(sightings);
    setIsSaved(true);
    setIsSaving(false);
  };


  const videoRef = React.useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);

  const handleSeek = (timecode: string) => {
    if (videoMetadata && videoRef.current) {
      const seconds = parseTimecode(timecode, videoMetadata.frameRate || 25);

      // Always pause to satisfy "show the PAUSED frame"
      videoRef.current.pause();

      // Avoid micro-seeks if already close - matching TranscriptionView behavior
      if (Math.abs(videoRef.current.currentTime - seconds) > 0.5) {
        videoRef.current.currentTime = seconds;
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <button onClick={onBack} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"><ArrowLeft size={20} />Back</button>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button onClick={handleCopyTable} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 rounded-lg">{copied ? <Check size={18} /> : <Copy size={18} />}{copied ? 'Copied!' : 'Copy Table'}</button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setShowExportModal(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 rounded-lg text-white font-semibold"
          >
            <Download size={18} />Export Options...
          </button>

          <ExportModal
            isOpen={showExportModal}
            onClose={() => setShowExportModal(false)}
            type="fauna"
            data={sightings}
            projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
            videoFileName={videoFileName}
            frameRate={videoMetadata?.frameRate || 25}
          />

          <button onClick={handleSaveToDatabase} disabled={isSaved || isSaving} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 rounded-lg disabled:bg-gray-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed">{isSaved ? <Check size={18} /> : <Database size={18} />}{isSaved ? 'Saved' : isSaving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save'}</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="text-center">
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-green-400 to-emerald-500">Species List / Artenliste</h2>
        <p className="text-gray-400 mt-2 font-semibold">{programTitle.toUpperCase()}</p>
      </div>

      {videoPreviewUrl && (
        <div className="mb-6 bg-black rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-700 shadow-xl relative aspect-video max-h-[400px] mx-auto">
          <video
            ref={videoRef}
            src={videoPreviewUrl}
            controls
            className="w-full h-full"
          />
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg text-center"><div className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-400">{filteredSpeciesList.length}</div><div className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1">Unique Species</div></div>
        <div className="bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg text-center"><div className="text-3xl font-bold text-emerald-400">{sightings.length}</div><div className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1">Total Sightings</div></div>
        <div className="bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg text-center"><div className="text-3xl font-bold text-teal-400">{categories.length}</div><div className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1">Categories</div></div>
      </div>

      {categories.length > 0 && <div className="flex items-center gap-3"><Filter size={18} className="text-gray-400" /><select value={filterCategory} onChange={e => setFilterCategory(e.target.value)} className="bg-gray-700 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500"><option value="all">All Categories ({speciesList.length})</option>{categories.map(cat => { const count = speciesList.filter(s => s.allSightings.some(sight => sight.category === cat)).length; return <option key={cat} value={cat}>{cat} ({count})</option>; })}</select></div>}

      <div className="border border-gray-700 rounded-lg overflow-hidden bg-gray-800/20">
        {/* Table Header - Static */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-[120px_1fr_1fr_1.5fr_100px_100px_100px] bg-gray-700/50 border-b border-gray-600 text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300">
          <div className="px-4 py-3 border-r border-gray-700">Preview</div>
          <div className="px-4 py-3 border-r border-gray-700">COMMON NAME</div>
          <div className="px-4 py-3 border-r border-gray-700">LATIN NAME</div>
          <div className="px-4 py-3 border-r border-gray-700">BEHAVIORS</div>
          <div className="px-4 py-3 border-r border-gray-700">IUCN</div>
          <div className="px-4 py-3 border-r border-gray-700 text-center">1st</div>
          <div className="px-4 py-3 text-center">COUNT</div>
        </div>

        {/* Virtualized List */}
        <FixedSizeList
          height={600}
          width="100%"
          itemCount={filteredSpeciesList.length}
          itemSize={80} // Height of each row
          className="scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-gray-600 scrollbar-track-gray-800"
        >
          {({ index, style }: { index: number; style: React.CSSProperties }) => {
            const species = filteredSpeciesList[index];
            return (
              <div
                style={style}
                onClick={() => handleSeek(species.firstTimecode)}
                className={`grid grid-cols-[120px_1fr_1fr_1.5fr_100px_100px_100px] items-center 
                  ${index % 2 === 0 ? 'bg-gray-800/40' : 'bg-gray-800/20'} 
                  hover:bg-gray-700/30 transition-colors cursor-pointer group border-b border-gray-700/50`}
              >
                <div className="px-4 py-2 border-r border-gray-700 relative h-full flex items-center justify-center">
                  <VideoThumbnail videoUrl={videoPreviewUrl} timecode={species.firstTimecode} className="w-24 h-14 rounded-md pointer-events-none opacity-80 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity object-cover bg-black" />
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 pointer-events-none">
                    <span className="bg-black/60 text-white rounded-full p-1"><ArrowLeft size={16} className="rotate-180" /></span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="px-4 py-2 border-r border-gray-700 text-sm text-gray-300 group-hover:text-white truncate" title={species.commonName}>{species.commonName}</div>
                <div className="px-4 py-2 border-r border-gray-700 text-sm text-gray-400 italic truncate" title={species.latinName}>{species.latinName}</div>
                <div className="px-4 py-2 border-r border-gray-700 text-sm text-gray-400 truncate" title={species.behaviors.join(', ')}>{species.behaviors.join(', ') || '-'}</div>
                <div className="px-4 py-2 border-r border-gray-700 text-sm text-gray-400 truncate">{species.iucnStatus || '-'}</div>
                <div className="px-4 py-2 border-r border-gray-700 text-sm font-mono text-center text-gray-400 group-hover:text-indigo-400 font-bold">{species.firstTimecode}</div>
                <div className="px-4 py-2 text-center text-sm"><span className="inline-block px-2 py-1 bg-green-500/20 text-green-300 rounded text-xs font-medium">{species.allSightings.length}</span></div>
              </div>
            );
          }}
        </FixedSizeList>
      </div>

      <div className="flex justify-center gap-4 pt-6 border-t border-gray-700/50"><button onClick={onReset} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 rounded-lg transition-colors font-medium">Analyze Another Video</button></div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default FaunaResultView;

--- START OF FILE: components/BioInputView.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';

interface BioInputViewProps {
  onGenerate: (
    talents: { name: string; context: string }[],
    language: string,
    autoDetect: boolean
  ) => void;
  onBack: () => void;
}

const BioInputView: React.FC<BioInputViewProps> = ({ onGenerate, onBack }) => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'auto' | 'manual'>('auto');
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState('English');
  const [videoTopic, setVideoTopic] = useState('');
  const [talents, setTalents] = useState<{ name: string; context: string }[]>([
    { name: '', context: '' },
  ]);

  const handleAddTalent = () => {
    setTalents([...talents, { name: '', context: '' }]);
  };

  const handleRemoveTalent = (index: number) => {
    setTalents(talents.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  const handleTalentChange = (index: number, field: 'name' | 'context', value: string) => {
    const updated = [...talents];
    updated[index][field] = value;
    setTalents(updated);
  };

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    if (mode === 'auto') {
      onGenerate([], language, true);
    } else {
      const validTalents = talents.filter(t => t.name.trim() !== '');
      if (validTalents.length > 0) {
        onGenerate(validTalents, language, false);
      }
    }
  };

  const isSubmitDisabled = mode === 'manual' && talents.every(t => t.name.trim() === '');

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <button
          onClick={onBack}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-300 transition-colors"
        >
          <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7" />
          </svg>
          Back
        </button>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-200">Bio Generation</h2>
        <div className="w-20"></div>
      </div>

      {/* Mode Selection */}
      <div className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-3">Detection Mode</label>
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('auto')}
            className={`flex-1 py-3 px-4 rounded-lg font-medium transition-all ${
              mode === 'auto'
                ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-lg'
                : 'bg-gray-700 text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-600'
            }`}
          >
            Auto-Detect Talent
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('manual')}
            className={`flex-1 py-3 px-4 rounded-lg font-medium transition-all ${
              mode === 'manual'
                ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-lg'
                : 'bg-gray-700 text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-600'
            }`}
          >
            Enter Manually
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Language Selection */}
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="bio-language" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1">
          Biography Language
        </label>
        <select
          id="bio-language"
          value={language}
          onChange={(e) => setLanguage(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 transition-colors"
        >
          <option>English</option>
          <option>Spanish</option>
          <option>French</option>
          <option>German</option>
          <option>Japanese</option>
          <option>Italian</option>
          <option>Portuguese</option>
          <option>Chinese</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      {/* Auto Mode Options */}
      {mode === 'auto' && (
        <div className="bg-indigo-500/10 border border-indigo-500/30 rounded-lg p-4">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-400 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
            </svg>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-300 mb-3">
                The system will automatically scan the video for on-screen talent introductions (lower thirds) and generate biographies for each person found.
              </p>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="video-topic" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1">
                  Video Topic (optional)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  id="video-topic"
                  value={videoTopic}
                  onChange={(e) => setVideoTopic(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 transition-colors"
                  placeholder="e.g., Marine biology experts, Tech entrepreneurs"
                />
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
                  Helps provide context for better biographies
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Manual Mode Options */}
      {mode === 'manual' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Talent List</label>
            <button
              onClick={handleAddTalent}
              className="text-sm text-indigo-400 hover:text-indigo-300 font-medium flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 4v16m8-8H4" />
              </svg>
              Add Person
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
            {talents.map((talent, index) => (
              <div key={index} className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 mb-1">
                        Name <span className="text-red-400">*</span>
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={talent.name}
                        onChange={(e) => handleTalentChange(index, 'name', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-white text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 transition-colors"
                        placeholder="e.g., Dr. Jane Smith"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 mb-1">
                        Context (optional)
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={talent.context}
                        onChange={(e) => handleTalentChange(index, 'context', e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-white text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 transition-colors"
                        placeholder="e.g., Marine biologist, Professor at MIT"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  {talents.length > 1 && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleRemoveTalent(index)}
                      className="flex-shrink-0 text-red-400 hover:text-red-300 p-1"
                    >
                      <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" />
                      </svg>
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Submit Button */}
      <div className="text-center pt-4">
        <button
          onClick={handleSubmit}
          disabled={isSubmitDisabled}
          className="px-10 py-3 font-bold text-white bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-blue-600 rounded-lg hover:from-indigo-700 hover:to-blue-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-gray-800 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg disabled:from-gray-600 disabled:to-gray-600 disabled:text-gray-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transform hover:scale-105 disabled:hover:scale-100"
        >
          Generate Biographies
        </button>
        {isSubmitDisabled && (
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mt-3">
            Please enter at least one talent name to continue.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default BioInputView;

--- START OF FILE: components/VideoThumbnail.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { thumbnailGenerator } from '../utils/thumbnailUtils';

interface VideoThumbnailProps {
  videoUrl: string | null;
  timecode: string | number;
  alt?: string;
  className?: string;
}

const VideoThumbnail: React.FC<VideoThumbnailProps> = ({ videoUrl, timecode, alt = 'Video thumbnail', className = '' }) => {
  const [thumbnail, setThumbnail] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);
  const imgRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (entries[0].isIntersecting) {
          setIsVisible(true);
          observer.disconnect();
        }
      },
      { rootMargin: '50px' }
    );

    if (imgRef.current) {
      observer.observe(imgRef.current);
    }

    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setThumbnail(null);
  }, [videoUrl, timecode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // If no video URL is provided, we can't generate a thumbnail.
    if (!isVisible || !videoUrl || thumbnail) return;

    let isMounted = true;
    thumbnailGenerator.generateThumbnail(videoUrl, timecode).then((dataUrl) => {
      if (isMounted) setThumbnail(dataUrl);
    });

    return () => { isMounted = false; };
  }, [isVisible, videoUrl, timecode, thumbnail]);

  return (
    <div ref={imgRef} className={`relative overflow-hidden bg-gray-800 rounded-md ${className}`} style={{ minHeight: '60px', minWidth: '100px' }}>
      {thumbnail ? (
        <img src={thumbnail} alt={alt} className="w-full h-full object-cover animate-fade-in" />
      ) : !videoUrl ? (
        // Fallback when video URL is missing (e.g. history/cloud mode)
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700/50 p-2 text-center">
          <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-600 mb-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={1.5} d="M3 15a4 4 0 004 4h9a5 5 0 10-.1-9.999 5.002 5.002 0 10-9.78 2.096A4.001 4.001 0 003 15z" /><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={1.5} d="M12 15V19M12 19L15 17M12 19L9 17" /></svg>
          <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-500 font-medium leading-tight">Cloud<br />File</span>
        </div>
      ) : (
        // Loading state
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
          <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-gray-600 border-t-gray-400 rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default VideoThumbnail;
--- END OF FILE: components/VideoThumbnail.tsx ---

--- START OF FILE: components/ProjectMetadataView.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ProjectMetadata, ExportTemplate } from '../types';
import { Save, ArrowLeft, X, LogIn, LogOut, CheckCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { authService } from '../services/authService';

interface ProjectMetadataViewProps {
    initialMetadata: ProjectMetadata;
    onSave: (metadata: ProjectMetadata, gcsToken?: string, gcsProjectId?: string, useProMode?: boolean) => void;
    onCheckAuth: () => void;
    onCancel: () => void;
    currentGcsToken?: string;
    currentGcsProjectId?: string;
    currentUseProMode?: boolean;
    googleUserEmail?: string;
    onGoogleSignIn: (email: string, token: string) => void;
    onGoogleSignOut: () => void;
}

const ProjectMetadataView: React.FC<ProjectMetadataViewProps> = ({
    initialMetadata,
    onSave,
    onCancel,
    currentGcsToken,
    currentGcsProjectId,
    currentUseProMode,
    googleUserEmail,
    onGoogleSignIn,
    onGoogleSignOut
}) => {
    const [metadata, setMetadata] = useState<ProjectMetadata>(initialMetadata);
    // Keep internal state for fallback/legacy token entry
    const [gcsToken, setGcsToken] = useState(currentGcsToken || '');
    const [gcsProjectId, setGcsProjectId] = useState(currentGcsProjectId || '');
    const [useProMode, setUseProMode] = useState(currentUseProMode || false);
    const [isAuthenticating, setIsAuthenticating] = useState(false);

    const handleChange = (field: keyof ProjectMetadata, value: string) => {
        setMetadata(prev => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));
    };

    const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        onSave(metadata, gcsToken, gcsProjectId, useProMode);
    };

    const handleGoogleSignIn = async () => {
        setIsAuthenticating(true);
        try {
            const user = await authService.signIn();
            onGoogleSignIn(user.email, user.accessToken);
            setGcsToken(user.accessToken); // Auto-fill the token field
            setUseProMode(true); // Auto-enable Pro Mode
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Google Sign-In failed", error);
            alert("Sign-In failed. Please try again or use manual token entry.");
        } finally {
            setIsAuthenticating(false);
        }
    };

    const handleGoogleSignOut = () => {
        authService.signOut();
        onGoogleSignOut();
        setGcsToken(''); // Clear the token
        setUseProMode(false); // Disable Pro Mode
    };

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in relative">
            <button
                onClick={onCancel}
                className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
            >
                <X size={24} />
            </button>

            <div className="text-center pb-6 border-b border-gray-700">
                <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-400 to-cyan-500">
                    Project Settings
                </h2>
                <p className="text-gray-400 mt-2">
                    Global metadata for your document headers
                </p>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="max-w-2xl mx-auto space-y-6">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                    {/* ... (Existing Metadata Fields - Kept Compact for Brevity) ... */}
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Production Company</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={metadata.productionCompany}
                            onChange={e => handleChange('productionCompany', e.target.value)}
                            className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white placeholder-gray-500"
                            placeholder="e.g. Discovery Channel"
                        />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Series Title</label>
                        <input type="text" value={metadata.seriesTitle} onChange={e => handleChange('seriesTitle', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white placeholder-gray-500" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Episode Title</label>
                        <input type="text" value={metadata.episodeTitle} onChange={e => handleChange('episodeTitle', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white placeholder-gray-500" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Episode Number</label>
                        <input type="text" value={metadata.episodeNumber} onChange={e => handleChange('episodeNumber', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white placeholder-gray-500" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Director</label>
                        <input type="text" value={metadata.director} onChange={e => handleChange('director', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Producer</label>
                        <input type="text" value={metadata.producer} onChange={e => handleChange('producer', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Editor</label>
                        <input type="text" value={metadata.editor} onChange={e => handleChange('editor', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Date</label>
                        <input type="date" value={metadata.date} onChange={e => handleChange('date', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Export Template</label>
                        <select value={metadata.exportTemplate || 'Generic'} onChange={e => handleChange('exportTemplate', e.target.value as ExportTemplate)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white">
                            <option value="Generic">Generic (Default)</option>
                            <option value="PBS">PBS (US Public TV)</option>
                            <option value="BBC">BBC (UK)</option>
                            <option value="ARTE">ARTE (Franco-German)</option>
                            <option value="ZDF">ZDF (German)</option>
                            <option value="FranceTV">France Télévisions</option>
                            <option value="ORF">ORF (Austrian)</option>
                            <option value="Custom">Custom Branding</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Start Timecode</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={metadata.timecodeStart || '00:00:00:00'}
                            onChange={e => handleChange('timecodeStart', e.target.value)}
                            className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 text-white font-mono placeholder-gray-500"
                            placeholder="00:00:00:00"
                        />
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Offset for exports (e.g. 01:00:00:00)</p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-6 border-t border-gray-700">
                    <div className="space-y-4">
                        <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold text-blue-400">Cloud Connection</h3>

                        {!googleUserEmail ? (
                            <div className="bg-gray-800 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-700 space-y-3">
                                <p className="text-sm text-gray-300">
                                    Sign in with Google to unlock <strong>Pro Mode</strong> (Unlimited Upload Size, Zero Hallucinations).
                                </p>
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={handleGoogleSignIn}
                                    disabled={isAuthenticating}
                                    className="w-full py-2 bg-white hover:bg-gray-100 text-gray-900 font-medium rounded flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors"
                                >
                                    {isAuthenticating ? (
                                        <span className="animate-pulse">Connecting...</span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <>
                                            <LogIn size={18} />
                                            Sign In with Google
                                        </>
                                    )}
                                </button>
                                <div className="relative flex py-2 items-center">
                                    <div className="flex-grow border-t border-gray-700"></div>
                                    <span className="flex-shrink-0 mx-4 text-gray-500 text-xs">OR USE MANUAL TOKEN</span>
                                    <div className="flex-grow border-t border-gray-700"></div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ) : (
                            <div className="bg-green-900/20 p-4 rounded-lg border border-green-800/50 space-y-3">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                    <CheckCircle className="text-green-500" size={24} />
                                    <div>
                                        <h4 className="font-semibold text-white">Connected</h4>
                                        <p className="text-xs text-green-400">{googleUserEmail}</p>
                                    </div>
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={handleGoogleSignOut}
                                        className="ml-auto p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-400 transition-colors"
                                        title="Disconnect"
                                    >
                                        <LogOut size={18} />
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                                <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                                    {currentGcsToken ? (
                                        <span className="text-green-400">Token active. Pro Mode is available.</span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <span className="text-yellow-400">Signed in, but token missing (Refresh page).</span>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {/* Manual entry fallback (hidden if signed in, but still available in DOM/state) */}
                        {!googleUserEmail && (
                            <div className="space-y-2 opacity-75">
                                <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">GCS Access Token (Manual)</label>
                                <input
                                    type="password"
                                    value={gcsToken}
                                    onChange={e => setGcsToken(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg text-white font-mono text-sm"
                                    placeholder="ya29.a0..."
                                />
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-gray-700">
                            <label className={`flex items-center space-x-3 cursor-pointer group ${!gcsToken ? 'opacity-50 pointer-events-none' : ''}`}>
                                <div className={`relative w-14 h-7 transition-colors rounded-full ${useProMode ? 'bg-blue-600' : 'bg-gray-600'}`}>
                                    <input
                                        type="checkbox"
                                        className="sr-only"
                                        checked={useProMode}
                                        onChange={e => setUseProMode(e.target.checked)}
                                        disabled={!gcsToken}
                                    />
                                    <div className={`absolute left-1 top-1 w-5 h-5 bg-white rounded-full transition-transform ${useProMode ? 'translate-x-7' : 'translate-x-0'}`} />
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <span className="text-sm font-medium text-white group-hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors">
                                        Pro Mode (Zero Hallucination)
                                    </span>
                                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5 max-w-sm">
                                        {gcsToken ? 'Enabled by active token.' : 'Sign in or enter token to enable.'}
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </label>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-8 flex justify-center gap-4">
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onCancel}
                        className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors"
                    >
                        <ArrowLeft size={20} />
                        Cancel
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="submit"
                        className="px-6 py-3 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors shadow-lg shadow-blue-900/20"
                    >
                        <Save size={20} />
                        Save Settings
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
};

export default ProjectMetadataView;
