--- START OF FILE: components/SynopsisResultView.tsx ---
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Synopsis, VideoMetadata, ProjectMetadata } from '../types';
import { generateCSV, downloadFile, copyToClipboard } from '../utils/tableUtils';
import { ArrowLeft, Download, Save, RefreshCw, Database, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { exportToWord } from '../services/documentExportService';
import { exportToJSON } from '../services/exportService';

interface SynopsisResultViewProps {
  synopsis: Synopsis;
  onReset: () => void;
  onBack: () => void;
  onSave: (data: Synopsis) => Promise<void>;
  videoFileName: string;
  videoMetadata: VideoMetadata | null;
  projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata;
}

const SynopsisResultView: React.FC<SynopsisResultViewProps> = ({ synopsis, onReset, onBack, onSave, videoFileName, videoMetadata, projectMetadata }) => {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [isSaved, setIsSaved] = useState(false);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  const handleDownloadCSV = () => {
    const headers = ['Section', 'Content'];
    const rows = [
      ['Title', synopsis.title],
      ['Written By', synopsis.writtenBy],
      ['Directed By', synopsis.directedBy],
      ['Logline', synopsis.oneLine],
      ['Summary', synopsis.paragraph],
      ['Full Synopsis', synopsis.fullSynopsis],
      ['Key Themes', synopsis.keyThemes],
      ['Target Audience', synopsis.targetAudience],
    ];
    const csvContent = generateCSV(headers, rows);
    downloadFile(csvContent, `${synopsis.title.replace(/ /g, '_')} _synopsis.csv`, 'text/csv');
  };

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    const text = `
${synopsis.title.toUpperCase()}
Written by ${synopsis.writtenBy}
Directed by ${synopsis.directedBy}

LOGLINE
${synopsis.oneLine}

SUMMARY
${synopsis.paragraph}

FULL SYNOPSIS
${synopsis.fullSynopsis}

KEY THEMES
${synopsis.keyThemes}

TARGET AUDIENCE
${synopsis.targetAudience}
`.trim();

    if (await copyToClipboard(text)) {
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveToDatabase = async () => {
    setIsSaving(true);
    await onSave(synopsis);
    setIsSaved(true);
    setIsSaving(false);
  };

  const handleExportJSON = () => {
    exportToJSON(synopsis, 'synopsis', videoFileName);
  };

  const handleExportWord = () => {
    exportToWord({
      type: 'synopsis',
      data: synopsis,
      projectMetadata: projectMetadata,
      fileName: videoFileName
    });
  };


  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="text-center pb-4 border-b border-gray-700">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-16 h-16 bg-green-500/20 rounded-full mb-4">
          <svg className="w-10 h-10 text-green-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M5 13l4 4L19 7" />
          </svg>
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-200">Synopsis Generated!</h3>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-white mb-2 text-center">
            {synopsis.title}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-center text-gray-400">
            Written by <span className="text-indigo-400">{synopsis.writtenBy}</span> •
            Directed by <span className="text-indigo-400">{synopsis.directedBy}</span>
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-900/30 to-indigo-900/30 rounded-lg p-4 border border-purple-500/30">
          <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-purple-400 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">
            Logline
          </h4>
          <p className="text-gray-200 italic">
            {synopsis.oneLine}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
          <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">
            Summary
          </h4>
          <p className="text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">
            {synopsis.paragraph}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
          <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">
            Full Synopsis
          </h4>
          <div className="text-gray-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
            {synopsis.fullSynopsis}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <div className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
            <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">
              Key Themes
            </h4>
            <p className="text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">
              {synopsis.keyThemes}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
            <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">
              Target Audience
            </h4>
            <p className="text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">
              {synopsis.targetAudience}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-center pt-4">
        <button
          onClick={handleCopy}
          className="px-6 py-3 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg transform hover:scale-105 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          {copied ? (
            <><svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M5 13l4 4L19 7" /></svg>Copied!</>
          ) : (
            <><svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M8 16H6a2 2 0 01-2-2V6a2 2 0 012-2h8a2 2 0 012 2v2m-6 12h8a2 2 0 002-2v-8a2 2 0 00-2-2h-8a2 2 0 00-2 2v8a2 2 0 002 2z" /></svg>Copy All</>
          )}
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={handleDownloadCSV}
          className="px-6 py-3 bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg transform hover:scale-105 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 17v-2m3 2v-4m3 4v-6m2 10H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2-2z" /></svg>
          Download CSV
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={handleExportWord}
          className="px-6 py-3 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg transform hover:scale-105 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M4 16v1a3 3 0 003 3h10a3 3 0 003-3v-1m-4-4l-4 4m0 0l-4-4m4 4V4" /></svg>
          Export Word
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={handleExportJSON}
          className="px-6 py-3 bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg transform hover:scale-105 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <Database size={20} />
          Export JSON
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={handleSaveToDatabase}
          disabled={isSaved || isSaving}
          className="px-6 py-3 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg transform hover:scale-105 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:bg-gray-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
        >
          {isSaved ? (
            <><Check size={18} />Saved</>
          ) : (
            <><Database size={18} />{isSaving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save to Database'}</>
          )}
        </button>
        <button onClick={onBack} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg">
          Back to Dashboard
        </button>
        <button onClick={onReset} className="px-6 py-3 bg-red-800 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg">
          New Video
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SynopsisResultView;

--- END OF FILE: components/SynopsisResultView.tsx ---

--- START OF FILE: components/GraphicsResultView.tsx ---
import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { GraphicsAnalysisResult, VideoMetadata, ProjectMetadata } from '../types';
import { Download, ArrowLeft, Copy, Check, Database } from 'lucide-react';
import { copyToClipboard } from '../utils/tableUtils';
import VideoThumbnail from './VideoThumbnail';
import ExportModal from './ExportModal';

interface GraphicsResultViewProps {
  result: GraphicsAnalysisResult;
  videoFileName: string;
  videoPreviewUrl: string | null;
  videoMetadata: VideoMetadata | null;
  projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata;
  onReset: () => void;
  onBack: () => void;
  onSave: (data: GraphicsAnalysisResult) => Promise<void>;
}

type CombinedGraphicItem = {
  type: 'graphic' | 'lowerThird';
  description: string;
  tcIn: string;
  tcOut: string;
  cleanTcIn?: string;
  cleanTcOut?: string;
};

const GraphicsResultView: React.FC<GraphicsResultViewProps> = ({
  result,
  videoFileName,
  videoPreviewUrl,
  videoMetadata,
  projectMetadata,
  onReset,
  onBack,
  onSave,
}) => {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [isSaved, setIsSaved] = useState(false);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [showExportModal, setShowExportModal] = useState(false);

  const combinedItems = useMemo((): CombinedGraphicItem[] => {
    const items: CombinedGraphicItem[] = [];
    result.graphics?.forEach(g => items.push({ type: 'graphic', description: g.description, tcIn: g.timecodeIn, tcOut: g.timecodeOut, cleanTcIn: g.cleanTimecodeIn, cleanTcOut: g.cleanTimecodeOut }));
    result.lowerThirds?.forEach(lt => items.push({ type: 'lowerThird', description: lt.text, tcIn: lt.timecodeIn, tcOut: lt.timecodeOut }));
    // @ts-ignore
    return items.sort((a, b) => a.tcIn.localeCompare(b.tcIn));
  }, [result]);

  const programTitle = videoFileName.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '');

  const generateTableText = (): string => {
    let output = `PROGRAM GRAPHICS & CLEAN SCENES LOG\nPROGRAM: ${programTitle.toUpperCase()}\nRUNTIME: ${result.runtime || 'N/A'}\n\n`;
    output += `┌──────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────┬──────────────┬──────────────┬──────────────┐\n`;
    output += `│ Description                              │ Program TC In│ Program TC Out│ Clean TC In  │ Clean TC Out │\n`;
    output += `├──────────────────────────────────────────┼──────────────┼──────────────┼──────────────┼──────────────┤\n`;
    combinedItems.forEach(item => {
      output += `│ ${item.description.substring(0, 40).padEnd(40)} │ ${item.tcIn.padEnd(12)} │ ${item.tcOut.padEnd(13)} │ ${(item.cleanTcIn || '').padEnd(12)} │ ${(item.cleanTcOut || '').padEnd(12)} │\n`;
    });
    output += `└──────────────────────────────────────────┴──────────────┴──────────────┴──────────────┴──────────────┘\n`;
    return output;
  };

  const handleCopyTable = async () => {
    if (await copyToClipboard(generateTableText())) {
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveToDatabase = async () => {
    setIsSaving(true);
    await onSave(result);
    setIsSaved(true);
    setIsSaving(false);
  };





  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <button onClick={onBack} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"><ArrowLeft size={20} />Back</button>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button onClick={handleCopyTable} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 rounded-lg">{copied ? <Check size={18} /> : <Copy size={18} />}{copied ? 'Copied!' : 'Copy Table'}</button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setShowExportModal(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 rounded-lg text-white font-semibold"
          >
            <Download size={18} />Export Options...
          </button>

          <ExportModal
            isOpen={showExportModal}
            onClose={() => setShowExportModal(false)}
            type="graphics"
            data={result}
            projectMetadata={projectMetadata}
            videoFileName={videoFileName}
            frameRate={videoMetadata?.frameRate || 25}
          />

          <button onClick={handleSaveToDatabase} disabled={isSaved || isSaving} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 rounded-lg disabled:bg-gray-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed">{isSaved ? <Check size={18} /> : <Database size={18} />}{isSaved ? 'Saved' : isSaving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save'}</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="text-center">
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-400 to-indigo-500">Graphics & Lower Thirds Log</h2>
        <p className="text-gray-400 mt-2">Program: {programTitle}</p>
        {result.runtime && <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm">Runtime: {result.runtime}</p>}
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg text-center"><div className="text-3xl font-bold text-purple-400">{result.graphics?.length || 0}</div><div className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1">Graphics</div></div>
        <div className="bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg text-center"><div className="text-3xl font-bold text-indigo-400">{result.lowerThirds?.length || 0}</div><div className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1">Lower Thirds</div></div>
        <div className="bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg text-center"><div className="text-3xl font-bold text-blue-400">{combinedItems.length}</div><div className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1">Total Items</div></div>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full border-collapse">
          <thead>
            <tr className="bg-gray-700/50">
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 border-b border-gray-600 border-r border-gray-700 last:border-r-0">Preview</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 border-b border-gray-600 border-r border-gray-700 last:border-r-0">Type</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 border-b border-gray-600 border-r border-gray-700 last:border-r-0">Description / Text</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 border-b border-gray-600 border-r border-gray-700 last:border-r-0">Program TC In</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-gray-300 border-b border-gray-600">Program TC Out</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {combinedItems.map((item, index) => (
              <tr key={index} className={`${index % 2 === 0 ? 'bg-gray-800/40' : 'bg-gray-800/20'} hover:bg-gray-700/30 transition-colors`}>
                <td className="px-4 py-2 border-b border-gray-700/50 border-r border-gray-700 last:border-r-0 w-32">
                  <VideoThumbnail videoUrl={videoPreviewUrl} timecode={item.tcIn} className="w-24 h-16 rounded-md" />
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm border-b border-gray-700/50 border-r border-gray-700 last:border-r-0">
                  <span className={`inline-block px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium ${item.type === 'graphic' ? 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300' : 'bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-300'}`}>
                    {item.type === 'graphic' ? 'Graphic' : 'Lower Third'}
                  </span>
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-300 border-b border-gray-700/50 border-r border-gray-700 last:border-r-0">{item.description}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-center font-mono text-gray-400 border-b border-gray-700/50 border-r border-gray-700 last:border-r-0">{item.tcIn}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-center font-mono text-gray-400 border-b border-gray-700/50">{item.tcOut}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <div className="flex justify-center gap-4 pt-6 border-t border-gray-700/50"><button onClick={onReset} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 rounded-lg transition-colors font-medium">Analyze Another Video</button></div>
    </div>
  );
};
export default GraphicsResultView;

--- START OF FILE: hooks/useVerificationState.ts ---
import { useState, useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

interface CursorPosition {
    rowIndex: number;
    fieldId: string;
}

interface VerificationState<T> {
    data: T[];
    isDirty: boolean;
    cursor: CursorPosition | null;
    updateCell: (rowIndex: number, field: keyof T, value: any) => void;
    setCursor: (cursor: CursorPosition | null) => void;
    navigate: (direction: 'next' | 'prev' | 'up' | 'down') => void;
    save: () => void;
    reset: (newData: T[]) => void;
}

export function useVerificationState<T>(initialData: T[], onSave: (data: T[]) => void) {
    const [data, setData] = useState<T[]>(initialData);
    const [isDirty, setIsDirty] = useState(false);
    const [cursor, setCursor] = useState<CursorPosition | null>(null);

    // Track fields order for navigation
    const fieldsRef = useRef<string[]>([]);

    const registerFields = useCallback((fields: string[]) => {
        fieldsRef.current = fields;
    }, []);

    const updateCell = useCallback((rowIndex: number, field: keyof T, value: any) => {
        setData(prev => {
            const newData = [...prev];
            newData[rowIndex] = { ...newData[rowIndex], [field]: value };
            return newData;
        });
        setIsDirty(true);
    }, []);

    const save = useCallback(() => {
        onSave(data);
        setIsDirty(false);
    }, [data, onSave]);

    const reset = useCallback((newData: T[]) => {
        setData(newData);
        setIsDirty(false);
        setCursor(null);
    }, []);

    const navigate = useCallback((direction: 'next' | 'prev' | 'up' | 'down') => {
        if (!cursor) return;

        const { rowIndex, fieldId } = cursor;
        const fieldIndex = fieldsRef.current.indexOf(fieldId);
        if (fieldIndex === -1) return;

        let newRow = rowIndex;
        let newField = fieldIndex;

        switch (direction) {
            case 'next':
                if (fieldIndex < fieldsRef.current.length - 1) {
                    newField++;
                } else if (rowIndex < data.length - 1) {
                    newRow++;
                    newField = 0;
                }
                break;
            case 'prev':
                if (fieldIndex > 0) {
                    newField--;
                } else if (rowIndex > 0) {
                    newRow--;
                    newField = fieldsRef.current.length - 1;
                }
                break;
            case 'up':
                if (rowIndex > 0) newRow--;
                break;
            case 'down':
                if (rowIndex < data.length - 1) newRow++;
                break;
        }

        if (newRow !== rowIndex || newField !== fieldIndex) {
            setCursor({ rowIndex: newRow, fieldId: fieldsRef.current[newField] });
        }
    }, [cursor, data.length]);

    // Global Keyboard Shortcuts for Save
    useEffect(() => {
        const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === 's') {
                e.preventDefault();
                save();
            }
        };
        window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
        return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    }, [save]);

    return {
        data,
        isDirty,
        cursor,
        setCursor,
        updateCell,
        navigate,
        save,
        reset,
        registerFields
    };
}

--- END OF FILE: hooks/useVerificationState.ts ---

--- START OF FILE: workers/ffmpeg.worker.ts ---
import { FFmpeg } from '@ffmpeg/ffmpeg';
import { fetchFile, toBlobURL } from '@ffmpeg/util';

const ffmpeg = new FFmpeg();

let loaded = false;

// Initialize FFmpeg singleton
async function loadFFmpeg() {
    if (loaded) return;
    try {
        const baseURL = 'https://unpkg.com/@ffmpeg/core@0.12.6/dist/esm';
        await ffmpeg.load({
            coreURL: await toBlobURL(`${baseURL}/ffmpeg-core.js`, 'text/javascript'),
            wasmURL: await toBlobURL(`${baseURL}/ffmpeg-core.wasm`, 'application/wasm'),
        });
        loaded = true;
        self.postMessage({ type: 'status', message: 'FFmpeg loaded' });
    } catch (error) {
        self.postMessage({ type: 'error', message: 'Failed to load FFmpeg: ' + error });
        throw error;
    }
}

self.onmessage = async ({ data }) => {
    const { type, videoFile } = data;

    try {
        await loadFFmpeg();

        if (!videoFile) return;

        const { name } = videoFile;
        self.postMessage({ type: 'status', message: 'Writing file to memory...' });
        await ffmpeg.writeFile(name, await fetchFile(videoFile));

        if (type === 'DETECT_SCENES') {
            const { threshold = 0.4 } = data;
            self.postMessage({ type: 'status', message: 'Analyzing scenes...' });

            const logData: string[] = [];
            const logHandler = ({ message }: { message: string }) => {
                if (message.includes('pts_time:')) {
                    logData.push(message);
                }
            };
            ffmpeg.on('log', logHandler);

            await ffmpeg.exec([
                '-i', name,
                '-vf', `select='gt(scene,${threshold})',showinfo`,
                '-f', 'null',
                '-'
            ]);

            ffmpeg.off('log', logHandler);

            const scenes = logData.map(log => {
                const match = log.match(/pts_time:([0-9.]+)/);
                return match ? parseFloat(match[1]) : null;
            }).filter(t => t !== null) as number[];

            self.postMessage({ type: 'result', payload: { scenes } });

        } else if (type === 'EXTRACT_KEYFRAMES') {
            self.postMessage({ type: 'status', message: 'Extracting I-frames...' });

            // Ensure output directory exists or just use root
            // We will output pattern: frame_%03d.jpg

            await ffmpeg.exec([
                '-i', name,
                '-vf', "select='eq(pict_type,I)'",
                '-vsync', 'vfr',
                'frame_%03d.jpg'
            ]);

            // Read all generated files
            const files = await ffmpeg.listDir('/');
            const frameFiles = files.filter((f: any) => f.name.startsWith('frame_') && f.name.endsWith('.jpg'));

            const blobs: Blob[] = [];

            for (const file of frameFiles) {
                const data = await ffmpeg.readFile(file.name);
                // FFmpeg 0.12+ uses SharedArrayBuffer which confuses standard Blob types
                blobs.push(new Blob([data as any], { type: 'image/jpeg' }));
                // Cleanup each frame immediately to save memory? No, maybe after.
                await ffmpeg.deleteFile(file.name);
            }

            self.postMessage({ type: 'result', payload: { blobs } });

        } else if (type === 'GET_METADATA') {
            self.postMessage({ type: 'status', message: 'Extracting metadata...' });

            const logData: string[] = [];
            const logHandler = ({ message }: { message: string }) => {
                logData.push(message);
            };
            ffmpeg.on('log', logHandler);

            // Run ffmpeg -i just to get the info (it will error because no output is specified, which is fine)
            try {
                await ffmpeg.exec(['-i', name]);
            } catch (e) {
                // Expected error: "At least one output file must be specified"
                // We ignore this as we only want the startup logs (metadata)
            }

            ffmpeg.off('log', logHandler);

            // Parse the logs to find relevant info
            const logs = logData.join('\n');

            // Simple regex extractors for the "Law of Physics"
            // Input #0, mov,mp4,m4a,3gp,3g2,mj2, from 'video.mp4':
            // Duration: 00:00:13.54, start: 0.000000, bitrate: 4405 kb/s

            const containerMatch = logs.match(/Input #0, ([^,]+)/);
            const durationMatch = logs.match(/Duration: ([^,]+)/);
            const bitrateMatch = logs.match(/bitrate: ([^ ]+)/);
            const streams = logs.split('Stream #').slice(1).map(s => 'Stream #' + s.split('\n')[0].trim());

            const metadata = {
                container: containerMatch ? containerMatch[1] : 'unknown',
                duration: durationMatch ? durationMatch[1] : 'unknown',
                bitrate: bitrateMatch ? bitrateMatch[1] + ' kb/s' : 'unknown',
                streams: streams,
                rawLogs: logs // For full transparency in the UI
            };

            self.postMessage({ type: 'result', payload: metadata });
        }

        // Cleanup input file
        await ffmpeg.deleteFile(name);

    } catch (error: any) {
        self.postMessage({ type: 'error', message: error.message || String(error) });
    }
};

import { ERROR_IDS } from '../types';

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TYPES: Google Cloud Video Intelligence API
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export interface VideoAnnotationResults {
    inputUri: string;
    segment: {
        startTimeOffset: string;
        endTimeOffset: string;
    };
    shotLabelAnnotations?: LabelAnnotation[];
    shotAnnotations?: VideoSegment[];
    speechTranscriptions?: SpeechTranscription[];
    error?: {
        code: number;
        message: string;
    };
}

export interface VideoSegment {
    startTimeOffset: string;
    endTimeOffset: string;
}

export interface LabelAnnotation {
    entity: {
        entityId: string;
        description: string;
        languageCode: string;
    };
    categoryEntities?: {
        entityId: string;
        description: string;
        languageCode: string;
    }[];
    segments: {
        segment: VideoSegment;
        confidence: number;
    }[];
}

export interface SpeechTranscription {
    alternatives: {
        transcript: string;
        confidence: number;
        words: {
            startTime: string;
            endTime: string;
            word: string;
        }[];
    }[];
    languageCode: string;
}

export interface AnnotateVideoRequest {
    inputUri: string;
    features: Feature[];
    videoContext?: {
        speechTranscriptionConfig?: {
            languageCode: string;
            enableAutomaticPunctuation: boolean;
            enableSpeakerDiarization?: boolean;
            diarizationSpeakerCount?: number;
        };
        shotChangeDetectionConfig?: {
            model: string;
        };
    };
    outputUri?: string; // Optional: Write JSON directly to GCS
}

export type Feature =
    | 'LABEL_DETECTION'
    | 'SHOT_CHANGE_DETECTION'
    | 'EXPLICIT_CONTENT_DETECTION'
    | 'SPEECH_TRANSCRIPTION'
    | 'TEXT_DETECTION'
    | 'OBJECT_TRACKING'
    | 'LOGO_RECOGNITION';

export interface Operation {
    name: string;
    metadata: {
        '@type': string;
        annotationProgress: {
            inputUri: string;
            progressPercent: number;
            startTime: string;
            updateTime: string;
        }[];
    };
    done: boolean;
    error?: {
        code: number;
        message: string;
    };
    response?: {
        '@type': string;
        annotationResults: VideoAnnotationResults[];
    };
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SERVICE IMPLEMENTATION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const API_ENDPOINT = 'https://videointelligence.googleapis.com/v1/videos:annotate';

/**
 * Perform deterministic video analysis using Google Cloud Video Intelligence API
 * Requires: Valid API Key with Video Intelligence API enabled
 * Note: inputUri must be a 'gs://' Google Cloud Storage URI
 */
export async function annotateVideo(
    apiKey: string,
    gcsUri: string,
    features: Feature[],
    onProgress: (progress: number) => void,
    signal?: AbortSignal,
    accessToken?: string,
    projectId?: string
): Promise<VideoAnnotationResults> {

    // 1. Start the Long Running Operation (LRO)
    const requestBody: AnnotateVideoRequest = {
        inputUri: gcsUri,
        features: features,
        videoContext: {
            speechTranscriptionConfig: {
                languageCode: 'en-US',
                enableAutomaticPunctuation: true,
                enableSpeakerDiarization: true,
            },
            shotChangeDetectionConfig: {
                model: 'builtin/stable',
            }
        }
    };

    let operation: Operation;

    try {
        const headers: HeadersInit = {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        };
        if (accessToken) {
            headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${accessToken}`;
        }
        if (projectId) {
            headers['X-Goog-User-Project'] = projectId;
        }

        const url = accessToken ? API_ENDPOINT : `${API_ENDPOINT}?key=${apiKey}`;

        const response = await fetch(url, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers,
            body: JSON.stringify(requestBody),
            signal
        });

        if (!response.ok) {
            const errorText = await response.text();
            throw new Error(`Failed to start Video Intelligence analysis: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`);
        }

        operation = await response.json();
        console.log('Video Intelligence LRO started:', operation.name);

    } catch (error) {
        if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
        throw error;
    }

    // 2. Poll for completion
    return pollForOperationCompletion(apiKey, operation.name, onProgress, signal, accessToken, projectId);
}

async function pollForOperationCompletion(
    apiKey: string,
    operationName: string,
    onProgress: (progress: number) => void,
    signal?: AbortSignal,
    accessToken?: string,
    projectId?: string
): Promise<VideoAnnotationResults> {

    const pollInterval = 1000; // 1 second for smoother updates

    while (true) {
        if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

        try {
            const headers: HeadersInit = {};
            if (accessToken) {
                headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${accessToken}`;
            }
            if (projectId) {
                headers['X-Goog-User-Project'] = projectId;
            }

            const url = accessToken
                ? `https://videointelligence.googleapis.com/v1/${operationName}`
                : `https://videointelligence.googleapis.com/v1/${operationName}?key=${apiKey}`;

            const response = await fetch(url, { headers });
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(`Polling failed: ${response.status}`);
            }

            const opStatus: Operation = await response.json();

            if (opStatus.error) {
                throw new Error(`Analysis failed: ${opStatus.error.message}`);
            }

            // Update progress if available
            if (opStatus.metadata?.annotationProgress?.[0]) {
                const progress = opStatus.metadata.annotationProgress[0].progressPercent;
                if (progress) onProgress(progress);
            }

            if (opStatus.done) {
                // Operation complete!
                // The results are typically inside opStatus.response.annotationResults
                if (opStatus.response?.annotationResults?.[0]) {
                    return opStatus.response.annotationResults[0];
                } else {
                    throw new Error('Operation completed but returned no results.');
                }
            }

            // Wait before next poll
            await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, pollInterval));

        } catch (error) {
            if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
            throw error;
        }
    }
}

--- START OF FILE: services/backendApiService.ts ---

import { JobDocument, FullAnalysisResult } from '../types';

const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'https://gemini-video-backend-193471035724.europe-west3.run.app';

export class BackendApiService {
    /**
     * Creates a new analysis job.
     * @param gcsUri The GCS URI of the video file (must be gs://...).
     * @param mimeType The mime type (e.g., video/mp4).
     * @param duration Optional duration in seconds.
     */
    static async createJob(gcsUri: string, mimeType: string, duration: number = 0): Promise<string> {
        console.log(`[BackendAPI] Creating job for ${gcsUri}`);
        const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/jobs`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                gcsUri,
                mimeType,
                duration,
                analysisConfig: {} // Config for all modules enabled by default
            })
        });

        if (!response.ok) {
            const errorText = await response.text();
            throw new Error(`Failed to create job: ${response.status} ${errorText}`);
        }

        const data = await response.json();
        return data.id;
    }

    /**
     * Fetches the current status of a job.
     */
    static async getJob(jobId: string): Promise<JobDocument> {
        const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/jobs/${jobId}`);
        if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(`Failed to fetch job: ${response.status}`);
        }
        return await response.json();
    }

    /**
     * Polls the job status until it is completed or failed.
     * @param jobId The ID of the job to monitor.
     * @param onProgress Callback for progress updates.
     * @param signal AbortSignal to cancel polling.
     */
    static async pollJob(
        jobId: string,
        onProgress: (status: string, percentage: number) => void,
        signal?: AbortSignal
    ): Promise<FullAnalysisResult> {
        let attempts = 0;
        const maxAttempts = 360; // 30 minutes
        const delay = 5000;

        while (attempts < maxAttempts) {
            if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Operation cancelled');

            const job = await this.getJob(jobId);

            // Update Progress
            const stage = job.progress?.currentStage || 'Processing...';
            const percent = job.progress?.overall || 0;
            onProgress(stage, percent);

            if (job.status === 'completed') {
                if (!job.results) throw new Error('Job completed but results are missing.');
                return job.results as unknown as FullAnalysisResult;
            }

            if (job.status === 'failed') {
                throw new Error(job.error?.message || 'Job failed during processing');
            }

            attempts++;
            await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, delay));
        }

        throw new Error('Analysis timed out');
    }
}

import { ffmpegService } from './ffmpegService';
import { embeddingService } from './embeddingService';
import { modelRouter } from './llm/ModelRouter';
import { VideoFrameExtractor } from '../utils/videoFrameExtractor';
import { retryWithBackoff } from '../utils/retryUtils';

// Helper to convert Blob to Base64
const blobToBase64 = (blob: Blob): Promise<string> => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onloadend = () => resolve(reader.result as string);
        reader.onerror = reject;
        reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
    });
};

// We need a way to call Gemini with a Blob image easily.
// I'll add a helper inside IndexingService to call ModelRouter.generateText with multimedia parts?
// `LLMProvider` interface has `generateText(prompt)`. It doesn't strictly support images in that signature.
// `analyzeVideo` is for video files.
// Implementation Detail: I will cast the Provider to specialized or just instantiate generic logic here?
// Better: Add `analyzeImage` to `LLMProvider`? 
// No, I'll stick to a focused implementation here that uses `ModelRouter` but constructs the request manually 
// or I update `ModelRouter` to be more flexible. 
// Let's update `ModelRouter` to support `generateContent(parts[])`? 
// That's too raw. 
// I will implement `describeImage` inside `IndexingService` by using `ModelRouter.getProvider('gemini')` 
// and accessing its internal `genAI` if I made it public? No, it's private.
// Okay, I will add `describeImage` to `LLMProvider` interface. It's a common task.

export class IndexingService {

    async indexVideo(file: File, onProgress: (msg: string, progress: number) => void) {
        onProgress('Detecting Scenes...', 10);

        // 1. Detect Scenes (WASM)
        // Ensure threshold is sensible (e.g., 0.3)
        const { scenes } = await ffmpegService.detectScenes(file, 0.3, (msg) => console.log(msg));

        if (scenes.length === 0) {
            // Fallback: If no scenes, just take one every 60s
            // or just start/middle/end
            scenes.push(file.size > 0 ? 0 : 0);
        }

        onProgress(`Found ${scenes.length} scenes. Extracting thumbnails...`, 30);

        // 2. Select Mid-Points (timestamp + 1s to avoid the fade/cut itself)
        const timestamps = scenes.map(s => s + 1.0);

        // 3. Extract Frames (Browser Canvas)
        const thumbnails = await VideoFrameExtractor.extractFrames(file, timestamps);

        onProgress('Analyzing scenes with AI...', 50);

        // 4. Describe & Embed
        const documents: any[] = [];
        let completed = 0;

        // Process in batches
        const BATCH_SIZE = 3;
        for (let i = 0; i < thumbnails.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) {
            const batch = thumbnails.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE);
            const batchIndices = Array.from({ length: batch.length }, (_, k) => i + k);

            await Promise.all(batch.map(async (blob, idx) => {
                const realIdx = batchIndices[idx];
                const _timestamp = timestamps[realIdx];
                const sceneTime = scenes[realIdx];

                try {
                    // Convert Blob to Base64 for Gemini Inline
                    const base64 = await new Promise<string>((resolve) => {
                        const reader = new FileReader();
                        reader.onloadend = () => resolve(reader.result as string);
                        reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
                    });
                    const cleanBase64 = base64.split(',')[1];

                    // Direct call to Gemini Flash for speed/cost (Using specific helper to bypass router if needed, 
                    // but ideally we route. Since Router doesn't have Image support yet, I'll effectively "Hack" it 
                    // or just use `generateJSON` with complex input if I updated the interface.
                    // For MVP speed: I will instantiate a temporary Gemini instance here or import the generic one?
                    // I will simply use the existing `uploadFileToGemini` for small files? No.
                    // I'll add `analyzeImage` to `LLMProvider` in next step. For now assume it exists.

                    const description = await modelRouter.analyzeImage(cleanBase64, "image/jpeg",
                        "Describe this scene in one detailed sentence. Focus on visual action, objects, and mood.");

                    documents.push({
                        id: `scene_${realIdx}`,
                        text: description,
                        metadata: {
                            timestamp: sceneTime,
                            thumbnail: base64 // Store small thumb for UI
                        }
                    });

                } catch (e) {
                    console.error(`Failed to analyze scene ${realIdx}`, e);
                }
            }));

            completed += batch.length;
            onProgress(`Analyzed ${completed}/${scenes.length} scenes...`, 50 + (completed / scenes.length) * 30);
        }

        onProgress('Generating Embeddings...', 90);

        // 5. Store in Vector DB
        await embeddingService.addDocumentsBatch(documents);

        onProgress('Indexing Complete!', 100);
    }
}

export const indexingService = new IndexingService();
