import {
    annotateVideo,
    VideoAnnotationResults,
    Feature
} from './googleVideoIntelligenceService';
import { uploadFileToGCS } from './gcsUploadService';
import { enrichShotList, generateShotList, transcribeMedia, analyzeVideoContent, generateSynopsisFromAnalysis, generateSynopsisFromText, generateBio, extractTalentFromVideo, analyzeGraphicsAndLowerThirds, analyzeVideoForSpecies, createContextCache } from './geminiService';
import { smartUploadToGemini } from './uploadService';
import { AnalysisMode, Shot, UploadProgress, SynopsisConfig, BioConfig, GraphicsConfig, FaunaConfig, ShotListConfig, VideoMetadata, DEFAULT_SHOTLIST_CONFIG, DEFAULT_SYNOPSIS_CONFIG } from '../types';
import { secondsToTimecode, secondsToDropFrameTimecode, detectTimecodeFormat } from '../utils/tableUtils';
import { BackendApiService } from './backendApiService';

// Types for the coordinated result
export interface CoordinatedAnalysisResult {
    shots?: Omit<Shot, 'nr' | 'thumbnail'>[];
    transcription?: any; // To be defined
    metadata?: any;
    rawApiResult?: VideoAnnotationResults;
}

interface AnalysisOptions {
    file: File;
    fileUri?: string;
    mode: AnalysisMode;
    apiKey: string;
    accessToken?: string; // OAuth Token
    projectId?: string; // Quota Project ID
    bucketName?: string;
    useProMode?: boolean; // Toggle for Zero Hallucination (true) or Standard (false)
    tokenIsValid?: boolean; // Gatekeeper: prevent backend usage if token is expired
    onProgress: (message: string, percentage: number) => void;
    onMultiProgress?: (mode: AnalysisMode, message: string, percentage: number) => void; // New: For parallel job updates
    signal?: AbortSignal;
    synopsisConfig?: SynopsisConfig;
    bioConfig?: BioConfig;
    graphicsConfig?: GraphicsConfig;
    faunaConfig?: FaunaConfig;
    shotListConfig?: ShotListConfig;
    videoMetadata?: VideoMetadata | null;
}

/**
 * Orchestrates the analysis workflow based on the selected mode.
 * - For Deterministic tasks (Shots, Transcription), uses Video Intelligence API (requires GCS).
 * - For Creative tasks (Synopsis, Bio, Fauna), uses Gemini API (can use File API).
 */
export async function performAnalysis(options: AnalysisOptions): Promise<any> {
    const { file, fileUri, mode, apiKey, accessToken, projectId, bucketName, tokenIsValid, onProgress, onMultiProgress, signal, videoMetadata } = options;

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // STRATEGY: CLOUD RUN BACKEND (Full Analysis)
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // We prefer the backend if:
    // 1. Mode is 'full_analysis' (Backend is optimized for this)
    // 2. We have GCS Access (Backend requires GCS URI)
    // 3. The Auth Token is VALID (Strict check to prevent 401 hangs)
    const canUseBackend = mode === 'full_analysis' && accessToken && bucketName && tokenIsValid;

    if (canUseBackend) {
        try {
            console.log("[Coordinator] Attempting Cloud Run Backend...");
            let gcsUri = fileUri;

            // 1. Ensure File is in GCS
            if (!gcsUri || !gcsUri.startsWith('gs://')) {
                // BUG FIX: Recent Uploads (Mock Files with size 0) cannot be re-uploaded to GCS.
                // We must perform this check BEFORE attempting upload.
                if (file.size === 0) {
                    console.warn("[Coordinator] Backend skipped: Cannot upload 'Restored File' to GCS (File content unavailable). Falling back to Client-Side.");
                    throw new Error("Cannot upload Virtual File (0 bytes) to Backend.");
                }

                console.log("[Coordinator] Backend path: ensuring GCS upload...");
                onProgress('Uploading to Cloud Storage (Zero Hallucination)...', 0);
                gcsUri = await uploadFileToGCS({
                    file,
                    bucketName: bucketName!,
                    apiKey,
                    accessToken: accessToken!,
                    onProgress: (p: UploadProgress) => {
                        onProgress(p.message, p.percentage * 0.2); // First 20% is upload
                    },
                    signal
                });
            } else {
                onProgress('File already in Cloud Storage...', 20);
            }

            // 2. Submit Job
            onProgress('Dispatching to Backend Workers...', 25);
            const jobId = await BackendApiService.createJob(gcsUri, file.type, videoMetadata?.duration);
            console.log(`[Coordinator] Job Created: ${jobId}`);

            // 3. Poll for Completion using backend service
            // 3. Poll for Completion using backend service
            // Robust Polling Plan with Safety Guards:
            // - Exponential Backoff: Start 2s, max 10s.
            // - Visibility Awareness: If hidden, poll slower (every 30s) or pause.
            // - Jitter: Randomize interval slightly.
            // - Timeouts: Max 90 mins total, Max 20 consecutive network failures.

            let attempt = 0;
            let consecutiveErrors = 0;
            const startTime = Date.now();

            const MAX_BACKOFF = 10000;
            const BASE_INTERVAL = 2000;
            const JOB_TIMEOUT = 90 * 60 * 1000; // 90 Minutes (Cloud Run Gen 2 limit is 60m, giving buffer)
            const MAX_CONSECUTIVE_ERRORS = 20; // If network fails 20 times in a row, give up.

            while (true) {
                if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Operation Cancelled');

                // 1. Global Timeout Check
                if (Date.now() - startTime > JOB_TIMEOUT) {
                    throw new Error("Analysis timed out (90 minutes limit reached). The job may still be running on the backend, but the frontend has stopped monitoring.");
                }

                // 2. Offline Guard (Laptop Sleep / Disconnect Handling)
                // You are right: If the job is in the cloud, we shouldn't fail just because the laptop slept.
                // If we are offline, we PAUSE polling (don't error out) and wait for the user to return.
                if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine) {
                    console.log("[Coordinator] Offline detected (Laptop sleep?). Pausing poll...");
                    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 5000));
                    continue; // Skip this iteration, don't increment failure stats
                }

                // 3. Visibility Check (Browser Tab Throttling Mitigation)
                const isHidden = typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.hidden;

                try {
                    const job = await BackendApiService.getJob(jobId);

                    // Acknowledge "still alive" - Reset error counters
                    attempt = 0;
                    consecutiveErrors = 0;

                    // Map Module Progress to UI
                    if (job.progress?.modules) {
                        const m = job.progress.modules;
                        if (onMultiProgress) {
                            if (m.fauna) onMultiProgress('fauna', m.fauna.status === 'completed' ? 'Done' : m.fauna.status, m.fauna.progress);
                            if (m.transcription) onMultiProgress('transcribe', m.transcription.status === 'completed' ? 'Done' : 'Processing', m.transcription.progress);
                            if (m.shotList) onMultiProgress('shotlist', m.shotList.status === 'completed' ? 'Done' : 'Processing', m.shotList.progress);
                            if (m.synopsis) onMultiProgress('synopsis', m.synopsis.status === 'completed' ? 'Done' : 'Processing', m.synopsis.progress);
                            if (m.graphics) onMultiProgress('graphics', m.graphics.status === 'completed' ? 'Done' : 'Processing', m.graphics.progress);
                            if (m.cast) onMultiProgress('bio', m.cast.status === 'completed' ? 'Done' : 'Processing', m.cast.progress);
                        }
                    }

                    // Update Overall Progress
                    // Map 0-100 backend progress to 25-100 frontend progress
                    const overall = 25 + ((job.progress?.overall || 0) * 0.75);
                    onProgress(job.progress?.currentStage || 'Processing on Cloud Run...', overall);

                    if (job.status === 'completed') {
                        if (!job.results && !job.hasSubcollections) throw new Error("Job completed but results are missing");
                        return job.results || {};
                    }

                    if (job.status === 'failed') {
                        throw new Error(job.error?.message || 'Backend processing failed');
                    }
                } catch (pollError: any) {
                    consecutiveErrors++;
                    console.warn(`[Coordinator] Poll failed (Attempt ${consecutiveErrors}/${MAX_CONSECUTIVE_ERRORS}): ${pollError.message}`);

                    // Terminal Errors: Don't retry
                    if (pollError.message.includes('404') || pollError.message.includes('Not Found')) {
                        throw new Error(`Job ID ${jobId} not found on server. It may have expired or was never created.`);
                    }

                    // Consecutive Failure Limit
                    if (consecutiveErrors >= MAX_CONSECUTIVE_ERRORS) {
                        throw new Error(`Connection lost. Failed to contact backend ${MAX_CONSECUTIVE_ERRORS} times in a row. Please check your internet connection.`);
                    }
                }

                // Adaptive Backoff
                // If hidden, poll rare (saving battery/resources and avoiding timeout buildup)
                // If we have errors, backoff AGGRESSIVELY to avoid spamming (up to 30s)
                let targetInterval = isHidden ? 30000 : Math.min(BASE_INTERVAL * Math.pow(1.5, attempt++), MAX_BACKOFF);

                // If we are in error state, wait longer
                if (consecutiveErrors > 0) targetInterval = Math.max(targetInterval, 5000);

                const jitter = Math.random() * 1000;
                await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, targetInterval + jitter));
            }

        } catch (backendError: any) {
            console.warn("[Coordinator] Backend Analysis failed. Falling back to Client-Side mode.", backendError);

            // Explicitly classify the error for debugging
            if (backendError?.message?.includes('401') || backendError?.message?.includes('403')) {
                console.warn("[Coordinator] Auth Error detected. Disabling Pro Mode for this session.");
            }

            onProgress(`Backend unavailable (${backendError?.message || 'Unknown'}). Switching to local mode...`, 10);
            options.useProMode = false;
        }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // PROGRESS SMOOTHER
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    const smoothProgress = {
        current: 0,
        target: 0,
        timer: null as any,
        callback: onProgress,

        set(target: number, message?: string) {
            this.target = target;
            if (message) this.currentMessage = message;
            this.startTicker();
        },

        currentMessage: '',

        startTicker() {
            if (this.timer) return;
            this.timer = setInterval(() => {
                if (Math.abs(this.current - this.target) < 0.5) {
                    this.current = this.target;
                    this.callback(this.currentMessage, this.current);
                    clearInterval(this.timer);
                    this.timer = null;
                } else {
                    // Ease towards target (10% of remaining distance)
                    const step = (this.target - this.current) * 0.1;
                    // Ensure minimum movement of 0.1 to avoid Zeno's paradox infinite stall
                    this.current += step > 0 ? Math.max(step, 0.5) : Math.min(step, -0.5);
                    // Cap at target
                    if ((step > 0 && this.current > this.target) || (step < 0 && this.current < this.target)) {
                        this.current = this.target;
                    }
                    this.callback(this.currentMessage, this.current);
                }
            }, 50); // 20fps updates
        },

        stop() {
            if (this.timer) {
                clearInterval(this.timer);
                this.timer = null;
            }
        },

        // Immediate set without smoothing (for resets or 100%)
        setImmediate(target: number, message: string) {
            this.target = target;
            this.current = target;
            this.currentMessage = message;
            this.callback(message, target);
        }
    };

    // Use smoothProgress.set() instead of direct onProgress()
    // Helper to replace direct calls
    const updateProgress = (msg: string, pct: number) => smoothProgress.set(pct, msg);

    // Default to 24fps if metadata is unavailable
    const frameRate = videoMetadata?.frameRate || 24;

    // 1. Determine Strategy
    // Only use Video Intelligence if explicitly requested via Pro Mode AND supported mode (Shot List only now)
    // IMPORTANT: If we fell back from Backend, options.useProMode might have been disabled above.
    let needsVideoIntelligence = options.useProMode && (mode === 'shotlist' || mode === 'full_analysis');

    // 2. Logic Check: CAN we use Video Intelligence?
    // If this is a "Recent Upload" (Dummy File with size 0) and the URI is NOT already a GCS URI,
    // we cannot upload it to GCS. Thus, we must disable Pro Mode for this run.
    const isGcsUri = fileUri?.startsWith('gs://');
    const isDummyFile = file.size === 0;

    if (needsVideoIntelligence && isDummyFile && !isGcsUri) {
        console.warn("Pro Mode (Video Intelligence) unavailable for Recent Uploads (Source file not present). Falling back to Gemini.");
        needsVideoIntelligence = false;
        // Notify user via progress updates? We might want to, but console warn is minimal safety.
        // We'll let the standard fallback logic assume Gemini.
    }

    let gcsUri = fileUri;

    // Try GCS Upload if needed
    if (needsVideoIntelligence) {
        // Variable isGcsUri is already declared above
        if (!isGcsUri) {
            try {
                if (!bucketName) throw new Error('Bucket not configured');

                updateProgress('Uploading to Google Cloud Storage (Zero Hallucination)...', 0);
                gcsUri = await uploadFileToGCS({
                    file,
                    bucketName,
                    apiKey,
                    accessToken,
                    onProgress: (p: UploadProgress) => {
                        updateProgress(p.message, p.percentage * 0.3);
                    },
                    signal
                });
            } catch (error: any) {
                // Check if this is a Scope/Permission error (403)
                // If so, DO NOT fallback, because the user needs to re-auth.
                const isScopeError = error?.message?.includes('ACCESS_TOKEN_SCOPE_INSUFFICIENT') ||
                    error?.message?.includes('insufficient authentication scopes') ||
                    JSON.stringify(error).includes('PERMISSION_DENIED');

                if (isScopeError) {
                    console.error("Pro Mode Auth Error:", error);
                    throw new Error("Authentication Error: Missing 'Cloud Platform' scope. Please Sign Out and Sign In again to upgrade your permissions.");
                }

                console.warn("Zero Hallucination (GCS) unavailable. Seamlessly falling back to Standard AI Analysis.");
                updateProgress("Pro Mode Unavailable (Access Denied). Switching to Standard Mode...", 5);
                needsVideoIntelligence = false; // Fallback to Gemini 1.5 Pro
            }
        } else {
            updateProgress('File already in GCS. Starting analysis...', 35);
        }
    }

    // 3. OPTIONAL: Video Intelligence API (Structure/Skeleton)
    let apiResult: any = null;
    if (needsVideoIntelligence && gcsUri) {
        // Don't reset to 35 if we are already there, just start from there
        if (smoothProgress.current < 35) smoothProgress.setImmediate(35, 'Analyzing video structure (Deterministic)...');
        else updateProgress('Analyzing video structure (Deterministic)...', 35);

        let features: Feature[] = [];
        if (mode === 'shotlist' || mode === 'full_analysis') features.push('SHOT_CHANGE_DETECTION');
        if (mode === 'full_analysis') {
            features.push('LABEL_DETECTION');
            features.push('TEXT_DETECTION');
        }

        try {
            apiResult = await annotateVideo(
                apiKey,
                gcsUri,
                features,
                (progressPercent) => {
                    const overall = 35 + (progressPercent * 0.45);
                    updateProgress(`Video Intelligence API: ${Math.round(progressPercent)}%`, overall);
                },
                signal,
                accessToken,
                projectId
            );
        } catch (error) {
            console.warn("Video Intelligence Analysis failed (likely Auth 401), proceeding with Gemini Fallback:", error);
            onMultiProgress?.('shotlist', 'Shot Detection Skipped (VI unavailable)', 0);
        }
    }

    // 4. REQUIRED: Gemini Files API (Creative Content)
    // Ensure we have a workable URI for Gemini (not gs://)
    // isGcsUri is already calculated at function start
    let geminiUri = isGcsUri ? undefined : fileUri;

    // We strictly use smartUploadToGemini for ALL files now (up to 20GB supported by API)
    // The uploadService handles chunking and reliability.
    const canUseGeminiVision = true;

    if ((!geminiUri || isGcsUri) && canUseGeminiVision) {
        updateProgress('Uploading to Gemini Files API (Visual Enrichment- this may take a while for large files)...', 45);

        try {
            geminiUri = await smartUploadToGemini({
                file,
                apiKey,
                onProgress: (p: UploadProgress) => updateProgress(p.message, 45 + (p.percentage * 0.1)),
                signal
            });
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn("Gemini Upload Failed. Proceeding in Text-Only Mode.", e);
            // Fallthrough: geminiUri will be undefined, triggering fallbacks below.
        }
    }

    const abortSignal = signal || new AbortController().signal;

    // 5. EXECUTION (Unified)
    if (mode === 'full_analysis') {
        // COST OPTIMIZATION: Context Caching "Smart Check"
        let contextCacheName: string | undefined = undefined;
        // DISABLED: Context caching caused "blind model" hallucinations.
        // Reverting to standard non-cached flow for reliability.
        const isLargeFile = false; // file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024;
        const isLongDuration = false; // (videoMetadata?.duration || 0) > 60;

        if (isLargeFile || isLongDuration) {
            updateProgress('Creating intelligent context cache (Cost Saving)...', 60);
            contextCacheName = await createContextCache(geminiUri!, file.type, 600);
            if (contextCacheName) {
                onMultiProgress?.('full_analysis' as AnalysisMode, 'Cache Active: ~80% Cost Reduction', 0);
            }
        }

        updateProgress('Running comprehensive analysis mostly in parallel...', 65);

        // 1. Transcription (Gemini Flash - Fast & Cheap)
        // 1. Transcription (Gemini Flash - Fast & Cheap) OR Video Intel (Ground Truth)
        const transcriptionPromise = (async () => {
            // OPTION A: Use Video Intelligence Result if available (Pro Mode)
            if (apiResult?.speechTranscriptions && apiResult.speechTranscriptions.length > 0) {
                onMultiProgress?.('transcribe', 'Using Pro Mode Transcript...', 100);
                // Convert VI format to App format
                const text = apiResult.speechTranscriptions
                    .map((st: any) => st.alternatives?.[0]?.transcript || '')
                    .join(' ');

                // TODO: Map words for speaker segments if needed. For now, simple text.
                return { segments: [], speakerMap: {}, text };
            }

            // OPTION B: Gemini Flash (Standard Mode)
            if (geminiUri) {
                try {
                    return await transcribeMedia(geminiUri!, file.type, abortSignal, (msg, pct) => onMultiProgress?.('transcribe', msg, pct), contextCacheName);
                } catch (e) {
                    console.warn('Transcription failed', e);
                    onMultiProgress?.('transcribe', 'Transcription failed', 0);
                    return { segments: [], speakerMap: {}, text: '' };
                }
            }

            return { segments: [], speakerMap: {}, text: '' };
        })();

        // 2. Shot List (VI + Enrichment OR Gemini Fallback)
        const shotsPromise = (async () => {
            // Priority 1: Use Video Intelligence (Pro Mode) if available
            if (apiResult?.shotAnnotations) {
                const enrichmentUri = geminiUri || "SKIP_ENRICHMENT";
                if (enrichmentUri === "SKIP_ENRICHMENT") {
                    onMultiProgress?.('shotlist', 'Visual enrichment skipped (Video > 2GB or Upload Failed)', 100);
                }
                return processShotList(apiResult, enrichmentUri, file.type, 'English', frameRate, signal, (msg, pct) => {
                    onMultiProgress?.('shotlist', msg, pct);
                }, contextCacheName);
            }

            // Priority 2: Fallback to Gemini-only Shotlist (Standard Mode)
            onMultiProgress?.('shotlist', 'Generating Shot List (Standard Mode)...', 10);
            try {
                if (!geminiUri) throw new Error("No video URI available (Upload failed or File too large)");
                const fullConfig = options.shotListConfig || { ...DEFAULT_SHOTLIST_CONFIG };
                return await generateShotList(
                    geminiUri!,
                    file.type,
                    fullConfig.language || 'English',
                    frameRate,
                    abortSignal,
                    (msg, pct) => onMultiProgress?.('shotlist', msg, pct),
                    contextCacheName
                );
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('[GEMINI_DEBUG] Gemini Shotlist Fallback failed with error:', e);
                const errorMessage = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
                onMultiProgress?.('shotlist', `Shot List Failed: ${errorMessage}`, 0);
                return [];
            }
        })();

        // 3. Synopsis (Creative - Gemini)
        // 3. Synopsis (Creative - Gemini)
        const synopsisPromise = (async () => {
            try {
                onMultiProgress?.('synopsis', 'Generating synopsis...', 10);
                const config: SynopsisConfig = options.synopsisConfig || DEFAULT_SYNOPSIS_CONFIG;

                // STRATEGY: Visual Analysis OR Text Fallback
                let analysis: any = null;

                if (geminiUri) {
                    // Standard Mode: Use Vision
                    analysis = await analyzeVideoContent(geminiUri!, file.type, abortSignal, (msg, pct) => onMultiProgress?.('synopsis', msg, pct * 0.5), contextCacheName);
                } else {
                    // Large File Mode / Fallback: Use Transcript
                    onMultiProgress?.('synopsis', 'Waiting for transcript (Large File Mode)...', 20);
                    const transcriptResult = await transcriptionPromise; // Wait for parallel task
                    if (transcriptResult.text && transcriptResult.text.length > 50) {
                        onMultiProgress?.('synopsis', 'Generating from transcript...', 50);
                        return generateSynopsisFromText(transcriptResult.text, config);
                    } else {
                        throw new Error("No video or transcript available for synopsis.");
                    }
                }

                onMultiProgress?.('synopsis', 'Writing creative summary...', 60);
                const result = await generateSynopsisFromAnalysis(analysis, config.writer || '', config.director || '', 'English', '', abortSignal, config);

                onMultiProgress?.('synopsis', 'Synopsis complete', 100);
                return result;
            } catch (e) {
                const errorMessage = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Unknown error';
                console.warn("Synopsis failed:", errorMessage);
                onMultiProgress?.('synopsis', `Synopsis failed: ${errorMessage}`, 0);
                return {
                    title: 'Analysis Unavailable',
                    writtenBy: '',
                    directedBy: '',
                    oneLine: `Analysis failed: ${errorMessage}`,
                    paragraph: '',
                    fullSynopsis: '',
                    keyThemes: '',
                    targetAudience: ''
                };
            }
        })();

        // 4. Bio (Creative - Gemini)
        // 4. Bio (Creative - Gemini)
        const bioPromise = (async () => {
            if (!geminiUri) {
                onMultiProgress?.('bio', 'Bio skipped (Video > 2GB)', 100);
                return [];
            }
            try {
                onMultiProgress?.('bio', 'Scanning for talent...', 10);
                const names = await extractTalentFromVideo(geminiUri!, file.type);
                const topNames = names.slice(0, 3);
                onMultiProgress?.('bio', `Found ${topNames.length} names`, 30);

                const bioResults = [];
                for (let i = 0; i < topNames.length; i++) {
                    const name = topNames[i];
                    onMultiProgress?.('bio', `Researching ${name}...`, 30 + ((i / topNames.length) * 60));
                    bioResults.push(await generateBio(name, 'Actor/Subject', 'English'));
                }
                onMultiProgress?.('bio', 'Bio generation complete', 100);
                return bioResults;
            } catch (e) {
                onMultiProgress?.('bio', 'Bio analysis failed', 0);
                return [];
            }
        })();

        // 5. Fauna (Creative - Gemini)
        // 5. Fauna (Creative - Gemini)
        const faunaPromise = (async () => {
            if (!geminiUri) {
                onMultiProgress?.('fauna', 'Fauna skipped (Video > 2GB)', 100);
                return [];
            }
            try {
                onMultiProgress?.('fauna', 'Identifiying species...', 30);
                const result = await analyzeVideoForSpecies(geminiUri!, file.type, 'English', 'German', videoMetadata?.duration || 0);
                onMultiProgress?.('fauna', 'Fauna analysis complete', 100);
                return result;
            } catch (e) {
                onMultiProgress?.('fauna', 'Fauna analysis failed', 0);
                return [];
            }
        })();

        // 6. Graphics (Creative - Gemini)
        // 6. Graphics (Creative - Gemini)
        const graphicsPromise = (async () => {
            if (!geminiUri) {
                onMultiProgress?.('graphics', 'Graphics skipped (Video > 2GB)', 100);
                return { programTitle: '', runtime: '', graphics: [], lowerThirds: [] };
            }
            try {
                onMultiProgress?.('graphics', 'Reading on-screen text...', 30);
                const result = await analyzeGraphicsAndLowerThirds(geminiUri!, file.type, videoMetadata?.duration || 0, file.name);
                onMultiProgress?.('graphics', 'Graphics analysis complete', 100);
                return result;
            } catch (e) {
                onMultiProgress?.('graphics', 'Graphics analysis failed', 0);
                return { programTitle: '', runtime: '', graphics: [], lowerThirds: [] };
            }
        })();

        const [transcription, shots, synopsis, bio, fauna, graphics] = await Promise.all([
            transcriptionPromise,
            shotsPromise,
            synopsisPromise,
            bioPromise,
            faunaPromise,
            graphicsPromise
        ]);

        smoothProgress.stop(); // Stop ticker

        return {
            transcription,
            shotList: shots,
            graphics,
            synopsis,
            bio,
            fauna
        };

    } else if (mode === 'shotlist') {
        const fullConfig = options.shotListConfig || { ...DEFAULT_SHOTLIST_CONFIG };
        return generateShotList(
            geminiUri!,
            file.type,
            fullConfig.language || 'English',
            frameRate,
            abortSignal,
            (msg, pct) => updateProgress(msg, 50 + (pct * 0.5))
        );
    } else if (mode === 'transcribe') {
        return transcribeMedia(
            geminiUri!,
            file.type,
            abortSignal,
            (msg, pct) => updateProgress(msg, 50 + (pct * 0.5))
        );
    } else if (mode === 'synopsis') {
        const config = options.synopsisConfig!;
        const analysis = await analyzeVideoContent(geminiUri!, file.type, abortSignal, (msg, pct) => updateProgress(msg, 20 + pct * 0.4));
        updateProgress('Generating synopsis...', 80);
        return generateSynopsisFromAnalysis(analysis, config.writer || '', config.director || '', config.language || 'English', config.keywords || '', abortSignal, config);
    } else if (mode === 'bio') {
        const config = options.bioConfig!;
        if (config.autoDetect) {
            updateProgress('Scanning for cast names...', 30);
            const names = await extractTalentFromVideo(geminiUri!, file.type);
            const results = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
                const name = names[i];
                updateProgress(`Generating bio for ${name}...`, 50 + ((i / names.length) * 40));
                results.push(await generateBio(name, 'Actor/Subject in video', config.language || 'English'));
            }
            return results;
        } else {
            const results = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < (config.talentDetails || []).length; i++) {
                const t = config.talentDetails![i];
                updateProgress(`Generating bio for ${t.name}...`, 20 + ((i / config.talentDetails!.length) * 70));
                results.push(await generateBio(t.name || 'Unknown', t.context || '', config.language || 'English'));
            }
            return results;
        }
    } else if (mode === 'graphics') {
        const config = options.graphicsConfig!;
        return analyzeGraphicsAndLowerThirds(geminiUri!, file.type, config.videoDuration || 0, config.videoFileName || file.name);
    } else if (mode === 'fauna') {
        const config = options.faunaConfig || { primaryLanguage: 'English', secondaryLanguage: 'German' }; // Default
        return analyzeVideoForSpecies(geminiUri!, file.type, config.primaryLanguage, config.secondaryLanguage, videoMetadata?.duration || 0);
    }

    return null;
    return null;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RESULT PROCESSORS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function processShotList(
    result: VideoAnnotationResults,
    gcsUri: string,
    mimeType: string,
    language: string,
    frameRate: number,
    signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
    onProgress: (message: string, percentage: number) => void,
    cacheName?: string
): Promise<Omit<Shot, 'nr' | 'thumbnail'>[]> {

    const shotAnnotations = result.shotAnnotations || [];
    if (shotAnnotations.length === 0) return [];

    const isDropFrame = detectTimecodeFormat(frameRate) === 'DF';

    // 1. Create Deterministic Skeleton
    const deterministicShots: Omit<Shot, 'nr' | 'thumbnail'>[] = shotAnnotations.map(segment => {
        const startSeconds = parseOffset(segment.startTimeOffset);
        const endSeconds = parseOffset(segment.endTimeOffset);
        const durationSeconds = endSeconds - startSeconds;

        const formatTc = (s: number) => isDropFrame ? secondsToDropFrameTimecode(s, frameRate) : secondsToTimecode(s, frameRate);

        return {
            tcIn: formatTc(startSeconds),
            tcOut: formatTc(endSeconds),
            duration: formatTc(durationSeconds),
            description: "Analyzing...", // Placeholder
            action: "Reading...", // Placeholder
            audio: ""
        };
    });

    // 2. Enrich with Gemini 1.5 Pro (Qualitative analysis on top of quantitative skeleton)
    if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Operation Cancelled');

    if (gcsUri === "SKIP_ENRICHMENT") {
        return deterministicShots;
    }

    return enrichShotList(
        gcsUri,
        mimeType,
        deterministicShots,
        language,
        signal || new AbortController().signal,
        (msg, pct) => {
            // Map enrichment progress (0-100) to remaining (85-100)
            onProgress(msg, 85 + (pct * 0.15));
        },
        cacheName
    );
}



// Helper: Parse "3.5s" to 3.5
function parseOffset(offset: string): number {
    if (!offset) return 0;
    return parseFloat(offset.replace('s', ''));
}



--- END OF FILE: services/analysisCoordinator.ts ---

--- START OF FILE: services/uploadService.ts ---
/**
 * Robust Large File Upload Service for Gemini API
 * 
 * Handles multi-gigabyte video uploads using:
 * - Resumable upload protocol (chunked uploads)
 * - Web Worker offloading (keeps UI responsive)
 * - Progress tracking with accurate byte counts
 * - Automatic retry with exponential backoff
 * - Cancellation support
 */

import { ERROR_IDS, UploadProgress } from '../types';

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CONFIGURATION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const GEMINI_UPLOAD_BASE_URL = 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/upload/v1beta/files';

// Chunk size must be multiple of 256KB (262144 bytes)
// 8MB chunks balance between progress granularity and request overhead
const CHUNK_SIZE = 8 * 1024 * 1024; // 8MB

const MAX_RETRIES = 3;
const INITIAL_RETRY_DELAY_MS = 1000;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TYPES
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export interface UploadResult {
  fileUri: string;
  fileName: string;
  mimeType: string;
  state: string;
}

export interface UploadOptions {
  apiKey: string;
  file: File;
  onProgress: (progress: UploadProgress) => void;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// HELPER FUNCTIONS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
  const k = 1024;
  const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
}

async function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

const UPLOAD_CACHE_PREFIX = 'gemini_completed_upload_';
const UPLOAD_CACHE_TTL_MS = 47 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 47 hours (Gemini files expire in 48h)

export function getStoredUploadUri(file: File): string | null {
  const key = `${UPLOAD_CACHE_PREFIX}${file.name}_${file.size}_${file.lastModified}`;
  const cached = localStorage.getItem(key);

  if (!cached) return null;

  try {
    const { uri, timestamp } = JSON.parse(cached);
    const age = Date.now() - timestamp;

    if (age > UPLOAD_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
      localStorage.removeItem(key);
      return null;
    }

    return uri;
  } catch (e) {
    localStorage.removeItem(key);
    return null;
  }
}

export function storeUploadUri(file: File, uri: string) {
  const key = `${UPLOAD_CACHE_PREFIX}${file.name}_${file.size}_${file.lastModified}`;
  localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify({
    uri,
    timestamp: Date.now()
  }));
}
export interface CachedUpload {
  name: string;
  uri: string;
  timestamp: number;
  size: number;
}

export function getAllCachedUploads(): CachedUpload[] {
  const uploads: CachedUpload[] = [];

  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return [];

  // Iterate over all keys
  for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
    const key = localStorage.key(i);
    if (key && key.startsWith(UPLOAD_CACHE_PREFIX)) {
      try {
        const json = localStorage.getItem(key);
        if (json) {
          const data = JSON.parse(json);
          // Parse info from key: gemini_completed_upload_NAME_SIZE_DATE
          const parts = key.replace(UPLOAD_CACHE_PREFIX, '').split('_');

          // Reconstruct name (might have been split by underscores effectively, so we need to be careful)
          // Actually, since we don't control the name format, it is safer to store the name in the value.
          // But looking at storeUploadUri, we only store { uri, timestamp }.
          // So we have to rely on parsing the key.
          // Last two parts are size and timestamp.
          const lastModified = parseInt(parts.pop() || '0');
          const size = parseInt(parts.pop() || '0');
          const name = parts.join('_');

          if (Date.now() - data.timestamp < UPLOAD_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
            uploads.push({
              name,
              uri: data.uri,
              timestamp: data.timestamp,
              size
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // Ignore malformed
      }
    }
  }

  return uploads.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RESUMABLE UPLOAD IMPLEMENTATION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Step 1: Initialize resumable upload session
 * Returns the upload URL to use for subsequent chunk uploads
 * 
 * IMPROVEMENT: Validates cached URL before returning it.
 */
async function initializeResumableUpload(
  apiKey: string,
  file: File,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string> {
  const storageKey = `gemini_upload_session_${file.name}_${file.size}_${file.lastModified}`;
  const cachedUrl = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);

  if (cachedUrl) {
    console.log("Found cached upload session:", cachedUrl);
    // VALIDATE CACHE: actively ping the URL to see if it's still alive.
    // If we get 404 (Not Found) or 410 (Gone), or a network error suggesting DNS issues, we discard it.
    try {
      // Query command is safe and cheap
      const isValid = await checkSessionValidity(cachedUrl, signal);
      if (isValid) {
        console.log("Cached session is valid. Resuming.");
        return cachedUrl;
      }
      console.warn("Cached session is invalid or expired. Discarding.");
      localStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Error checking cached session validity. Discarding.", e);
      localStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
    }
  }

  // Start fresh session
  const url = `${GEMINI_UPLOAD_BASE_URL}?key=${apiKey}`;

  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'X-Goog-Upload-Protocol': 'resumable',
      'X-Goog-Upload-Command': 'start',
      'X-Goog-Upload-Header-Content-Length': file.size.toString(),
      'X-Goog-Upload-Header-Content-Type': file.type,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      file: {
        display_name: file.name,
      },
    }),
    signal,
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    const errorText = await response.text();
    console.error('Failed to initialize upload:', response.status, errorText);
    throw new Error(`Failed to initialize upload: ${response.status}`);
  }

  const uploadUrl = response.headers.get('X-Goog-Upload-URL');
  if (!uploadUrl) {
    throw new Error('No upload URL returned from initialization');
  }

  // Cache the URL
  localStorage.setItem(storageKey, uploadUrl);
  return uploadUrl;
}

/**
 * Helper to check if a resumable session URL is still valid
 */
async function checkSessionValidity(uploadUrl: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(uploadUrl, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'X-Goog-Upload-Command': 'query',
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      signal
    });

    // 200 OK means active or finalized. Both are "valid" enough to proceed (finalized will just finish immediately)
    // 404/410 means gone.
    // 400 might be legitimate error, but usually implies protocol mismatch.
    return response.ok;
  } catch (e) {
    // Network errors (DNS, etc) should count as "Invalid" so we try to get a new one.
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Clean up resume cache after success
 */
function clearResumableSession(file: File) {
  const storageKey = `gemini_upload_session_${file.name}_${file.size}_${file.lastModified}`;
  localStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
}

/**
 * Query the server for the current upload offset
 * This is crucial for resuming uploads after a browser reload or crash
 */
async function queryUploadStatus(uploadUrl: string, file: File, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<number> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(uploadUrl, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'X-Goog-Upload-Command': 'query',
        'Content-Type': 'application/json', // Sometimes required even if body is empty
      },
      signal
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      if (response.status === 404 || response.status === 410) {
        // Session expired or invalid - Throwing here allows the strict retry loop to handle "Session Dead"
        throw new Error("Upload session expired");
      }
      // Other errors might be temporary?
      const text = await response.text();
      console.warn(`Failed to query upload status: ${response.status} ${text}`);
      return 0; // Fallback to 0 if unsure, but usually safer to assume 0
    }

    const offsetHeader = response.headers.get('X-Goog-Upload-Status'); // Sometimes 'active' or 'final'
    const sizeHeader = response.headers.get('X-Goog-Upload-Size-Received');

    if (sizeHeader) {
      return parseInt(sizeHeader, 10);
    }

    return 0;
  } catch (e) {
    // If it's a network error, we should probably re-throw so we don't assume 0 and corrupt state
    // But for "offline" persistence, maybe 0 is ok?
    // Actually, if we can't reach server, we can't upload.
    console.warn("Error querying upload status:", e);
    throw e;
  }
}

async function uploadChunks(
  uploadUrl: string,
  file: File,
  onProgress: (progress: UploadProgress) => void,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<UploadResult> {
  const totalBytes = file.size;
  const totalChunks = Math.ceil(totalBytes / CHUNK_SIZE);

  // Checking actual server status to resume correctly
  // If this throws (network error), we bubble up.
  let bytesUploaded = await queryUploadStatus(uploadUrl, file, signal);

  if (bytesUploaded > 0) {
    const startChunkIndex = Math.floor(bytesUploaded / CHUNK_SIZE);
    console.log(`Resuming upload from server offset: ${formatBytes(bytesUploaded)} (${startChunkIndex}/${totalChunks} chunks skipped)`);
    onProgress({
      phase: 'uploading',
      message: `Resuming upload from ${formatBytes(bytesUploaded)}...`,
      percentage: Math.round((bytesUploaded / totalBytes) * 80),
      bytesUploaded,
      totalBytes,
      currentChunk: startChunkIndex,
      totalChunks,
    });
  }

  for (let chunkIndex = 0; chunkIndex < totalChunks; chunkIndex++) {
    if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

    const chunkStart = chunkIndex * CHUNK_SIZE;
    const chunkEnd = Math.min(chunkStart + CHUNK_SIZE, totalBytes);

    // If this chunk is already fully uploaded, skip it
    if (chunkEnd <= bytesUploaded) {
      continue;
    }

    // Determine upload alignment
    const uploadOffset = Math.max(chunkStart, bytesUploaded);
    if (uploadOffset >= chunkEnd) continue;

    const chunkSize = chunkEnd - uploadOffset;
    const isLastChunk = chunkIndex === totalChunks - 1;

    // Read chunk from file
    const chunk = file.slice(uploadOffset, chunkEnd);
    const chunkBuffer = await chunk.arrayBuffer();

    // Retry logic for each chunk
    let lastError: Error | null = null;
    for (let retry = 0; retry <= MAX_RETRIES; retry++) {
      try {
        const command = isLastChunk ? 'upload, finalize' : 'upload';

        const response = await fetch(uploadUrl, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'X-Goog-Upload-Command': command,
            'X-Goog-Upload-Offset': uploadOffset.toString(),
            'Content-Length': chunkSize.toString(),
            'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream',
          },
          body: chunkBuffer,
          signal,
        });

        if (!response.ok) {
          const errorText = await response.text();

          // SPECIAL HANDLING: Offset Mismatch (400)
          if (response.status === 400 && errorText.includes('offset')) {
            console.warn(`Offset mismatch error: ${errorText}. Updating offset and retrying...`);
            // Query status to get the TRUTH
            const newOffset = await queryUploadStatus(uploadUrl, file, signal);
            if (newOffset !== bytesUploaded) {
              bytesUploaded = newOffset;
              chunkIndex--; // Decrement to re-eval outer loop
              break; // Break retry loop
            }
          }

          // SPECIAL HANDLING: Gone/Not Found (410/404) -> Session Died
          if (response.status === 404 || response.status === 410) {
            throw new Error("Upload session expired during upload");
          }

          throw new Error(`Chunk upload failed: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`);
        }

        // Success - update global bytesUploaded tracker to the END of this chunk
        bytesUploaded = chunkEnd;

        // Update progress
        const percentage = Math.round((bytesUploaded / totalBytes) * 80); // Reserve 20% for processing
        onProgress({
          phase: 'uploading',
          message: `Uploading ${formatBytes(bytesUploaded)} of ${formatBytes(totalBytes)}...`,
          percentage,
          bytesUploaded,
          totalBytes,
          currentChunk: chunkIndex + 1,
          totalChunks,
        });

        // If this is the last chunk, parse the response for file info
        if (isLastChunk) {
          const result = await response.json();
          return {
            fileUri: result.file?.uri || result.uri,
            fileName: result.file?.name || result.name,
            mimeType: result.file?.mimeType || file.type,
            state: result.file?.state || 'PROCESSING',
          };
        }

        break; // Success, move to next chunk
      } catch (error) {
        lastError = error as Error;

        if (signal?.aborted) {
          throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
        }

        // Pass through fatal session errors without retry
        if (lastError.message === "Upload session expired during upload") {
          throw lastError;
        }

        if (retry < MAX_RETRIES) {
          const delay = INITIAL_RETRY_DELAY_MS * Math.pow(2, retry);
          console.warn(`Chunk ${chunkIndex + 1} failed, retrying in ${delay}ms...`, error);
          onProgress({
            phase: 'uploading',
            message: `Retrying chunk ${chunkIndex + 1}/${totalChunks}...`,
            percentage: Math.round((bytesUploaded / totalBytes) * 80),
            bytesUploaded,
            totalBytes,
            currentChunk: chunkIndex + 1,
            totalChunks,
          });
          await sleep(delay);
        }
      }
    }

    if (lastError) {
      throw lastError;
    }
  }

  throw new Error('Upload completed without finalization response');
}

/**
 * Step 3: Poll for file processing completion
 */
async function waitForProcessing(
  apiKey: string,
  fileName: string,
  onProgress: (progress: UploadProgress) => void,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string> {
  const funMessages = [
    "Time to grab a coffee ☕",
    "Perfect time for a bathroom break 🚽",
    "Call that person you've been avoiding 📞",
    "Clear out those delayed emails 📧",
    "Time to chat with a colleague 🗣️",
    "Time to water the plants 🪴",
    "Stretch your legs for a moment 🧘",
  ];

  // Pick ONE random message and stick to it
  const randomMessage = funMessages[Math.floor(Math.random() * funMessages.length)];
  const messagePrefix = "Processing video: ";


  let messageIndex = 0;
  let pollCount = 0;
  const maxPolls = 900; // 15 minutes max (at 1 second intervals) for large 4K files

  while (pollCount < maxPolls) {
    if (signal?.aborted) {
      throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
    }

    const url = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/${fileName}?key=${apiKey}`;
    const response = await fetch(url, { signal });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to check file status: ${response.status}`);
    }

    const fileInfo = await response.json();

    if (fileInfo.state === 'ACTIVE') {
      return fileInfo.uri;
    }

    if (fileInfo.state === 'FAILED') {
      throw new Error('File processing failed on Gemini server');
    }

    // Still processing
    // Still processing
    const message = `${messagePrefix}${randomMessage}`;
    onProgress({
      phase: 'processing',
      message,
      percentage: 80 + Math.min(pollCount / 2, 19), // Slowly increase from 80 to 99
      bytesUploaded: 0,
      totalBytes: 0,
    });

    messageIndex++;
    pollCount++;
    await sleep(1000);
  }

  throw new Error('File processing timed out');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MAIN EXPORT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Upload a large file to Gemini using resumable upload protocol
 * 
 * Includes SESSION RECOVERY:
 * If a resumable upload fails in a way that suggests the session is dead (404/410/Gone),
 * we automatically clear the cache and start a fresh session (once).
 */
export async function uploadLargeFileToGemini(options: UploadOptions): Promise<string> {
  const { apiKey, file, onProgress, signal } = options;
  const ATTEMPTS = 2; // 1 normal + 1 recovery

  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= ATTEMPTS; attempt++) {
    try {
      const isRecovery = attempt > 1;

      // Phase 1: Initialize (or Resume)
      onProgress({
        phase: 'initializing',
        message: isRecovery ? 'Previous session expired. Starting fresh...' : 'Initializing secure upload...',
        percentage: 2,
        bytesUploaded: 0,
        totalBytes: file.size,
      });

      const uploadUrl = await initializeResumableUpload(apiKey, file, signal);

      // Phase 2: Upload chunks
      onProgress({
        phase: 'uploading',
        message: 'Starting chunked upload...',
        percentage: 5,
        bytesUploaded: 0,
        totalBytes: file.size,
        currentChunk: 0,
        totalChunks: Math.ceil(file.size / CHUNK_SIZE),
      });

      const uploadResult = await uploadChunks(uploadUrl, file, onProgress, signal);

      // Phase 3: Wait for processing (if needed)
      // If we reach here, upload itself was successful.
      if (uploadResult.state === 'PROCESSING') {
        const finalUri = await waitForProcessing(apiKey, uploadResult.fileName, onProgress, signal);

        onProgress({
          phase: 'complete',
          message: 'Upload complete. Ready for analysis.',
          percentage: 100,
          bytesUploaded: file.size,
          totalBytes: file.size,
        });

        clearResumableSession(file); // Clean up
        storeUploadUri(file, finalUri);
        return finalUri;
      }

      // Success without processing wait (Already Active)
      clearResumableSession(file);
      storeUploadUri(file, uploadResult.fileUri);

      onProgress({
        phase: 'complete',
        message: 'Upload complete. Ready for analysis.',
        percentage: 100,
        bytesUploaded: file.size,
        totalBytes: file.size,
      });

      return uploadResult.fileUri;

    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`Large file upload attempt ${attempt} failed:`, error);

      // Check if we should recover
      const isSessionError = error instanceof Error &&
        (error.message.includes("Upload session expired") ||
          error.message.includes("404") ||
          error.message.includes("410"));

      // If it's a fatal session error, AND we haven't tried recovering yet...
      if (isSessionError && attempt < ATTEMPTS) {
        console.warn("Detected expired/invalid session. Clearing cache and retrying from scratch...");
        clearResumableSession(file);
        // Loop will continue to attempt 2
        continue;
      }

      // Otherwise (or if updated failed again), we fail for real.
      onProgress({
        phase: 'error',
        message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Upload failed',
        percentage: 0,
        bytesUploaded: 0,
        totalBytes: file.size,
      });

      if (error instanceof Error && (error.message === ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED || error.name === 'AbortError')) {
        throw error;
      }

      throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.UPLOAD_FAIL);
    }
  }

  throw new Error("Upload failed after recovery attempt.");
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FALLBACK: Simple upload for small files (< 20MB)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const SIMPLE_UPLOAD_THRESHOLD = 20 * 1024 * 1024; // 20MB

/**
 * Smart upload that chooses the best method based on file size
 * - Small files (< 20MB): Use simple multipart upload (faster)
 * - Large files (>= 20MB): Use resumable chunked upload (reliable)
 */
export async function smartUploadToGemini(options: UploadOptions): Promise<string> {
  const { file } = options;

  if (file.size < SIMPLE_UPLOAD_THRESHOLD) {
    // Use the original simple upload for small files
    return uploadSmallFile(options);
  }

  // Use chunked resumable upload for large files
  return uploadLargeFileToGemini(options);
}

async function uploadSmallFile(options: UploadOptions): Promise<string> {
  const { apiKey, file, onProgress, signal } = options;

  onProgress({
    phase: 'uploading',
    message: 'Uploading file...',
    percentage: 10,
    bytesUploaded: 0,
    totalBytes: file.size,
  });

  // Simple multipart upload for small files
  const url = `${GEMINI_UPLOAD_BASE_URL}?key=${apiKey}`;

  const formData = new FormData();
  formData.append('metadata', JSON.stringify({
    file: { display_name: file.name }
  }));
  formData.append('file', file);

  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: 'POST',
    body: formData,
    signal,
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Upload failed: ${response.status}`);
  }

  const result = await response.json();
  const fileName = result.file?.name || result.name;

  onProgress({
    phase: 'processing',
    message: 'Processing file...',
    percentage: 80,
    bytesUploaded: file.size,
    totalBytes: file.size,
  });

  // Wait for processing
  const finalUri = await waitForProcessing(apiKey, fileName, onProgress, signal);

  onProgress({
    phase: 'complete',
    message: 'Upload complete.',
    percentage: 100,
    bytesUploaded: file.size,
    totalBytes: file.size,
  });

  storeUploadUri(file, finalUri);
  return finalUri;
}

--- START OF FILE: services/embeddingService.ts ---
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { retryWithBackoff } from '../utils/retryUtils';
import { ERROR_IDS } from '../types';

export interface Document {
    id: string; // e.g. "scene_0"
    text: string; // The description
    embedding?: number[];
    metadata?: any; // e.g. timestamp, thumbnail
}

export interface SearchResult extends Document {
    similarity: number;
}

export class EmbeddingService {
    private genAI: GoogleGenerativeAI;
    private documents: Document[] = [];
    private model = 'text-embedding-004';

    constructor(apiKey: string) {
        this.genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(apiKey);
    }

    /**
     * Generates a vector embedding for the given text.
     */
    async generateEmbedding(text: string): Promise<number[]> {
        try {
            return await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
                const model = this.genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: this.model });
                const result = await model.embedContent(text);
                return result.embedding.values;
            }, 3, 1000);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Embedding generation failed:", error);
            throw new Error("Failed to generate embedding.");
        }
    }

    /**
     * Adds a document to the store (calculating embedding if missing).
     */
    async addDocument(doc: Document): Promise<void> {
        if (!doc.embedding) {
            doc.embedding = await this.generateEmbedding(doc.text);
        }
        this.documents.push(doc);
    }

    /**
     * Bulk add documents for efficiency.
     * Note: The Gemini API supports batch embedding, but for simplicity/reliability with current SDK version,
     * we'll do parallelized individual calls for MVP or batch if SDK supports it well.
     * Batch is cleaner.
     */
    async addDocumentsBatch(docs: Document[], onProgress?: (completed: number, total: number) => void): Promise<void> {
        // We will process in chunks to avoid rate limits
        const BATCH_SIZE = 5;
        let processed = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < docs.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) {
            const batch = docs.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE);
            await Promise.all(batch.map(async (doc) => {
                await this.addDocument(doc);
            }));
            processed += batch.length;
            if (onProgress) onProgress(processed, docs.length);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Searches for similar documents using cosine similarity.
     */
    async search(query: string, limit: number = 5): Promise<SearchResult[]> {
        const queryEmbedding = await this.generateEmbedding(query);

        const results = this.documents.map(doc => {
            if (!doc.embedding) return { ...doc, similarity: 0 };
            const similarity = this.cosineSimilarity(queryEmbedding, doc.embedding);
            return { ...doc, similarity };
        });

        return results
            .sort((a, b) => b.similarity - a.similarity)
            .slice(0, limit);
    }

    /**
     * Clears the vector store.
     */
    clear(): void {
        this.documents = [];
    }

    // --- Math Utils ---

    private cosineSimilarity(vecA: number[], vecB: number[]): number {
        const dotProduct = vecA.reduce((acc, val, i) => acc + val * vecB[i], 0);
        const magA = Math.sqrt(vecA.reduce((acc, val) => acc + val * val, 0));
        const magB = Math.sqrt(vecB.reduce((acc, val) => acc + val * val, 0));

        if (magA === 0 || magB === 0) return 0;
        return dotProduct / (magA * magB);
    }
}

// Singleton for simplicity in the App
export const embeddingService = new EmbeddingService(import.meta.env.VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY || '');

--- START OF FILE: services/authService.ts ---
export interface GoogleUser {
    email: string;
    accessToken: string;
    expiresIn: number;
}

// Default Client ID if not in env
const CLIENT_ID = import.meta.env.VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID || '193471035724-r1ls7f68v5sjvldr0ss922bkeqr6q2ji.apps.googleusercontent.com';
const SCOPES = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/devstorage.read_write https://www.googleapis.com/auth/userinfo.email https://www.googleapis.com/auth/cloud-platform';

class AuthService {
    private tokenClient: any;
    private accessToken: string | null = null;
    private tokenExpiration: number = 0;
    private userEmail: string | null = null;

    constructor() {
        this.initializeClient();
        this.restoreSession();
    }

    private restoreSession() {
        const storedToken = localStorage.getItem('gcs_access_token');
        const storedExpiration = localStorage.getItem('gcs_token_expiration');
        const storedEmail = localStorage.getItem('gcs_user_email');
        const currentTime = Date.now();

        console.log('[Auth Debug] Attempting Restore - Token:', storedToken ? 'YES' : 'NO', 'Expiration:', storedExpiration, 'Email:', storedEmail);

        if (storedToken && storedExpiration) {
            const exp = parseInt(storedExpiration, 10);
            const isValid = currentTime < exp;
            console.log(`[Auth Debug] Token Check: Exp=${exp}, Now=${currentTime}, Valid=${isValid}`);

            if (isValid) {
                this.accessToken = storedToken;
                this.tokenExpiration = exp;
                this.userEmail = storedEmail;
                console.log('[Auth Debug] Session Restored Successfully');
            } else {
                console.warn('[Auth Debug] Stored token is expired. Clearing storage.');
                this.signOut(); // Clear invalid data
            }
        }
    }

    private initializeClient() {
        if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !window.google) return;

        try {
            this.tokenClient = window.google.accounts.oauth2.initTokenClient({
                client_id: CLIENT_ID,
                scope: SCOPES,
                callback: (response: any) => {
                    // This callback isn't used for promise-based flows but required by init
                    console.debug('Token callback received', response);
                },
            });
            console.log("Google Auth Client Initialized");
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Failed to initialize Google Auth Client:", error);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Triggers the Google Sign-In popup
     * @returns Promise resolving to the access token
     */
    public async signIn(): Promise<GoogleUser> {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            if (!this.tokenClient) {
                this.initializeClient();
                if (!this.tokenClient) {
                    return reject(new Error("Google Identity Services not loaded. Check internet connection."));
                }
            }

            // Override the callback for this specific request
            this.tokenClient.callback = async (response: any) => {
                if (response.error) {
                    reject(response);
                    return;
                }

                this.accessToken = response.access_token;
                // Calculate expiration (usually 3599 seconds)
                this.tokenExpiration = Date.now() + (response.expires_in * 1000);

                try {
                    // Fetch user email using the token
                    const userInfo = await this.fetchUserInfo(response.access_token);
                    this.userEmail = userInfo.email;

                    // Persist session
                    localStorage.setItem('gcs_access_token', response.access_token);
                    localStorage.setItem('gcs_token_expiration', String(this.tokenExpiration));
                    localStorage.setItem('gcs_user_email', userInfo.email);

                    resolve({
                        email: userInfo.email,
                        accessToken: response.access_token,
                        expiresIn: response.expires_in
                    });
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error("Failed to fetch user info", error);
                    // Still return token even if email fetch fails
                    resolve({
                        email: 'Unknown User',
                        accessToken: response.access_token,
                        expiresIn: response.expires_in
                    });
                }
            };

            // Request permission directly (Popup)
            this.tokenClient.requestAccessToken({ prompt: 'consent' });
        });
    }

    public signOut() {
        if (this.accessToken) {
            window.google?.accounts.oauth2.revoke(this.accessToken, () => {
                console.log('Access token revoked');
            });
        }
        this.accessToken = null;
        this.userEmail = null;
        this.tokenExpiration = 0;
        localStorage.removeItem('gcs_access_token');
        localStorage.removeItem('gcs_token_expiration');
        localStorage.removeItem('gcs_user_email');
    }

    public getAccessToken(): string | null {
        if (this.isValid()) {
            return this.accessToken;
        }
        return null;
    }

    public getUserEmail(): string | null {
        return this.userEmail;
    }

    public isValid(): boolean {
        return !!this.accessToken && Date.now() < this.tokenExpiration;
    }

    private async fetchUserInfo(accessToken: string): Promise<{ email: string }> {
        const response = await fetch('https://www.googleapis.com/oauth2/v3/userinfo', {
            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` }
        });
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch user info');
        return await response.json();
    }
}

export const authService = new AuthService();

--- START OF FILE: services/exportService.ts ---
import { saveAs } from 'file-saver';
import { AnalysisMode } from '../types';

export interface ExportData<T> {
    version: string;
    type: AnalysisMode;
    date: string;
    sourceFile: string;
    data: T;
}

const CURRENT_VERSION = '1.0.0';

/**
 * Export analysis data to JSON file
 */
export function exportToJSON<T>(
    data: T,
    mode: AnalysisMode,
    fileName: string
) {
    const exportPayload: ExportData<T> = {
        version: CURRENT_VERSION,
        type: mode,
        date: new Date().toISOString(),
        sourceFile: fileName,
        data: data
    };

    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(exportPayload, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
    saveAs(blob, `${fileName}_${mode}_analysis.json`);
}

/**
 * Import analysis data from JSON file
 * Returns null if validation fails
 */
export async function importFromJSON<T>(file: File): Promise<ExportData<T> | null> {
    try {
        const text = await file.text();
        const data = JSON.parse(text) as ExportData<T>;

        // Basic validation
        if (!data.version || !data.type || !data.data) {
            console.error('Invalid import file format');
            return null;
        }

        return data;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to parse JSON import', error);
        return null;
    }
}

--- START OF FILE: services/nleExportService.ts ---
/**
 * NLE Export Service
 * Generates exchange formats for professional Non-Linear Editors.
 * Supported Formats:
 * - EDL (CMX 3600): Standard for Avid/Premiere/Resolve (Shot List)
 * - FCP XML (Version 5): Legacy XML supported by most NLEs (Shot List and Sequences)
 * - CSV Markers: For DaVinci Resolve etc.
 */

import { Shot, ProjectMetadata } from '../types';
import { applyTimecodeOffset, parseTimecode } from '../utils/tableUtils';

export type NLEFormat = 'edl' | 'fcp_xml' | 'resolve_csv' | 'avid_markers';

/**
 * Generate CMX 3600 EDL
 */
export function generateEDL(shots: Shot[], fileName: string, projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata, frameRate: number = 25): string {
    const title = (projectMetadata.episodeTitle || fileName).toUpperCase().substring(0, 50);
    const date = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-GB'); // DD/MM/YYYY typical for EDL

    let edl = `TITLE: ${title}\nFCM: NON-DROP FRAME\n\n`; // Simple Header

    shots.forEach((shot, index) => {
        const editId = (index + 1).toString().padStart(3, '0');
        const reel = 'AX'; // Auxiliary / Generic Reel
        const track = 'V';  // Video
        const type = 'C';   // Cut

        // Apply Offsets
        const tcIn = applyTimecodeOffset(shot.tcIn, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate);
        const tcOut = applyTimecodeOffset(shot.tcOut, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate);

        // Start Timecode in Sequence (Record In)
        // For a shot list, we usually lay them out sequentially OR just list them.
        // If it's a "Shot List" for logging, we might want "Record In" to match "Source In".
        // But EDLs imply a sequence.
        // Let's assume sequential layout for now, starting at timecodeStart.

        // HOWEVER, if the purpose is "Locators" or "Subclips", EDL might not be the best.
        // Ideally, we replicate the Source TC into the Record TC for simple logging EDLs.

        edl += `${editId}  ${reel}    ${track}     ${type}        ${tcIn} ${tcOut} ${tcIn} ${tcOut}\n`;
        edl += `* FROM CLIP NAME: ${fileName}\n`;
        if (shot.description) edl += `* COMMENT: ${shot.description}\n`;
        edl += `\n`;
    });

    return edl;
}

/**
 * Generate FCP XML (v5)
 */
export function generateFCPXML(shots: Shot[], fileName: string, projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata, frameRate: number = 25): string {
    // Basic XML Structure
    const duration = shots.reduce((acc, shot) => {
        const start = parseTimecode(shot.tcIn, frameRate);
        const end = parseTimecode(shot.tcOut, frameRate);
        return acc + Math.max(0, end - start);
    }, 0);

    // FPS Integer approximation
    const timebase = Math.round(frameRate);

    let xml = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE xmeml>
<xmeml version="5">
  <sequence>
    <name>${fileName}</name>
    <duration>${Math.round(duration * frameRate)}</duration>
    <rate>
      <timebase>${timebase}</timebase>
      <ntsc>${frameRate % 1 !== 0 ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE'}</ntsc>
    </rate>
    <media>
      <video>
        <track>
`;

    shots.forEach((shot, index) => {
        const shotDuration = parseTimecode(shot.duration, frameRate);
        const frameDuration = Math.round(shotDuration * frameRate);
        const startTc = applyTimecodeOffset(shot.tcIn, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate);
        const endTc = applyTimecodeOffset(shot.tcOut, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate);

        // We need source frame counts
        const startInFrames = Math.round(parseTimecode(startTc, frameRate) * frameRate);
        const endInFrames = startInFrames + frameDuration;

        xml += `          <clipitem id="clipitem-${index + 1}">
            <name>${shot.description || `Shot ${index + 1}`}</name>
            <duration>${frameDuration}</duration>
            <rate>
              <timebase>${timebase}</timebase>
              <ntsc>${frameRate % 1 !== 0 ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE'}</ntsc>
            </rate>
            <start>${startInFrames}</start>
            <end>${endInFrames}</end>
            <in>${startInFrames}</in>
            <out>${endInFrames}</out>
            <file id="file-1">
              <name>${fileName}</name>
              <pathurl>file://localhost/${fileName}</pathurl>
              <rate>
                <timebase>${timebase}</timebase>
                <ntsc>${frameRate % 1 !== 0 ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE'}</ntsc>
              </rate>
              <duration>${frameDuration}</duration>
            </file>
            <labels>
              <label2>Action: ${shot.action}</label2>
            </labels>
          </clipitem>
`;
    });

    xml += `        </track>
      </video>
    </media>
  </sequence>
</xmeml>`;

    return xml;
}

/**
 * Generate DaVinci Resolve CSV Markers
 */
export function generateResolveCSV(shots: Shot[], projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata, frameRate: number = 25): string {
    // Header
    let csv = `Source In,Source Out,Duration,Note,Name,Color\n`;

    shots.forEach(shot => {
        const tcIn = applyTimecodeOffset(shot.tcIn, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate);
        const tcOut = applyTimecodeOffset(shot.tcOut, projectMetadata.timecodeStart, frameRate);

        // Escape content
        const note = `"${(shot.description || '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
        const name = `"${(shot.action || '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;

        csv += `${tcIn},${tcOut},${shot.duration},${note},${name},Blue\n`;
    });

    return csv;
}

/**
 * Download functions for NLE formats
 */
export function downloadNLEFile(content: string, fileName: string, format: NLEFormat) {
    let extension = 'txt';
    let mimeType = 'text/plain';

    switch (format) {
        case 'edl': extension = 'edl'; break;
        case 'fcp_xml': extension = 'xml'; mimeType = 'text/xml'; break;
        case 'resolve_csv': extension = 'csv'; mimeType = 'text/csv'; break;
        case 'avid_markers': extension = 'txt'; break;
    }

    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mimeType });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `${fileName}_export.${extension}`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

--- START OF FILE: services/subtitleService.ts ---
/**
 * Subtitle Export Service
 * Generates SRT and VTT files for captions and subtitles.
 * Ensures compatibility with NLEs and web players.
 */

import { TranscriptionResult } from '../types';
import { applyTimecodeOffset, parseTimecode, secondsToTimecode } from '../utils/tableUtils';

export type SubtitleFormat = 'srt' | 'vtt';

// Helper to format timecode for SRT (HH:MM:SS,mmm)
// Video standard uses frames, but SRT uses milliseconds
function formatSrtTime(timeStr: string, frameRate: number): string {
    const seconds = parseTimecode(timeStr, frameRate);
    const date = new Date(0);
    date.setMilliseconds(seconds * 1000);
    const iso = date.toISOString().substr(11, 12); // HH:MM:SS.mmm
    return iso.replace('.', ',');
}

// Helper to format timecode for VTT (HH:MM:SS.mmm)
function formatVttTime(timeStr: string, frameRate: number): string {
    const seconds = parseTimecode(timeStr, frameRate);
    const date = new Date(0);
    date.setMilliseconds(seconds * 1000);
    return date.toISOString().substr(11, 12); // HH:MM:SS.mmm
}

export function generateSubtitles(
    result: TranscriptionResult,
    format: SubtitleFormat,
    timecodeOffset?: string,
    frameRate: number = 25
): string {
    let output = '';

    if (format === 'vtt') {
        output += 'WEBVTT\n\n';
    }

    result.segments.forEach((seg, index) => {
        // 1. Calculate Offsets
        const startTc = applyTimecodeOffset(seg.startTime, timecodeOffset, frameRate);
        const endTc = applyTimecodeOffset(seg.endTime, timecodeOffset, frameRate);

        // 2. Format for Subtitle Spec (SRT=Comma, VTT=Dot)
        // Note: applyTimecodeOffset returns HH:MM:SS:FF (video standard)
        // We need to convert that video TC back to seconds, then to SRT/VTT timestamp
        // This seems redundant but ensures the Offset Frame Math happened correctly first.
        // Or we can just calculate seconds directly.
        // Let's rely on applyTimecodeOffset to handle the drop-frame logic correctly if needed,
        // but SRT/VTT are strictly wall-clock time (milliseconds).
        // Drop-frame TC is a labelling convention, but SRT is absolute time.
        // If we want the SRT to MATCH the NLE timecode display, we might need to be careful.
        // NLEs usually import SRT maps to absolute time.
        // So we should calculate the total seconds including offset, then format as MS.

        const baseStartSeconds = parseTimecode(seg.startTime, frameRate);
        const baseEndSeconds = parseTimecode(seg.endTime, frameRate);
        const offsetSeconds = timecodeOffset ? parseTimecode(timecodeOffset, frameRate) : 0;

        const finalStartSeconds = baseStartSeconds + offsetSeconds;
        const finalEndSeconds = baseEndSeconds + offsetSeconds;

        const dateStart = new Date(0); dateStart.setMilliseconds(finalStartSeconds * 1000);
        const dateEnd = new Date(0); dateEnd.setMilliseconds(finalEndSeconds * 1000);

        let startStr = dateStart.toISOString().substr(11, 12);
        let endStr = dateEnd.toISOString().substr(11, 12);

        if (format === 'srt') {
            startStr = startStr.replace('.', ',');
            endStr = endStr.replace('.', ',');

            output += `${index + 1}\n`;
            output += `${startStr} --> ${endStr}\n`;
            output += `${seg.transcript}\n\n`;
        } else {
            // VTT
            output += `${startStr} --> ${endStr}\n`;
            output += `${seg.transcript}\n\n`;
        }
    });

    return output;
}

export function downloadSubtitle(content: string, fileName: string, format: SubtitleFormat) {
    const mimeType = format === 'vtt' ? 'text/vtt' : 'text/plain'; // .srt is technically text/plain or application/x-subrip
    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mimeType });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `${fileName}.${format}`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

import { GoogleGenerativeAI, SchemaType as Type } from "@google/generative-ai";
