// Context Caching is an advanced feature not fully exposed in the standard browser SDK yet for all models.
// For stable deployment, we will SKIP explicit cache creation for now and rely on standard context window.
// Context Caching Implementation via REST
export const createContextCache = async (
  fileUri: string,
  mimeType: string,
  ttlSeconds: number = 600
): Promise<string> => {
  try {
    const apiKey = import.meta.env.VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY;
    const response = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/cachedContents?key=${apiKey}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: 'models/gemini-2.5-flash',
        contents: [{
          parts: [{ fileData: { fileUri, mimeType } }],
          role: 'user'
        }],
        ttl: `${ttlSeconds}s`
      })
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      console.warn('Context Cache creation failed', await response.text());
      return '';
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    console.log('Context Cache created:', data.name);
    return data.name; // Returns "cachedContents/..."
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('Context Cache error:', error);
    return ''; // Fallback
  }
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// UTILITY FUNCTIONS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// UTILITY FUNCTIONS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

function repairJson(jsonString: string): string {
  let fixed = jsonString.trim();

  // 1. Remove Markdown code blocks
  fixed = fixed.replace(/^```(json)?|```$/g, '');

  // 2. Extract JSON object/array if embedded in text
  const jsonMatch = fixed.match(/(\{[\s\S]*\}|\[[\s\S]*\])/);
  if (jsonMatch) {
    fixed = jsonMatch[0];
  }

  // 3. Fix trailing commas (common AI error)
  fixed = fixed.replace(/,(\s*[}\]])/g, '$1');

  // 4. Fix missing commas between objects (e.g. }{ or ][)
  fixed = fixed.replace(/}(\s*){/g, '},$1{');
  fixed = fixed.replace(/](\s*)\[/g, '],$1[');

  // 5. Attempt to close unclosed arrays/objects
  const openBraces = (fixed.match(/{/g) || []).length;
  const closeBraces = (fixed.match(/}/g) || []).length;
  const openBrackets = (fixed.match(/\[/g) || []).length;
  const closeBrackets = (fixed.match(/]/g) || []).length;

  if (openBraces > closeBraces) fixed += '}'.repeat(openBraces - closeBraces);
  if (openBrackets > closeBrackets) fixed += ']'.repeat(openBrackets - closeBrackets);

  return fixed;
}

function safeJsonParse<T>(jsonString: string): T {
  try {
    const cleanString = repairJson(jsonString);
    if (!cleanString) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.INVALID_API_RESPONSE);
    return JSON.parse(cleanString);
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("Initial JSON parse failed, attempting aggressive repair...", e);
    try {
      // Secondary aggressive repair for specific edge cases like "Expected ',' or ']'"
      let aggressiveFix = jsonString.replace(/,(\s*)/g, ',$1'); // Ensure spacing
      // Remove any trailing text after the last closing brace/bracket
      aggressiveFix = aggressiveFix.replace(/([}\]])[\s\S]*$/, '$1');
      return JSON.parse(repairJson(aggressiveFix));
    } catch (e2) {
      console.error("Failed to parse JSON response:", jsonString, e2);
      throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.INVALID_API_RESPONSE);
    }
  }
}

function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
  if (isNaN(seconds) || seconds < 0) return "00:00:00";
  const totalSeconds = Math.floor(seconds);
  const h = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600), m = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60), s = totalSeconds % 60;
  return [h, m, s].map(v => String(v).padStart(2, '0')).join(':');
}

import { smartUploadToGemini } from './uploadService';

// Threshold for using chunked upload (files >= 50MB use chunked)
const CHUNKED_UPLOAD_THRESHOLD = 50 * 1024 * 1024;

/**
 * Upload a file to Gemini for analysis
 * 
 * Automatically chooses the best upload method:
 * - Small files (< 50MB): SDK's built-in upload (simpler, faster)
 * - Large files (>= 50MB): Chunked resumable upload (handles multi-GB files)
 */
export const uploadFileToGemini = async (
  file: File,
  progressCallback: (message: string, percentage: number) => void,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string> => {
  // For ANY file size, use smartUploadToGemini to ensure consistency
  // logic simplified to direct return of the robust uploader
  return smartUploadToGemini({
    apiKey: import.meta.env.VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY || '',
    file,
    onProgress: (progress: UploadProgress) => {
      progressCallback(progress.message, progress.percentage);
    },
    signal,
  });
};

// SYNOPSIS GENERATION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export const analyzeVideoContent = async (
  fileUri: string,
  mimeType: string,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  progressCallback: (message: string, percentage: number) => void,
  cacheName?: string
): Promise<AnalysisResult> => {

  try {
    if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
    progressCallback('Analyzing video content...', 30);

    const prompt = `
      You are an expert video analysis AI. Analyze the provided video file.
      Your task is to synthesize all information into a final, cohesive analysis in JSON format.
      - Transcribe spoken words if present.
      - Summarize the entire video in one sentence.
      - Create a single, chronological list of the most important key events.
      - Infer any potential dialogue or important sounds based on visual cues if audio is unclear.
      
      **CRITICAL:** Base all descriptions strictly on the video content. Do not invent details not present in the video.
    `;

    const modelConfig: any = {
      model: 'gemini-2.5-pro',
      generationConfig: {
        responseMimeType: 'application/json',
        responseSchema: {
          type: Type.OBJECT,
          properties: {
            summary: { type: Type.STRING, description: "A brief, one-sentence summary of the video's content." },
            events: {
              type: Type.ARRAY,
              items: {
                type: Type.OBJECT,
                properties: {
                  timestamp: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Approximate time or sequence of the event (e.g., '00:05', 'Beginning', 'Middle')." },
                  description: { type: Type.STRING, description: "A detailed description of what is happening in the event." },
                },
                required: ['timestamp', 'description'],
              },
            },
            inferred_dialogue: { type: Type.STRING, description: "An inferred transcription of what might be said, based on visual cues." },
            transcription: { type: Type.STRING, description: "A transcription of the spoken content from the audio track. If no audio was provided, state that." },
          },
          required: ['summary', 'events', 'inferred_dialogue', 'transcription'],
        },
      }
    };

    if (cacheName) {
      modelConfig.cachedContent = cacheName;
    }

    const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel(modelConfig);

    const contents = cacheName
      ? [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: prompt }] }]
      : [
        {
          role: 'user',
          parts: [
            { fileData: { fileUri: fileUri, mimeType: mimeType } },
            { text: prompt }
          ]
        }
      ];

    const response = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
      if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
      return await model.generateContent({ contents });
    }, 3, 2000); // 3 retries, starting at 2s delay

    const result = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
      // ... (retry logic)
      if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
      return await model.generateContent({ contents });
    }, 3, 2000);

    if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
    const rawText = result.response.text() || '';
    try {
      return safeJsonParse<AnalysisResult>(rawText);
    } catch (parseError) {
      console.error("[GEMINI_DEBUG] Analysis (Synopsis) Parse Failed. Preview:", rawText.slice(0, 1000));
      throw parseError;
    }

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error analyzing video with Gemini:", error);
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED) throw error;
    throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.ANALYSIS_FAIL);
  }
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SYNOPSIS GENERATION (TEXT ONLY FALLBACK)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export const generateSynopsisFromText = async (
  transcriptText: string,
  config: SynopsisConfig
): Promise<Synopsis> => {
  const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
    model: 'gemini-2.5-pro',
    generationConfig: {
      responseMimeType: 'application/json',
      responseSchema: {
        type: Type.OBJECT,
        properties: {
          title: { type: Type.STRING },
          writtenBy: { type: Type.STRING },
          directedBy: { type: Type.STRING },
          oneLine: { type: Type.STRING },
          paragraph: { type: Type.STRING },
          fullSynopsis: { type: Type.STRING },
          keyThemes: { type: Type.STRING },
          targetAudience: { type: Type.STRING },
        }
      }
    }
  });

  const prompt = `
    You are a professional film screenwriter.
    Analyze the following TRANSCRIPT of a video to generate a professional synopsis.
    
    TRANSCRIPT:
    "${transcriptText.slice(0, 100000)}..." (Truncated for context limit if needed)

    Generate a JSON object with:
    1. Title: Creative title based on content.
    2. WrittenBy: "${config.writer || 'Unknown'}"
    3. DirectedBy: "${config.director || 'Unknown'}"
    4. OneLine: A compelling logline (${config.loglineMaxWords} words).
    5. Paragraph: A summary paragraph (${config.summaryMaxWords} words).
    6. FullSynopsis: Detailed synopsis.
    7. KeyThemes: Comma-separated themes.
    8. TargetAudience: Likely audience demographic.
  `;

  try {
    const response = await model.generateContent(prompt);
    return safeJsonParse<Synopsis>(response.response.text());
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Text-Only Synopsis Failed:", e);
    throw e;
  }
};

export const generateSynopsisFromAnalysis = async (
  analysis: AnalysisResult,
  writer: string,
  director: string,
  language: string,
  keywords: string,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  synopsisConfig: SynopsisConfig
): Promise<Synopsis> => {
  if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

  try {
    const prompt = `
          You are a master screenwriter and film marketing expert. Your task is to transform raw video analysis data into a compelling, professional film synopsis and marketing profile. Your writing should be evocative and ready for a pitch deck.

          **Content to Generate:**
          1.  **Title:** A creative, fitting title for the video.
          2.  **One Line (Logline):** A single, powerful sentence capturing the core story.
          3.  **Paragraph (Summary):** An engaging paragraph setting the scene and narrative.
          4.  **Full Synopsis:** A detailed, multi-paragraph synopsis with a clear beginning, middle, and end.
          5.  **Key Themes:** A concise summary of the central themes and ideas.
          6.  **Target Audience:** A description of the ideal viewer for this content.

          **Your Constraints:**
          - **Language:** Generate all content in: ${language}.
          - **Title:** Maximum ${synopsisConfig.titleMaxChars} characters.
          - **One Line (Logline):** Approximately ${synopsisConfig.loglineMaxWords} words.
          - **Paragraph (Summary):** Approximately ${synopsisConfig.summaryMaxWords} words.
          - **Key Themes:** Approximately ${synopsisConfig.keyThemesMaxWords} words.
          - **Target Audience:** Approximately ${synopsisConfig.targetAudienceMaxWords} words.
          - **Full Synopsis:** No strict length limit, but should be comprehensive and well-structured.

${keywords ? `
          **Guiding Keywords:** Use these to guide the tone and focus: ${keywords}.` : ''}

          **Video Analysis Data:**
          - Transcription: "${analysis.transcription}"
          - Summary: "${analysis.summary}"
          - Key Events: ${JSON.stringify(analysis.events.map(e => e.description))}
          - Inferred Dialogue: "${analysis.inferred_dialogue}"

          Your output must be a single JSON object with keys: "title", "oneLine", "paragraph", "fullSynopsis", "keyThemes", "targetAudience".
      `;

    if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

    const response = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
      if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
      const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
        model: 'gemini-2.5-pro',
        generationConfig: {
          responseMimeType: 'application/json',
          responseSchema: {
            type: Type.OBJECT,
            properties: {
              title: { type: Type.STRING },
              oneLine: { type: Type.STRING },
              paragraph: { type: Type.STRING },
              fullSynopsis: { type: Type.STRING },
              keyThemes: { type: Type.STRING },
              targetAudience: { type: Type.STRING }
            },
            required: ['title', 'oneLine', 'paragraph', 'fullSynopsis', 'keyThemes', 'targetAudience']
          }
        }
      });
      return await model.generateContent({
        contents: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: prompt }] }]
      });
    }, 3, 2000);

    const aiGeneratedContent = safeJsonParse<any>(response.response.text() || '');

    const resultJson: Synopsis = {
      ...aiGeneratedContent,
      writtenBy: writer,
      directedBy: director,
    };

    return resultJson;

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error generating synopsis with Gemini:", error);
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED) {
      throw error;
    }
    throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.SYNOPSIS_FAIL);
  }
};

// ... Bio Generation Wrapper ...

export const extractTalentFromVideo = async (fileUri: string, mimeType: string, cacheName?: string): Promise<string[]> => {
  const prompt = `Analyze the video. Identify any people introduced with text on the screen, commonly known as a 'lower third'. Extract their full names. Return the result as a valid JSON object with a single key 'names' which is an array of strings. For example: {"names": ["Lisa Hywood", "Ellen Connelly"]}. If no names are found, return an empty array. Do not include titles or roles, only full names.`;

  try {
    const response = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
      // Using Gemini 2.5 Flash as the standard efficient model
      const STANDARD_MODEL = 'gemini-2.5-flash';

      const modelConfig: any = {
        model: STANDARD_MODEL,
        generationConfig: { temperature: 0.1, responseMimeType: 'application/json' },
      };

      if (cacheName) {
        modelConfig.cachedContent = cacheName;
      }

      const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel(modelConfig);

      const parts = cacheName
        ? [{ text: prompt }]
        : [{ fileData: { fileUri, mimeType } }, { text: prompt }];

      return await model.generateContent({
        contents: [{ role: 'user', parts }]
      });
    }, 3, 1000);

    const parsed = safeJsonParse<{ names: any }>(response.response.text() || '');
    if (parsed.names && Array.isArray(parsed.names)) {
      return [...new Set<string>(parsed.names.filter((name: any): name is string => typeof name === 'string' && name.trim() !== ''))];
    }
    return [];
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Error analyzing video for talent:', e);
    throw new Error('An API error occurred while analyzing the video for talent.');
  }
};

// ... generateBio Wrapper needed ...


// ... Extract Talent (Done in previous step) ...

interface BioResponse {
  fullName: string;
  title: string;
  biography: string;
  sources?: string[];
}

export const generateBio = async (talentName: string, context: string, language: string): Promise<BioResponse> => {
  const prompt = `You are a professional biographer. Research and write a professional biography for the person named "${talentName}". Their specific context or role is: "${context}". Use this context to find the correct person. The biography should be engaging, accurate, and suitable for promotional materials. The entire response must be written in ${language}. 
  
  **CRITICAL OUTPUT RULE:**
  - You must return a SINGLE, VALID JSON object.
  - Do NOT output any markdown, conversational text, or explanations outside the JSON.
  - Do NOT start with "##".
  
  **JSON Structure:**
  {
    "fullName": "Name",
    "title": "Role/Title",
    "biography": "The biography text...",
    "found": boolean
  }
  
  **If you are NOT 100% sure:**
  - Do NOT return "Information Not Found" as plain text.
  - Instead, return the JSON with "found": false, or provide your **best educated guess** based on visual appearance and context (e.g., "This appears to be [Name], known for...").
  - If guessing, explicitly state in the biography: "Likely [Name], pending verification." and append: "Is this [Name]?"
  
  **If you cannot find sufficient information:**
  Return exactly: {"fullName": "${talentName}", "title": "Information Not Found", "biography": "Could not find sufficient information to generate a biography.", "found": false}`;

  try {
    const response = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
      const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
        model: 'gemini-2.5-pro',
        tools: [{ googleSearch: {} } as any], // Cast to allow Google Search despite potential type definition lag
        generationConfig: {
          temperature: 0.2, // Lowered temperature for more factual responses
        },
      });
      return await model.generateContent({
        contents: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: prompt }] }]
      });
    }, 3, 2000);

    const parsed = safeJsonParse<any>(response.response.text() || '');

    // Extract Grounding Sources
    const sources: string[] = [];
    if (response.response.candidates?.[0]?.groundingMetadata?.groundingChunks) {
      response.response.candidates[0].groundingMetadata.groundingChunks.forEach((chunk: any) => {
        if (chunk.web?.uri) {
          sources.push(chunk.web.uri);
        }
      });
    }

    return { ...parsed, sources: [...new Set(sources)] } as BioResponse;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(`Error generating bio for ${talentName}:`, e);
    throw new Error(`Failed to generate biography for "${talentName}".`);
  }
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// GRAPHICS & LOWER THIRDS ANALYSIS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export async function analyzeGraphicsAndLowerThirds(
  fileUri: string, mimeType: string, duration: number, filmTitle: string, cacheName?: string
): Promise<GraphicsAnalysisResult> {

  const prompt = `You are a post-production assistant with excellent OCR skills. Analyze the provided video file. Your task is to identify and transcribe all on-screen graphics (e.g., title cards, location indicators) and lower thirds (text identifying a person or place, usually at the bottom of the screen). 
  
  **CRITICAL RULES:**
  - Be extremely accurate. 
  - Output ONLY text that is clearly visible on screen. 
  - Do NOT correct spelling errors in the source text. Transcribe exactly what you see.
  - Do NOT invent text that is not there.
  
  Output must be a single, valid JSON object strictly adhering to the schema.`;

  try {
    const response = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
      const modelConfig: any = {
        model: 'gemini-2.5-flash',
        generationConfig: {
          responseMimeType: 'application/json',
          temperature: 0.2,
          responseSchema: {
            type: Type.OBJECT,
            properties: {
              programTitle: { type: Type.STRING },
              runtime: { type: Type.STRING },
              graphics: {
                type: Type.ARRAY,
                items: {
                  type: Type.OBJECT, properties: {
                    description: { type: Type.STRING },
                    timecodeIn: { type: Type.STRING },
                    timecodeOut: { type: Type.STRING },
                    cleanTimecodeIn: { type: Type.STRING },
                    cleanTimecodeOut: { type: Type.STRING },
                  },
                  required: ["description", "timecodeIn", "timecodeOut"],
                },
              },
              lowerThirds: {
                type: Type.ARRAY,
                items: {
                  type: Type.OBJECT, properties: {
                    text: { type: Type.STRING },
                    timecodeIn: { type: Type.STRING },
                    timecodeOut: { type: Type.STRING }
                  },
                  required: ["text", "timecodeIn", "timecodeOut"],
                },
              },
            },
            required: ["programTitle", "runtime", "graphics", "lowerThirds"],
          },
        }
      };

      if (cacheName) {
        modelConfig.cachedContent = cacheName;
      }

      const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel(modelConfig);

      const parts = cacheName
        ? [{ text: prompt }]
        : [{ fileData: { fileUri, mimeType } }, { text: prompt }];

      return await model.generateContent({
        contents: [{ role: 'user', parts }]
      });
    }, 3, 2000);

    const rawText = response.response.text() || '';
    let parsedResult;
    try {
      parsedResult = safeJsonParse<GraphicsAnalysisResult>(rawText);
    } catch (parseError) {
      console.error("[GEMINI_DEBUG] Graphics Parse Failed. Preview:", rawText.slice(0, 1000));
      throw parseError;
    }
    parsedResult.programTitle = filmTitle;
    parsedResult.runtime = formatDuration(duration);
    return parsedResult;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Error analyzing graphics:", e);
    throw new Error("Graphics analysis failed.");
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FAUNA FINDER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export async function analyzeVideoForSpecies(
  fileUri: string,
  mimeType: string,
  primaryLanguage: string,
  secondaryLanguage: string,
  videoDuration: number = 0,
  cacheName?: string
): Promise<Omit<SpeciesSighting, 'id' | 'thumbnail'>[]> {

  // Strategy: Segmented Analysis for Large Files
  // To prevent output token limits (truncation) and "Invalid Format" errors, we split long videos.
  const SEGMENT_LENGTH = 300; // 5 minutes per segment
  const segments: { start: number; end: number }[] = [];

  if (videoDuration > SEGMENT_LENGTH) {
    for (let t = 0; t < videoDuration; t += SEGMENT_LENGTH) {
      segments.push({ start: t, end: Math.min(t + SEGMENT_LENGTH, videoDuration) });
    }
  } else {
    // Single pass for short videos or if duration unknown
    segments.push({ start: 0, end: videoDuration });
  }

  const allSightings: Omit<SpeciesSighting, 'id' | 'thumbnail'>[] = [];

  // Helper for prompt time formatting
  const fmtTime = (s: number) => {
    // If duration is 0 (unknown), return empty to avoid weird prompts
    if (s === 0 && segments.length === 1 && segments[0].end === 0) return "";

    const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
    const sec = Math.floor(s % 60);
    return `${m.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${sec.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < segments.length; i++) {
    const { start, end } = segments[i];
    const isSegmented = segments.length > 1;

    // Construct Prompt
    const timeInstruction = isSegmented
      ? `**CRITICAL FOCUS:** Analyze ONLY the video segment from ${fmtTime(start)} to ${fmtTime(end)}. Ignore anything outside this timeframe.`
      : `Analyze the entire video.`;

    const prompt = `Analyze this video. Your task is to identify **every single animal sighting**. 
    ${timeInstruction}
    Do not summarize or group them. If the same animal appears in multiple shots, create a separate entry for each sighting. 
    For each sighting, provide: 
    - 'commonName': The common name in ${primaryLanguage}.
    - 'latinName': The scientific name.
    - 'germanName': The common name in ${secondaryLanguage}.
    - 'timecode': The EXACT timecode of the frame where the animal is seen.
    - 'category': A simple category (e.g., "Mammal", "Bird", "Insect", "Fish").
    - 'confidence': A score from 0.0 to 1.0 indicating your confidence in the identification.
    - 'behavior': Briefly describe what the animal is doing (e.g., "Grazing", "Hunting", "Resting", "Flying").
    - 'iucnStatus': The IUCN Red List status if known (e.g., "LC", "VU", "EN", "CR").
    - 'encounterGroup': If the same individual animal is seen in a continuous sequence of shots, assign a unique simple ID (e.g., "Lion_1") to group these sightings. If unsure or isolated, leave blank.
    
    **CRITICAL ANTI-HALLUCINATION RULES:**
    - If you are not 100% sure of the exact species, provide the Genus or Family name instead. 
    - Do NOT guess a specific species without clear visual evidence.
    - If the animal is too blurry or distant to identify, do not invent a name.

    The final output must be a valid JSON object with a single key "sightings" containing the list of sightings.`;

    try {
      // We run purely sequential to respect rate limits and ensure order
      const response = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
        const modelConfig: any = {
          model: 'gemini-2.5-flash',
          generationConfig: {
            responseMimeType: "application/json",
            responseSchema: {
              type: Type.OBJECT,
              properties: {
                sightings: {
                  type: Type.ARRAY,
                  items: {
                    type: Type.OBJECT,
                    properties: {
                      commonName: { type: Type.STRING },
                      latinName: { type: Type.STRING },
                      germanName: { type: Type.STRING },
                      timecode: { type: Type.STRING },
                      category: { type: Type.STRING },
                      confidence: { type: Type.NUMBER },
                      behavior: { type: Type.STRING },
                      iucnStatus: { type: Type.STRING },
                      encounterGroup: { type: Type.STRING },
                    },
                    required: ["commonName", "latinName", "timecode", "category", "confidence"],
                  },
                },
              },
              required: ["sightings"],
            },
          },
        };

        if (cacheName) {
          modelConfig.cachedContent = cacheName;
        }

        const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel(modelConfig);

        const parts = cacheName
          ? [{ text: prompt }]
          : [{ fileData: { fileUri, mimeType } }, { text: prompt }];

        const result = await model.generateContent({
          contents: [{ role: 'user', parts }]
        });
        return result;
      }, 3, 2000);

      const parsed = safeJsonParse<any>(response.response.text() || '');

      // Normalize and Append
      let segmentSightings: any[] = [];
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        segmentSightings = parsed;
      } else if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
        const potentialArray = parsed.sightings || parsed.animals || parsed.fauna || parsed.result;
        if (Array.isArray(potentialArray)) {
          segmentSightings = potentialArray;
        }
      }

      if (segmentSightings.length > 0) {
        allSightings.push(...segmentSightings);
      }

    } catch (e) {
      console.error(`Error analyzing fauna segment ${i} (${fmtTime(start)}-${fmtTime(end)}):`, e);
      // We continue to next segment even if one fails
    }
  }

  if (allSightings.length === 0 && segments.length > 1) {
    throw new Error("Fauna analysis failed for all segments.");
  }

  return allSightings; // Return valid array (even if empty) to avoid crashes
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TRANSCRIPTION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export async function transcribeMedia(
  fileUri: string, mimeType: string, signal: AbortSignal, progressCallback: (message: string, percentage: number) => void, cacheName?: string
): Promise<Omit<TranscriptionResult, 'speakerMap'>> {

  progressCallback('Transcribing audio with AI...', 50);

  const prompt = `You are an expert audio transcription AI. Transcribe the provided media, identifying different speakers as SPEAKER_00, SPEAKER_01, etc. Provide the output as a time-coded transcript in a JSON object adhering to the schema.`;

  if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

  try {
    const response = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
      if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

      const modelConfig: any = {
        model: 'gemini-2.5-flash',
        generationConfig: {
          responseMimeType: 'application/json',
          responseSchema: {
            type: Type.OBJECT,
            properties: {
              segments: {
                type: Type.ARRAY,
                items: {
                  type: Type.OBJECT,
                  properties: {
                    startTime: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Start time in HH:MM:SS.mmm format." },
                    endTime: { type: Type.STRING, description: "End time in HH:MM:SS.mmm format." },
                    speaker: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Speaker identifier (e.g., SPEAKER_00)." },
                    transcript: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Transcribed text." },
                  },
                  required: ['startTime', 'endTime', 'speaker', 'transcript'],
                },
              },
            },
            required: ['segments'],
          },
        },
      };

      if (cacheName) {
        modelConfig.cachedContent = cacheName;
      }

      const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel(modelConfig);

      const parts = cacheName
        ? [{ text: prompt }]
        : [{ fileData: { fileUri, mimeType } }, { text: prompt }];

      return await model.generateContent({
        contents: [{ role: 'user', parts }]
      });
    }, 3, 2000);

    if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
    progressCallback('Finalizing transcription...', 95);

    const rawText = response.response.text() || '';
    try {
      return safeJsonParse<Omit<TranscriptionResult, 'speakerMap'>>(rawText);
    } catch (parseError) {
      console.error("[GEMINI_DEBUG] Transcription Parse Failed. Preview:", rawText.slice(0, 1000));
      throw parseError;
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Transcription failed:", e);
    if (e instanceof Error && e.message === ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED) throw e;
    throw new Error("Transcription failed.");
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SHOT LIST GENERATION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export async function generateShotList(
  fileUri: string, mimeType: string, language: string, frameRate: number, signal: AbortSignal, progressCallback: (message: string, percentage: number) => void, cacheName?: string
): Promise<Omit<Shot, 'nr' | 'thumbnail'>[]> {

  progressCallback('Analyzing video for shot detection...', 10);

  const prompt = `You are an expert film editor analyzing this video to identify distinct shots. A shot change is defined by any change in camera angle, subject framing, or location. Be very granular and sensitive to subtle changes. For each shot you identify, provide a description, action, and audio cues in ${language}. Provide timecodes in HH:MM:SS:FF format based on a ${frameRate}fps frame rate. The final output must be a valid JSON object adhering to the schema.`;

  try {
    const response = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
      if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

      const modelConfig: any = {
        model: 'gemini-2.5-flash',
        generationConfig: {
          responseMimeType: 'application/json',
          responseSchema: {
            type: Type.OBJECT, properties: {
              shots: {
                type: Type.ARRAY, items: {
                  type: Type.OBJECT, properties: {
                    tcIn: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Timecode IN in HH:MM:SS:FF format." },
                    tcOut: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Timecode OUT in HH:MM:SS:FF format." },
                    duration: { type: Type.STRING, description: "Duration in HH:MM:SS:FF format." },
                    description: { type: Type.STRING, description: `Visual description in ${language}.` },
                    action: { type: Type.STRING, description: `Main action in ${language}.` },
                    audio: { type: Type.STRING, description: `Audio description in ${language}.` },
                  }, required: ['tcIn', 'tcOut', 'duration', 'description', 'action', 'audio'],
                },
              },
            }, required: ['shots'],
          },
        },
      };

      if (cacheName) {
        modelConfig.cachedContent = cacheName;
      }

      const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel(modelConfig);

      const parts = cacheName
        ? [{ text: prompt }]
        : [{ fileData: { fileUri, mimeType } }, { text: prompt }];

      return await model.generateContent({
        contents: [{ role: 'user', parts }]
      });
    }, 3, 2500); // Higher retry delay for expensive shotlist calls

    if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

    const rawText = response.response.text();
    let result;
    try {
      result = safeJsonParse<{ shots: Omit<Shot, 'nr' | 'thumbnail'>[] }>(rawText || '');
    } catch (parseError) {
      console.error("[GEMINI_DEBUG] Shot List JSON Parse Failed. Raw Response Preview:", rawText.slice(0, 1000));
      throw parseError; // Re-throw to trigger the main catch
    }

    if (!result || !Array.isArray(result.shots)) {
      console.warn("[GEMINI_DEBUG] Gemini returned invalid shot list structure:", result);
      return [];
    }

    // Force sequential numbering to prevent duplicate IDs breaking the UI
    return result.shots.map((shot, index) => ({
      ...shot,
      nr: index + 1 // Guaranteed unique ID
    }));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("[GEMINI_DEBUG] Shotlist generation failed details:", e);
    if (e instanceof Error && e.message === ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED) throw e;
    throw new Error("Shotlist generation failed.");
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// HYBRID WORKFLOW: ENRICHMENT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export async function enrichShotList(
  fileUri: string,
  mimeType: string,
  shots: Omit<Shot, 'nr' | 'thumbnail'>[],
  language: string,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  progressCallback: (message: string, percentage: number) => void,
  cacheName?: string
): Promise<Omit<Shot, 'nr' | 'thumbnail'>[]> {

  progressCallback('Enriching shot descriptions with AI...', 10);

  const shotListContext = JSON.stringify(shots.map((s, i) => ({ id: i, start: s.tcIn, end: s.tcOut })));

  const prompt = `
    You are an expert film editor assistant. I have a list of ${shots.length} shots with exact timecodes.
    Your task is to watch the video and provide a brief but descriptive "Visual Description" and "Main Action" for EACH shot.
    - Be precise.
    - Use professional film terminology.
    - Output MUST be a valid JSON array of objects, where each object corresponds to the input shot ID.
    - Language: ${language}

    Input Shot List:
    ${shotListContext}

    Output Schema:
    Array<{ id: number, description: string, action: string, audio: string }>
  `;

  try {
    const response = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
      if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

      const modelConfig: any = {
        model: 'gemini-2.5-flash',
        generationConfig: {
          responseMimeType: 'application/json',
          responseSchema: {
            type: Type.ARRAY,
            items: {
              type: Type.OBJECT,
              properties: {
                id: { type: Type.NUMBER },
                description: { type: Type.STRING },
                action: { type: Type.STRING },
                audio: { type: Type.STRING }
              },
              required: ['id', 'description', 'action', 'audio']
            }
          }
        }
      };

      if (cacheName) {
        modelConfig.cachedContent = cacheName;
      }

      const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel(modelConfig);

      const parts = cacheName
        ? [{ text: prompt }]
        : [{ fileData: { fileUri, mimeType } }, { text: prompt }];

      return await model.generateContent({
        contents: [{ role: 'user', parts: parts }]
      });
    }, 3, 2000);

    if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
    const enrichedData = safeJsonParse<any[]>(response.response.text() || '');

    const enrichmentMap = new Map((enrichedData || []).map(e => [e.id, e]));

    return shots.map((shot, index) => {
      const enrichment = enrichmentMap.get(index);
      if (enrichment) {
        return {
          ...shot,
          description: enrichment.description || shot.description,
          action: enrichment.action || shot.action,
          audio: enrichment.audio || shot.audio
        };
      }
      return shot;
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error enriching shot list:", error);
    return shots;
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TEMPLATE CONVERTER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export const convertTemplate = async (
  sourceFileUri: string,
  targetFileUri: string,
  sourceMimeType: string,
  targetMimeType: string,
  originalFileName: string,
  targetTemplateName: string,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  progressCallback: (message: string, percentage: number) => void
): Promise<TemplateConverterResult> => {

  progressCallback('Analyzing template structure...', 20);

  const prompt = `
    You are an expert document formatter and layout specialist.
    
    I have provided two files:
    1. A **Source File** (first file) containing specific content/text.
    2. A **Target File** (second file) containing a specific layout, format, or style (e.g., a specific screenplay format, a report style, a invoice layout).

    **Your Task:**
    Extract **all** the content/text from the **Source File** and rewrite it so that it perfectly matches the structural, stylistic, and formatting rules of the **Target File**.
    
    - **Do not** change the meaning of the source content.
    - **Do not** use the specific content of the Target file (it is just a blueprint).
    - **Do** adopt the Target's headers, indentation, bullet styles, font hierarchy (simulated in Markdown), and sectioning.
    - If the Target is a screenplay, format the Source as a screenplay.
    - If the Target is a technical report, format the Source as a technical report.
    
    **Output:**
    Provide the final result as a raw Markdown string that replicates the visual structure of the Target as closely as possible.
  `;

  if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

  try {
    const response = await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
      if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
      const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
        model: 'gemini-2.5-pro', // Pro for better complex formatting
        generationConfig: {
          temperature: 0.2, // Low temp for precision
          responseMimeType: 'text/plain', // We want raw text/markdown, NOT JSON, for the document content
        }
      });

      return await model.generateContent({
        contents: [{
          role: 'user',
          parts: [
            { fileData: { fileUri: sourceFileUri, mimeType: sourceMimeType } },
            { fileData: { fileUri: targetFileUri, mimeType: targetMimeType } },
            { text: prompt }
          ]
        }]
      });
    }, 3, 2000);

    if (signal.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
    progressCallback('Finalizing conversion...', 90);

    const convertedContent = response.response.text() || "Conversion failed to generate text.";

    return {
      convertedContent,
      originalFileName,
      targetTemplateName
    };

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error converting template:", error);
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED) throw error;
    throw new Error('Template conversion failed.');
  }
};

--- START OF FILE: services/gcsUploadService.ts ---
import { ERROR_IDS, UploadProgress } from '../types';

export interface GCSUploadOptions {
    file: File;
    bucketName: string; // Kept for interface compatibility, but unused (backend decides bucket)
    apiKey: string;     // Unused
    accessToken?: string; // Unused
    onProgress: (progress: UploadProgress) => void;
    signal?: AbortSignal;
}

const API_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:8080';

/**
 * Upload a file specifically to GCS using a Backend-Signed URL.
 * This bypasses the need for frontend tokens and avoiding 4MB/1MB limitations.
 * Returns the gs:// URI (e.g., gs://my-bucket/my-video.mp4)
 */
export async function uploadFileToGCS(options: GCSUploadOptions): Promise<string> {
    const { file, onProgress, signal } = options;

    try {
        // Phase 1: Get Signed URL from Backend
        onProgress({
            phase: 'initializing',
            message: 'Requesting secure upload link...',
            percentage: 1,
            bytesUploaded: 0,
            totalBytes: file.size,
        });

        const urlResponse = await fetch(`${API_URL}/api/upload-url`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                fileName: file.name,
                contentType: file.type
            }),
            signal
        });

        if (!urlResponse.ok) {
            const errText = await urlResponse.text();
            throw new Error(`Failed to get upload URL: ${urlResponse.status} ${errText}`);
        }

        const { uploadUrl, gcsUri } = await urlResponse.json();

        // Phase 2: Direct Upload to GCS using XMLHttpRequest (for progress)
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
            xhr.open('PUT', uploadUrl, true);
            xhr.setRequestHeader('Content-Type', file.type);

            // Progress Handler
            xhr.upload.onprogress = (event) => {
                if (event.lengthComputable) {
                    const percent = Math.round((event.loaded / event.total) * 99); // Cap at 99 until done
                    onProgress({
                        phase: 'uploading',
                        message: `Uploading to Cloud Storage ${formatBytes(event.loaded)} / ${formatBytes(event.total)}...`,
                        percentage: percent,
                        bytesUploaded: event.loaded,
                        totalBytes: event.total,
                    });
                }
            };

            // Completion Handler
            xhr.onload = () => {
                if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
                    onProgress({
                        phase: 'processing', // Move to next phase
                        message: 'Upload complete. Processing...',
                        percentage: 100,
                        bytesUploaded: file.size,
                        totalBytes: file.size,
                    });
                    resolve(gcsUri);
                } else {
                    reject(new Error(`GCS Upload Failed: ${xhr.status} ${xhr.statusText}`));
                }
            };

            // Error Handler
            xhr.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Network Error during GCS Upload'));

            // Abort Handler
            if (signal) {
                signal.addEventListener('abort', () => {
                    xhr.abort();
                    reject(new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED));
                });
            }

            xhr.send(file);
        });

    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error('GCS Upload Failed:', error);
        if (signal?.aborted || error.message === ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED) {
            throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);
        }
        throw new Error(`Failed to upload video: ${error.message}`);
    }
}

function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
    if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
    const k = 1024;
    const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
    const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
    return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
}

import JSZip from 'jszip';
import { saveAs } from 'file-saver';
import { Packer } from 'docx';
import {
    generateShotListDoc,
    generateSynopsisDoc,
    generateDialogueListDoc,
    generateBioDoc,
    generateFaunaLogDoc,
    generateGraphicsLogDoc,
    generateTechSpecsDoc,
    generateAsBroadcastScriptDoc
} from './documentExportService';
import {
    ShotListResult,
    Synopsis,
    TranscriptionResult,
    Bio,
    SpeciesSighting,
    GraphicsAnalysisResult,
    VideoMetadata,
    ProjectMetadata,
    FullAnalysisResult,
    AllJobs
} from '../types';

/**
 * Batch Export Service
 * Zips all available analysis results into a single download package.
 */

export async function exportAllDeliverables(
    jobs: AllJobs,
    projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata,
    videoFileName: string
) {
    const zip = new JSZip();
    const baseName = videoFileName.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, "");
    const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    const folderName = `${baseName}_Deliverables_${dateStr}`;

    const root = zip.folder(folderName);
    if (!root) throw new Error("Failed to create zip folder");

    const promises: Promise<void>[] = [];

    // 1. Technical Specs
    if (jobs.techspecs.result) {
        promises.push(addToZip(root, 'TechSpecs.docx', generateTechSpecsDoc(jobs.techspecs.result, projectMetadata)));
    }
    // Fallback: Check full_analysis? (Not strictly implemented in full analysis state yet, usually calculated in App)

    // 2. Shot List
    if (jobs.shotlist.result) {
        promises.push(addToZip(root, 'ShotList.docx', generateShotListDoc(jobs.shotlist.result.shots, projectMetadata)));
    }

    // 3. Transcription / Dialogue List
    if (jobs.transcribe.result) {
        promises.push(addToZip(root, 'DialogueList.docx', generateDialogueListDoc(jobs.transcribe.result, projectMetadata)));
    }

    // 4. Synopsis
    if (jobs.synopsis.result) {
        promises.push(addToZip(root, 'Synopsis.docx', generateSynopsisDoc(jobs.synopsis.result, projectMetadata)));
    }

    // 5. Bio
    if (jobs.bio.result) {
        promises.push(addToZip(root, 'TalentBios.docx', generateBioDoc(jobs.bio.result, projectMetadata)));
    }

    // 6. Graphics Log
    if (jobs.graphics.result) {
        promises.push(addToZip(root, 'GraphicsLog.docx', generateGraphicsLogDoc(jobs.graphics.result, projectMetadata)));
    }

    if (jobs.fauna.result) {
        promises.push(addToZip(root, 'FaunaLog.docx', generateFaunaLogDoc(jobs.fauna.result, projectMetadata)));
    }

    // 8. AS-BROADCAST SCRIPT (Combined)
    if (jobs.transcribe.result && jobs.shotlist.result) {
        const asBroadcastData = {
            transcription: jobs.transcribe.result,
            shotList: jobs.shotlist.result,
            graphics: jobs.graphics.result || undefined
        };
        promises.push(addToZip(root, 'AsBroadcastScript.docx', generateAsBroadcastScriptDoc(asBroadcastData, projectMetadata)));
    }

    // Wait for all generations
    await Promise.all(promises);

    // Generate zip
    const content = await zip.generateAsync({ type: "blob" });
    saveAs(content, `${folderName}.zip`);
}

async function addToZip(folder: JSZip, filename: string, docCreator: any) {
    try {
        // Doc creator returns a docx Document object
        const blob = await Packer.toBlob(docCreator);
        folder.file(filename, blob);
    } catch (e) {
        console.error(`Failed to generate ${filename}`, e);
    }
}

--- START OF FILE: services/db.ts ---

import { SavedAnalysis, AnalysisMode } from '../types';

const DB_NAME = 'ClipAnalyzerDB';
const STORE_NAME = 'analyses';
const DB_VERSION = 1;

export class AnalysisDatabase {
  private db: IDBDatabase | null = null;

  async init(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.db) return;

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);

      request.onerror = (event) => {
        console.error("Database error:", event);
        reject("Failed to open database");
      };

      request.onupgradeneeded = (event) => {
        const db = (event.target as IDBOpenDBRequest).result;
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_NAME)) {
          const store = db.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME, { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
          store.createIndex('type', 'type', { unique: false });
          store.createIndex('date', 'date', { unique: false });
          store.createIndex('timestamp', 'timestamp', { unique: false });
        }
      };

      request.onsuccess = (event) => {
        this.db = (event.target as IDBOpenDBRequest).result;
        resolve();
      };
    });
  }

  async saveAnalysis(type: AnalysisMode, fileName: string, data: any): Promise<number> {
    await this.init();
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      if (!this.db) return reject("Database not initialized");

      const transaction = this.db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
      const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);

      const analysis: Omit<SavedAnalysis, 'id'> = {
        type,
        fileName,
        date: new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        data
      };

      const request = store.add(analysis);

      request.onsuccess = (event) => {
        resolve((event.target as IDBRequest).result as number);
      };

      request.onerror = () => {
        reject("Failed to save analysis");
      };
    });
  }

  async getAllAnalyses(): Promise<SavedAnalysis[]> {
    await this.init();
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      if (!this.db) return reject("Database not initialized");

      const transaction = this.db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readonly');
      const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
      const index = store.index('timestamp'); // Sort by timestamp
      
      // Use openCursor to get results in reverse order (newest first)
      const request = index.openCursor(null, 'prev');
      const results: SavedAnalysis[] = [];

      request.onsuccess = (event) => {
        const cursor = (event.target as IDBRequest).result as IDBCursorWithValue;
        if (cursor) {
          results.push(cursor.value);
          cursor.continue();
        } else {
          resolve(results);
        }
      };

      request.onerror = () => {
        reject("Failed to fetch analyses");
      };
    });
  }

  async deleteAnalysis(id: number): Promise<void> {
    await this.init();
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      if (!this.db) return reject("Database not initialized");

      const transaction = this.db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
      const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
      const request = store.delete(id);

      request.onsuccess = () => resolve();
      request.onerror = () => reject("Failed to delete item");
    });
  }
}

export const dbService = new AnalysisDatabase();

--- START OF FILE: services/ffmpegService.ts ---
export interface SceneDetectionResult {
    scenes: number[]; // Array of timestamps in seconds
}

export interface KeyframeExtractionResult {
    blobs: Blob[];
}

export interface VideoMetadataResult {
    container: string;
    duration: string;
    bitrate: string;
    streams: string[];
    rawLogs: string;
}

class FFmpegService {
    private worker: Worker | null = null;

    private getWorker(): Worker {
        if (!this.worker) {
            this.worker = new Worker(new URL('../workers/ffmpeg.worker.ts', import.meta.url), {
                type: 'module',
            });
        }
        return this.worker;
    }

    public detectScenes(videoFile: File, threshold: number = 0.4, onStatus?: (msg: string) => void): Promise<SceneDetectionResult> {
        return this.runCommand('DETECT_SCENES', { videoFile, threshold }, onStatus) as Promise<SceneDetectionResult>;
    }

    public extractKeyframes(videoFile: File, onStatus?: (msg: string) => void): Promise<KeyframeExtractionResult> {
        return this.runCommand('EXTRACT_KEYFRAMES', { videoFile }, onStatus) as Promise<KeyframeExtractionResult>;
    }

    public getMetadata(videoFile: File, onStatus?: (msg: string) => void): Promise<VideoMetadataResult> {
        return this.runCommand('GET_METADATA', { videoFile }, onStatus) as Promise<VideoMetadataResult>;
    }

    private runCommand(type: string, data: any, onStatus?: (msg: string) => void): Promise<any> {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const worker = this.getWorker();

            const handleMessage = (e: MessageEvent) => {
                const { type: msgType, message, payload } = e.data;

                if (msgType === 'status') {
                    if (onStatus) onStatus(message);
                } else if (msgType === 'result') {
                    worker.removeEventListener('message', handleMessage);
                    resolve(payload);
                } else if (msgType === 'error') {
                    worker.removeEventListener('message', handleMessage);
                    reject(message);
                }
            };

            worker.addEventListener('message', handleMessage);
            worker.postMessage({ type, ...data });
        });
    }

    public terminate() {
        if (this.worker) {
            this.worker.terminate();
            this.worker = null;
        }
    }
}

export const ffmpegService = new FFmpegService();

/**
 * Document Export Service
 * Generates professional Word (.docx) documents for film deliverables.
 */

import { Document, Packer, Paragraph, Table, TableRow, TableCell, WidthType, BorderStyle, HeadingLevel, TextRun, Header, Footer, AlignmentType, PageBreak } from 'docx';
import { saveAs } from 'file-saver';
import { Shot, Synopsis, TranscriptionResult, ProjectMetadata, Bio, SpeciesSighting, GraphicsAnalysisResult, ShotListResult } from '../types';
import { applyTimecodeOffset } from '../utils/tableUtils';

// Type definitions for passing data to the exporter
export interface ExportData {
    type: 'shotlist' | 'synopsis' | 'transcription' | 'graphics' | 'techspecs' | 'bio' | 'fauna' | 'asbroadcast';
    data: any; // Shot[], Synopsis, TranscriptionResult, Bio[], SpeciesSighting[], GraphicsAnalysisResult etc.
    projectMetadata: ProjectMetadata; // Production info
    fileName: string; // Base filename
    frameRate?: number; // Video frame rate for accurate timecode calc
}

/**
 * GENERATES A PROFESSIONAL COVER PAGE
 */
function generateCoverPage(meta: ProjectMetadata, title: string): Paragraph[] {
    const style = getStyle(meta.exportTemplate);
    const dateStr = meta.date || new Date().toLocaleDateString();

    return [
        new Paragraph({ text: "", spacing: { before: 5000 } }), // Top Spacer
        new Paragraph({
            children: [new TextRun({ text: meta.productionCompany || "PRODUCTION COMPANY", font: style.font, size: 28, bold: true })],
            alignment: AlignmentType.CENTER,
            spacing: { after: 300 }
        }),
        new Paragraph({
            children: [new TextRun({ text: meta.seriesTitle || "SERIES TITLE", font: style.font, size: 48, bold: true })],
            alignment: AlignmentType.CENTER,
            spacing: { after: 200 }
        }),
        new Paragraph({
            children: [new TextRun({ text: meta.episodeTitle || "EPISODE TITLE", font: style.font, size: 36, italics: true })],
            alignment: AlignmentType.CENTER,
            spacing: { after: 100 }
        }),
        new Paragraph({
            children: [new TextRun({ text: meta.episodeNumber ? `Episode ${meta.episodeNumber}` : "", font: style.font, size: 24 })],
            alignment: AlignmentType.CENTER,
            spacing: { after: 800 }
        }),
        new Paragraph({
            children: [new TextRun({ text: title.toUpperCase(), font: style.font, size: 32, bold: true, underline: { type: "single", color: "000000" } })],
            alignment: AlignmentType.CENTER,
            spacing: { after: 800 }
        }),
        new Paragraph({
            children: [
                new TextRun({ text: `Director: ${meta.director || "N/A"}\n`, font: style.font, size: 24 }),
                new TextRun({ text: `Producer: ${meta.producer || "N/A"}\n`, font: style.font, size: 24 }),
                new TextRun({ text: `Editor: ${meta.editor || "N/A"}\n`, font: style.font, size: 24 }),
                new TextRun({ text: `Date: ${dateStr}`, font: style.font, size: 24 })
            ],
            alignment: AlignmentType.CENTER,
            spacing: { line: 360 } // 1.5 spacing
        }),
        new Paragraph({
            children: [new PageBreak()]
        })
    ];
}

/**
 * Main export function
 */
export async function exportToWord(exportData: ExportData) {
    const { type, data, projectMetadata, fileName } = exportData;
    let doc: Document;

    switch (type) {
        case 'shotlist':
            doc = generateShotListDoc(data as Shot[], projectMetadata, exportData.frameRate);
            break;
        case 'synopsis':
            doc = generateSynopsisDoc(data as Synopsis, projectMetadata);
            break;
        case 'transcription':
            doc = generateDialogueListDoc(data as TranscriptionResult, projectMetadata, exportData.frameRate);
            break;
        case 'bio':
            doc = generateBioDoc(data as Bio[], projectMetadata);
            break;
        case 'fauna':
            doc = generateFaunaLogDoc(data as SpeciesSighting[], projectMetadata, exportData.frameRate);
            break;
        case 'graphics':
            doc = generateGraphicsLogDoc(data as GraphicsAnalysisResult, projectMetadata, exportData.frameRate);
            break;
        case 'techspecs':
            doc = generateTechSpecsDoc(data as any, projectMetadata); // data is VideoMetadata
            break;
        case 'asbroadcast':
            doc = generateAsBroadcastScriptDoc(data as any, projectMetadata, exportData.frameRate);
            break;
        default:
            console.warn("Unsupported export type:", type);
            return;
    }

    // Generate and save
    const blob = await Packer.toBlob(doc);
    saveAs(blob, `${fileName}_${type.toUpperCase()}_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.docx`);
}

// ... existing code ...

/**
 * TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS GENERATOR
 */
export function generateTechSpecsDoc(video: any, meta: ProjectMetadata): Document {
    // Helper to format bytes
    const formatSize = (bytes: number) => {
        if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
        const k = 1024;
        const classes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
        const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
        return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + classes[i];
    };

    // Helper for Timecode & Scan Type
    const tcFormat = (Math.abs(video.frameRate - 29.97) < 0.01 || Math.abs(video.frameRate - 59.94) < 0.01) ? "Drop-Frame (SMPTE 12M)" : "Non-Drop-Frame";
    const scanType = "Progressive";
    const dateStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { year: 'numeric', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' });

    // Table Data
    const specs = [
        ["File Format / Container", video.format || "Unknown"],
        ["Video Codec", video.codec || "Unknown"],
        ["Resolution", `${video.width} x ${video.height}`],
        ["Frame Rate", `${video.frameRate.toFixed(3)} fps`],
        ["Aspect Ratio", video.aspectRatio || "Unknown"],
        ["Scan Type", scanType],
        ["Audio Channels", video.hasAudio ? "Stereo (2.0) [Assumed]" : "None"],
        ["Sample Rate", "48 kHz [Standard]"],
        ["Total Runtime", (video.duration && !isNaN(video.duration)) ? new Date(video.duration * 1000).toISOString().substr(11, 12) + " (Approx)" : "Unknown"],
        ["File Size", formatSize(video.fileSize)],
        ["Timecode Format", tcFormat]
    ];

    const tableRows = specs.map(([label, value]) =>
        new TableRow({
            children: [
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: label, bold: true })] })], width: { size: 40, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE } }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph(value)], width: { size: 60, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE } })
            ]
        })
    );

    return new Document({
        sections: [{
            headers: { default: createHeader(meta, "TECHNICAL SPECS") },
            footers: { default: createFooter(meta) },
            children: [
                ...generateCoverPage(meta, "TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS REPORT"),
                new Paragraph({ text: "TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS", heading: HeadingLevel.HEADING_2 }),
                new Paragraph({ text: "" }),

                new Table({
                    width: { size: 100, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE },
                    rows: tableRows
                }),

                new Paragraph({ text: "" }),
                new Paragraph({ text: "COMPLIANCE CHECK", heading: HeadingLevel.HEADING_2 }),
                new Paragraph({ text: "✓ Resolution meets HD Broadcast Standards (1920x1080+)" }),
                new Paragraph({ text: `✓ Frame Rate is standard (${video.frameRate.toFixed(2)})` }),
                new Paragraph({ text: `✓ Timecode format is compliant (${tcFormat})` })
            ]
        }]
    });
}

/**
 * SHOT LIST GENERATOR
 */
export function generateShotListDoc(shots: Shot[], meta: ProjectMetadata, frameRate: number = 25): Document {
    const style = getStyle(meta.exportTemplate);
    // Apply offsets
    const processedShots = shots.map(s => ({
        ...s,
        tcIn: applyTimecodeOffset(s.tcIn, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate),
        tcOut: applyTimecodeOffset(s.tcOut, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate)
    }));

    return new Document({
        sections: [{
            headers: { default: createHeader(meta, "SHOT LIST") },
            footers: { default: createFooter(meta) },
            children: [
                ...generateCoverPage(meta, "SHOT LIST"),
                createShotListTable(processedShots, style)
            ],
        }],
    });
}

function createShotListTable(shots: Shot[], style: { font: string, size: number }): Table {
    const headerRow = new TableRow({
        children: ['Shot #', 'TC IN', 'TC OUT', 'Duration', 'Description', 'Action'].map(text =>
            new TableCell({
                width: { size: 100 / 6, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE },
                children: [new Paragraph({
                    children: [new TextRun({ text, bold: true, font: style.font, size: style.size })]
                })],
                shading: { fill: "E0E0E0" }
            })
        ),
    });

    const rows = shots.map((shot, index) =>
        new TableRow({
            children: [
                (shot.nr || index + 1).toString(),
                shot.tcIn || "00:00:00:00",
                shot.tcOut || "00:00:00:00",
                shot.duration || "00:00:00:00",
                shot.description || "",
                shot.action || ""
            ].map(text => new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text, font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] })),
        })
    );

    return new Table({
        width: { size: 100, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE },
        rows: [headerRow, ...rows],
    });
}

/**
 * SYNOPSIS GENERATOR
 */
export function generateSynopsisDoc(synopsis: Synopsis, meta: ProjectMetadata): Document {
    const style = getStyle(meta.exportTemplate);
    return new Document({
        sections: [{
            headers: { default: createHeader(meta, "SYNOPSIS") },
            footers: { default: createFooter(meta) },
            children: [
                ...generateCoverPage(meta, "SYNOPSIS"),

                new Paragraph({
                    children: [new TextRun({ text: "LOGLINE", font: style.font, size: style.headerSize, bold: true })],
                    heading: HeadingLevel.HEADING_2
                }),
                new Paragraph({
                    children: [new TextRun({ text: synopsis.oneLine, font: style.font, size: style.size })]
                }),
                new Paragraph({ text: "" }),

                new Paragraph({
                    children: [new TextRun({ text: "SYNOPSIS", font: style.font, size: style.headerSize, bold: true })],
                    heading: HeadingLevel.HEADING_2
                }),
                new Paragraph({
                    children: [new TextRun({ text: synopsis.fullSynopsis, font: style.font, size: style.size })]
                }),
                new Paragraph({ text: "" }),

                new Paragraph({
                    children: [new TextRun({ text: "KEY THEMES", font: style.font, size: style.headerSize, bold: true })],
                    heading: HeadingLevel.HEADING_3
                }),
                new Paragraph({
                    children: [new TextRun({ text: synopsis.keyThemes, font: style.font, size: style.size })]
                }),
            ]
        }]
    });
}

/**
 * DIALOGUE LIST GENERATOR
 */
export function generateDialogueListDoc(transcription: TranscriptionResult, meta: ProjectMetadata, frameRate: number = 25): Document {
    const style = getStyle(meta.exportTemplate);
    const dialogueParagraphs = transcription.segments.map(seg => {
        const speakerName = (transcription.speakerMap && transcription.speakerMap[seg.speaker]) || seg.speaker;
        const status = seg.isOnScreen === false ? ' (V.O.)' : ''; // Default to On Screen if undefined

        const tcStart = applyTimecodeOffset(seg.startTime, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate);
        const tcEnd = applyTimecodeOffset(seg.endTime, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate);

        return [
            // Timecode Range
            new Paragraph({
                children: [new TextRun({ text: `${tcStart} - ${tcEnd}`, bold: true, size: Math.max(16, style.size - 4), font: 'Courier New' })], // Always Courier for TC
                spacing: { before: 200 }
            }),
            // Speaker
            new Paragraph({
                children: [new TextRun({ text: `${speakerName.toUpperCase()}${status}`, bold: true, font: style.font, size: style.size })],
                alignment: AlignmentType.CENTER, // Center the character name
                spacing: { before: 100, after: 0 }
            }),
            // Dialogue
            new Paragraph({
                children: [new TextRun({ text: seg.transcript, font: style.font, size: style.size })],
                indent: { left: 720, right: 720 }, // Indent dialogue for script look (~0.5 inch)
                spacing: { after: 200 }
            })
        ];
    }).flat();

    return new Document({
        sections: [{
            headers: { default: createHeader(meta, "DIALOGUE LIST") },
            footers: { default: createFooter(meta) },
            children: [
                ...generateCoverPage(meta, "DIALOGUE LIST"),
                ...dialogueParagraphs
            ]
        }]
    });
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SHARED COMPONENTS & STYLES
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const TEMPLATE_STYLES: Record<string, { font: string, size: number, headerSize: number, titleSize: number }> = {
    'BBC': { font: 'Courier New', size: 24, headerSize: 20, titleSize: 28 }, // 12pt body (half-points)
    'PBS': { font: 'Arial', size: 24, headerSize: 20, titleSize: 28 },
    'ARTE': { font: 'Helvetica', size: 22, headerSize: 18, titleSize: 26 },
    'ZDF': { font: 'Arial', size: 22, headerSize: 18, titleSize: 26 },
    'FranceTV': { font: 'Times New Roman', size: 24, headerSize: 20, titleSize: 28 },
    'ORF': { font: 'Arial', size: 22, headerSize: 18, titleSize: 26 },
    'Generic': { font: 'Calibri', size: 22, headerSize: 18, titleSize: 28 },
    'Custom': { font: 'Calibri', size: 22, headerSize: 18, titleSize: 28 }
};

function getStyle(template?: string) {
    return TEMPLATE_STYLES[template || 'Generic'] || TEMPLATE_STYLES['Generic'];
}

function createHeader(meta: ProjectMetadata, docType: string): Header {
    const style = getStyle(meta.exportTemplate);
    return new Header({
        children: [
            new Paragraph({
                children: [
                    new TextRun({
                        text: meta.seriesTitle ? `${meta.seriesTitle} - ${meta.episodeTitle}` : (meta.episodeTitle || "Untitled Project"),
                        bold: true,
                        font: style.font,
                        size: style.headerSize
                    }),
                    new TextRun({
                        text: ` | ${docType}`,
                        italics: true,
                        font: style.font,
                        size: style.headerSize
                    })
                ],
                border: { bottom: { color: "auto", space: 1, style: BorderStyle.SINGLE, size: 6 } }
            })
        ],
    });
}

function createFooter(meta: ProjectMetadata): Footer {
    const style = getStyle(meta.exportTemplate);
    return new Footer({
        children: [
            new Paragraph({
                children: [
                    new TextRun({
                        text: meta.productionCompany || "Generated by Gemini Film Analyzer",
                        font: style.font,
                        size: 16 // Small footer
                    }),
                    new TextRun({ text: " | Page ", children: ["PAGE"], font: style.font, size: 16 }),
                    new TextRun({ text: " of ", font: style.font, size: 16 }),
                    new TextRun({ children: ["NUMPAGES"], font: style.font, size: 16 })
                ],
                alignment: AlignmentType.RIGHT,
                border: { top: { color: "auto", space: 1, style: BorderStyle.SINGLE, size: 6 } }
            })
        ]
    });
}


/**
 * BIO GENERATOR
 */
export function generateBioDoc(bios: Bio[], meta: ProjectMetadata): Document {
    const style = getStyle(meta.exportTemplate);
    const paragraphs = bios.map(bio => [
        new Paragraph({
            children: [new TextRun({ text: bio.fullName.toUpperCase(), font: style.font, size: style.headerSize, bold: true })],
            heading: HeadingLevel.HEADING_2,
            spacing: { before: 200 }
        }),
        new Paragraph({
            children: [new TextRun({ text: bio.title, italics: true, font: style.font, size: style.size })],
            spacing: { after: 100 }
        }),
        new Paragraph({
            children: [new TextRun({ text: bio.biography, font: style.font, size: style.size })]
        })
    ]).flat();

    return new Document({
        sections: [{
            headers: { default: createHeader(meta, "BIOGRAPHIES") },
            footers: { default: createFooter(meta) },
            children: [
                ...generateCoverPage(meta, "BIOGRAPHIES"),
                ...paragraphs
            ]
        }]
    });
}
