export function generateFaunaLogDoc(sightings: SpeciesSighting[], meta: ProjectMetadata, frameRate: number = 25): Document {
    const style = getStyle(meta.exportTemplate);

    const tableRows = sightings.map(s =>
        new TableRow({
            children: [
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: s.commonName, font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: s.latinName, font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: s.behavior || '', font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: s.iucnStatus || '', font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: applyTimecodeOffset(s.timecode, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate), font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: s.category || '', font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
            ]
        })
    );

    const headerRow = new TableRow({
        children: ['Common Name', 'Latin Name', 'Behavior', 'IUCN', 'Timecode', 'Category'].map(t =>
            new TableCell({
                children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: t, bold: true, font: style.font, size: style.size })] })],
                shading: { fill: "E0E0E0" }
            })
        )
    });

    return new Document({
        sections: [{
            headers: { default: createHeader(meta, "FAUNA LOG") },
            footers: { default: createFooter(meta) },
            children: [
                ...generateCoverPage(meta, "SPECIES SIGHTINGS LOG"),
                new Table({
                    width: { size: 100, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE },
                    rows: [headerRow, ...tableRows]
                })
            ]
        }]
    });
}

/**
 * GRAPHICS LOG GENERATOR
 */
export function generateGraphicsLogDoc(result: GraphicsAnalysisResult, meta: ProjectMetadata, frameRate: number = 25): Document {
    const style = getStyle(meta.exportTemplate);

    const graphicsRows = (result.graphics || []).map(g =>
        new TableRow({
            children: [
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: "Graphic", font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: g.description, font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: applyTimecodeOffset(g.timecodeIn, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate), font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: applyTimecodeOffset(g.timecodeOut, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate), font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
            ]
        })
    );

    const lowerThirdRows = (result.lowerThirds || []).map(lt =>
        new TableRow({
            children: [
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: "Lower Third", font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: lt.text, font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: applyTimecodeOffset(lt.timecodeIn, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate), font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
                new TableCell({ children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: applyTimecodeOffset(lt.timecodeOut, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate), font: style.font, size: style.size })] })] }),
            ]
        })
    );

    const headerRow = new TableRow({
        children: ['Type', 'Description/Text', 'TC IN', 'TC OUT'].map(t =>
            new TableCell({
                children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: t, bold: true, font: style.font, size: style.size })] })],
                shading: { fill: "E0E0E0" }
            })
        )
    });

    return new Document({
        sections: [{
            headers: { default: createHeader(meta, "GRAPHICS LOG") },
            footers: { default: createFooter(meta) },
            children: [
                ...generateCoverPage(meta, "GRAPHICS & LOWER THIRDS LOG"),
                new Paragraph({
                    children: [new TextRun({ text: `Runtime: ${result.runtime || 'N/A'}`, font: style.font, size: style.size })],
                    alignment: AlignmentType.CENTER
                }),
                new Paragraph({ text: "" }),
                new Table({
                    width: { size: 100, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE },
                    rows: [headerRow, ...graphicsRows, ...lowerThirdRows]
                })
            ]
        }]
    });
}

/**
 * AS-BROADCAST SCRIPT GENERATOR
 */
export function generateAsBroadcastScriptDoc(data: { transcription: TranscriptionResult, shotList: ShotListResult, graphics?: GraphicsAnalysisResult }, meta: ProjectMetadata, frameRate: number = 25): Document {
    const style = getStyle(meta.exportTemplate);

    // Combine events
    interface ScriptEvent {
        type: 'shot' | 'dialogue' | 'graphic';
        tcIn: string;
        tcOut: string;
        content: string; // Description or Dialogue
        details?: string; // Action or Speaker
    }

    const events: ScriptEvent[] = [];

    // Process Shots
    if (data.shotList && data.shotList.shots) {
        data.shotList.shots.forEach(s => {
            events.push({
                type: 'shot',
                tcIn: applyTimecodeOffset(s.tcIn, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                tcOut: applyTimecodeOffset(s.tcOut, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                content: s.description,
                details: s.action
            });
        });
    }

    // Process Dialogue
    if (data.transcription && data.transcription.segments) {
        data.transcription.segments.forEach(ts => {
            const speaker = (data.transcription.speakerMap && data.transcription.speakerMap[ts.speaker]) || ts.speaker;
            const status = ts.isOnScreen === false ? ' (V.O.)' : '';
            events.push({
                type: 'dialogue',
                tcIn: applyTimecodeOffset(ts.startTime, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                tcOut: applyTimecodeOffset(ts.endTime, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                content: ts.transcript,
                details: speaker.toUpperCase() + status
            });
        });
    }

    // Process Graphics
    if (data.graphics && data.graphics.graphics) {
        data.graphics.graphics.forEach(g => {
            events.push({
                type: 'graphic',
                tcIn: applyTimecodeOffset(g.timecodeIn, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                tcOut: applyTimecodeOffset(g.timecodeOut, meta.timecodeStart, frameRate),
                content: `GRAPHIC: ${g.description}`,
                details: 'ON SCREEN'
            });
        });
    }

    // Sort by Timecode In
    events.sort((a, b) => a.tcIn.localeCompare(b.tcIn));

    // Create Table
    const tableRows = events.map(e => {
        const isDialogue = e.type === 'dialogue';

        // Video Column Content
        const videoContent = isDialogue ? [] : [
            new Paragraph({
                children: [
                    new TextRun({ text: e.type.toUpperCase(), bold: true, font: style.font, size: Math.max(14, style.size - 6) }),
                    new TextRun({ text: "\n" }),
                    new TextRun({ text: e.content, font: style.font, size: style.size })
                ]
            })
        ];

        // Audio Column Content
        const audioContent = isDialogue ? [
            new Paragraph({
                children: [
                    new TextRun({ text: e.details || "", bold: true, font: style.font, size: style.size }),
                    new TextRun({ text: "\n" }),
                    new TextRun({ text: e.content, font: style.font, size: style.size })
                ]
            })
        ] : [];

        return new TableRow({
            children: [
                new TableCell({ // TC
                    width: { size: 15, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE },
                    children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: e.tcIn, font: 'Courier New', size: Math.max(16, style.size - 4) })] })],
                    verticalAlign: AlignmentType.CENTER
                }),
                new TableCell({ // VIDEO
                    width: { size: 42, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE },
                    children: videoContent,
                    verticalAlign: AlignmentType.CENTER
                }),
                new TableCell({ // AUDIO
                    width: { size: 43, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE },
                    children: audioContent,
                    verticalAlign: AlignmentType.CENTER
                })
            ]
        });
    });

    const headerRow = new TableRow({
        children: ['Timecode', 'Video', 'Audio'].map(t =>
            new TableCell({
                children: [new Paragraph({ children: [new TextRun({ text: t, bold: true, font: style.font, size: style.size })] })],
                shading: { fill: "E0E0E0" }
            })
        )
    });

    return new Document({
        sections: [{
            headers: { default: createHeader(meta, "AS-BROADCAST SCRIPT") },
            footers: { default: createFooter(meta) },
            children: [
                ...generateCoverPage(meta, "AS-BROADCAST MASTER SCRIPT"),
                new Table({
                    width: { size: 100, type: WidthType.PERCENTAGE },
                    rows: [headerRow, ...tableRows]
                })
            ]
        }]
    });
}

--- START OF FILE: services/llm/LLMProvider.ts ---

export interface LLMProvider {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    description?: string;

    /**
     * Generates text based on a prompt.
     * Useful for internal logic, summarization, or chat.
     */
    generateText(prompt: string, options?: LLMOptions): Promise<string>;

    /**
     * Generates structured JSON based on a prompt.
     * Critical for application logic (e.g. bio generation, synopsis).
     */
    generateJSON<T>(prompt: string, schema?: any, options?: LLMOptions): Promise<T>;

    /**
     * Analyzes a video file (multimodal).
     * Note: Some providers (like DeepSeek) might not support this natively.
     * They might throw an error or require a "Vision Adapter" (out of scope for now).
     */
    analyzeVideo<T>(
        fileUri: string,
        mimeType: string,
        prompt: string,
        schema?: any,
        options?: LLMOptions
    ): Promise<T>;

    /**
     * Analyzes a single image (Base64).
     * Useful for frame-by-frame analysis.
     */
    analyzeImage(
        base64Data: string,
        mimeType: string,
        prompt: string,
        options?: LLMOptions
    ): Promise<string>;
}

export interface LLMOptions {
    temperature?: number;
    model?: string;
    signal?: AbortSignal;
    cacheName?: string;
    maxRetries?: number;
}

--- END OF FILE: services/llm/LLMProvider.ts ---

--- START OF FILE: services/llm/GeminiProvider.ts ---
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { LLMProvider, LLMOptions } from './LLMProvider';
import { retryWithBackoff } from '../../utils/retryUtils';
import { ERROR_IDS } from '../../types';

// Helper for safe JSON parsing
function safeJsonParse<T>(jsonString: string): T {
    try {
        const cleanString = jsonString.trim().replace(/^```(json)?|```$/g, '');
        if (!cleanString) {
            throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.INVALID_API_RESPONSE);
        }
        return JSON.parse(cleanString);
    } catch (e) {
        console.error("Failed to parse JSON response:", jsonString, e);
        throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.INVALID_API_RESPONSE);
    }
}

export class GeminiProvider implements LLMProvider {
    id = 'gemini';
    name = 'Google Gemini';
    description = 'Multimodal Native (Video, Audio, Text). Best for large context analysis.';

    private genAI: GoogleGenerativeAI;

    constructor(apiKey: string) {
        this.genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(apiKey);
    }

    async generateText(prompt: string, options?: LLMOptions): Promise<string> {
        const modelName = options?.model || 'gemini-2.5-flash';

        return await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
            const model = this.genAI.getGenerativeModel({
                model: modelName,
                generationConfig: {
                    temperature: options?.temperature ?? 0.7,
                }
            });

            const result = await model.generateContent(prompt);
            return result.response.text();
        }, options?.maxRetries ?? 3, 1000);
    }

    async generateJSON<T>(prompt: string, schema?: any, options?: LLMOptions): Promise<T> {
        const modelName = options?.model || 'gemini-2.5-flash';

        // Convert schema to Gemini format if passed (simplified for now, assuming schema is already Gemini-compatible or generic)
        // In a robust implementation, we might need a schema adapter.

        return await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
            const model = this.genAI.getGenerativeModel({
                model: modelName,
                generationConfig: {
                    temperature: options?.temperature ?? 0.2,
                    responseMimeType: 'application/json',
                    responseSchema: schema // Pass strict schema if provided
                }
            });

            const result = await model.generateContent(prompt);
            return safeJsonParse<T>(result.response.text());
        }, options?.maxRetries ?? 3, 1000);
    }

    async analyzeVideo<T>(
        fileUri: string,
        mimeType: string,
        prompt: string,
        schema?: any,
        options?: LLMOptions
    ): Promise<T> {
        const modelName = options?.model || 'gemini-2.5-pro';

        return await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
            if (options?.signal?.aborted) throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.OPERATION_CANCELLED);

            const config: any = {
                model: modelName,
                generationConfig: {
                    temperature: options?.temperature ?? 0.2,
                    responseMimeType: 'application/json',
                    responseSchema: schema
                }
            };

            if (options?.cacheName) {
                config.cachedContent = options.cacheName;
            }

            const model = this.genAI.getGenerativeModel(config);

            const parts = options?.cacheName
                ? [{ text: prompt }]
                : [{ fileData: { fileUri, mimeType } }, { text: prompt }];

            const result = await model.generateContent({
                contents: [{ role: 'user', parts }]
            });

            return safeJsonParse<T>(result.response.text());

        }, options?.maxRetries ?? 3, 2000);
    }

    async analyzeImage(
        base64Data: string,
        mimeType: string,
        prompt: string,
        options?: LLMOptions
    ): Promise<string> {
        const modelName = options?.model || 'gemini-2.5-flash';

        return await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
            const model = this.genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: modelName });
            const result = await model.generateContent([
                { inlineData: { data: base64Data, mimeType } },
                { text: prompt }
            ]);
            return result.response.text();
        }, options?.maxRetries ?? 3, 1000);
    }
}

--- START OF FILE: services/llm/DeepSeekProvider.ts ---
import { LLMProvider, LLMOptions } from './LLMProvider';
import { retryWithBackoff } from '../../utils/retryUtils';
import { ERROR_IDS } from '../../types';

// Helper for safe JSON parsing (DeepSeek might allow markdown wrapping)
function safeJsonParse<T>(jsonString: string): T {
    try {
        const cleanString = jsonString.trim().replace(/^```(json)?|```$/g, '');
        if (!cleanString) {
            throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.INVALID_API_RESPONSE);
        }
        return JSON.parse(cleanString);
    } catch (e) {
        console.error("Failed to parse DeepSeek JSON response:", jsonString, e);
        throw new Error(ERROR_IDS.INVALID_API_RESPONSE);
    }
}

export class DeepSeekProvider implements LLMProvider {
    id = 'deepseek';
    name = 'DeepSeek R1';
    description = 'Advanced reasoning model. Great for text, logic, and code. No video support.';

    private apiKey: string;
    private baseUrl = 'https://api.deepseek.com';

    constructor(apiKey: string) {
        this.apiKey = apiKey;
    }

    async generateText(prompt: string, options?: LLMOptions): Promise<string> {
        const model = options?.model || 'deepseek-chat';

        return await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
            const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/chat/completions`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`
                },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    model: model,
                    messages: [
                        { role: 'system', content: 'You are a helpful assistant.' },
                        { role: 'user', content: prompt }
                    ],
                    temperature: options?.temperature ?? 0.7,
                    stream: false
                })
            });

            if (!response.ok) {
                const errorText = await response.text();
                throw new Error(`DeepSeek API Error: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`);
            }

            const data = await response.json();
            return data.choices[0].message.content;
        }, options?.maxRetries ?? 3, 1000);
    }

    async generateJSON<T>(prompt: string, _schema?: any, options?: LLMOptions): Promise<T> {
        const model = options?.model || 'deepseek-chat';

        // DeepSeek might not support 'response_format: { type: "json_object" }' perfectly in all tiers
        // We will prompt engineering it + attempt to use the flag if safe.
        // For safety, we enforce JSON in system prompt.

        return await retryWithBackoff(async () => {
            const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/chat/completions`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`
                },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    model: model,
                    messages: [
                        { role: 'system', content: 'You are a helpful assistant. Output ONLY valid JSON.' },
                        { role: 'user', content: prompt }
                    ],
                    temperature: options?.temperature ?? 0.2,
                    response_format: { type: 'json_object' }
                })
            });

            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(`DeepSeek API Error: ${response.status}`);
            }

            const data = await response.json();
            return safeJsonParse<T>(data.choices[0].message.content);
        }, options?.maxRetries ?? 3, 1000);
    }

    async analyzeVideo<T>(
        _fileUri: string,
        _mimeType: string,
        _prompt: string,
        _schema?: any,
        _options?: LLMOptions
    ): Promise<T> {
        throw new Error("DeepSeek does not support Video Analysis yet. Please use Gemini.");
    }

    async analyzeImage(
        _base64Data: string,
        _mimeType: string,
        _prompt: string,
        _options?: LLMOptions
    ): Promise<string> {
        throw new Error("DeepSeek does not support Image Analysis. Please use Gemini.");
    }
}

--- END OF FILE: services/llm/DeepSeekProvider.ts ---

--- START OF FILE: services/llm/ModelRouter.ts ---
import { LLMProvider, LLMOptions } from './LLMProvider';
import { GeminiProvider } from './GeminiProvider';
import { DeepSeekProvider } from './DeepSeekProvider';

export class ModelRouter {
    private static instance: ModelRouter;
    private providers: Map<string, LLMProvider> = new Map();
    private activeProviderId: string = 'gemini'; // Default

    private constructor() {
        // Initialize with Gemini from env if available
        const geminiKey = import.meta.env.VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY;
        if (geminiKey) {
            this.registerProvider(new GeminiProvider(geminiKey));
        }

        // Initialize DeepSeek from localStorage if available
        // Note: In a real app we'd have a more robust key manager
        const deepSeekKey = localStorage.getItem('DEEPSEEK_API_KEY');
        if (deepSeekKey) {
            this.registerProvider(new DeepSeekProvider(deepSeekKey));
        }
    }

    public static getInstance(): ModelRouter {
        if (!ModelRouter.instance) {
            ModelRouter.instance = new ModelRouter();
        }
        return ModelRouter.instance;
    }

    public registerProvider(provider: LLMProvider) {
        this.providers.set(provider.id, provider);
    }

    public setActiveProvider(id: string) {
        if (this.providers.has(id)) {
            this.activeProviderId = id;
        } else {
            console.warn(`Provider ${id} not found. Keeping ${this.activeProviderId}`);
        }
    }

    public getActiveProvider(): LLMProvider {
        const provider = this.providers.get(this.activeProviderId);
        if (!provider) {
            // Fallback to Gemini if active is missing
            return this.providers.get('gemini')!;
        }
        return provider;
    }

    public getProvider(id: string): LLMProvider | undefined {
        return this.providers.get(id);
    }

    public getAllProviders(): LLMProvider[] {
        return Array.from(this.providers.values());
    }

    // --- Routing Methods ---

    public async generateText(prompt: string, options?: LLMOptions): Promise<string> {
        return this.getActiveProvider().generateText(prompt, options);
    }

    public async generateJSON<T>(prompt: string, schema?: any, options?: LLMOptions): Promise<T> {
        return this.getActiveProvider().generateJSON<T>(prompt, schema, options);
    }

    public async analyzeVideo<T>(
        fileUri: string,
        mimeType: string,
        prompt: string,
        schema?: any,
        options?: LLMOptions
    ): Promise<T> {
        const provider = this.getActiveProvider();

        // simple capability check (try/catch is another way)
        if (provider.id === 'deepseek') {
            console.warn("DeepSeek selected for Video task. Falling back to Gemini.");
            const gemini = this.getProvider('gemini');
            if (gemini) return gemini.analyzeVideo(fileUri, mimeType, prompt, schema, options);
            throw new Error("No video-capable model available.");
        }

        return provider.analyzeVideo(fileUri, mimeType, prompt, schema, options);
    }

    public async analyzeImage(
        base64Data: string,
        mimeType: string,
        prompt: string,
        options?: LLMOptions
    ): Promise<string> {
        const provider = this.getActiveProvider();

        if (provider.id === 'deepseek') {
            // Fallback for visual tasks
            const gemini = this.getProvider('gemini');
            if (gemini) return gemini.analyzeImage(base64Data, mimeType, prompt, options);
            throw new Error("No image-capable model available.");
        }

        return provider.analyzeImage(base64Data, mimeType, prompt, options);
    }
}

export const modelRouter = ModelRouter.getInstance();

--- END OF FILE: services/llm/ModelRouter.ts ---
